# CareerOS Mail (Sub-project 3b) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Let the candidate connect Gmail once from Settings, have every job email land on the right job's timeline automatically, read recruiter mail safely inside the app, and send replies and email applications only after approving the exact message.

**Architecture:** A new shell-neutral package `@careeros/mail` (IMAP read-only sync, matching, storage, HTML sanitising, MIME build, OS scheduler text) sits between `@careeros/core` (files are the truth: email sidecars, drafts, index table, migration 2, CLI `mail draft`) and `@careeros/services` (handlers, sync timer, the `email_send` approval executor, the `careeros-mail:` asset server). The renderer gains a `mail` bridge namespace, a Mail screen, a Job Mail tab, a setup wizard and an email preview on approval cards; the Electron shell adds the `careeros-mail:` protocol, a cookie-less image session, OS scheduler hosting and the `--background-sync` entry point.

**Tech Stack:** everything from 3a (Node 22.13+, TypeScript 5.9, React 19.3, Vitest 3.2, RTL 16, Electron 44.7.0, esbuild 0.28.2, Playwright 1.64), plus `imapflow` 2.2.11, `nodemailer` 10.0.14, `mailparser` 3.9.36 and `sanitize-html` 2.18.0.

**Spec:** `docs/specs/2026-10-08-mail-design.md` (open questions resolved 2026-10-08: no App Password fallback; Mail to sort keeps items 30 days). Builds on `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-08-app-shell.md` (3a), which is assumed complete: every file, name and test that plan creates exists exactly as written there.

## Global Constraints

- Everything in the 3a plan's Global Constraints still holds: Node `>=22.13`, no native npm modules, Electron hardening, renderer never imports `electron`, `node:*`, `@careeros/core`, `@careeros/services` or `@careeros/mail`, renderer never receives a secret, brand tokens only in `packages/app-ui` (no hex, `rgb(`, `font-family` or px font sizes), all record writes through `packages/core/src/store.ts`, exact dependency pins, `npm run typecheck && npm test` before every commit, never `--no-verify`.
- Provider: Gmail only. IMAP `imap.gmail.com:993` and SMTP `smtp.gmail.com:465`, implicit TLS on both. No STARTTLS, no plain ports, certificate validation always on (`rejectUnauthorized: true`, TLS 1.2 minimum). Mail code may connect only to the hosts in `net.allowedHosts` (`imap.gmail.com`, `smtp.gmail.com`).
- Secret: the App Password lives only in 3a's `SecretStore` as `gmail_app_password`. Never in the workspace, `settings/`, `.careeros/mail-state.json`, logs, crash reports or IPC replies. Linux without a secret service: refuse to save.
- Sync is read-only: mailboxes opened with `EXAMINE` (`getMailboxLock(path, { readOnly: true })`), bodies fetched with `BODY.PEEK[]`. No flags, labels, moves or deletes, ever.
- Sync defaults: every 15 minutes while the app runs, first sync looks back 30 days, caps of 25 MB per message, 500 messages per run and 60 seconds of network per run; a run that hits a cap resumes next time. One sync at a time, enforced by a lock file in user data.
- Personal mail is never written to disk. Only headers are fetched to decide. The ignore list stores senders and domains only.
- Every send is one `email_send` approval whose payload carries the SHA-256 digest of every input to the MIME build. Approve rebuilds and compares; any change fails with "The draft changed. Review it again." `send_limit_per_hour` defaults to 10. No BCC, no bulk send.
- Viewer: `sanitize-html` allow list in the main process, iframe `sandbox=""`, frame CSP `default-src 'none'; img-src careeros-mail: data:; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'`. Remote images never load until "Load images"; then main fetches them over https only, without cookies or referrer, at most 5 MB each, never SVG.
- Attachments are never opened or saved automatically. Saved names are slugged ASCII, 80 characters max, no path parts, no leading dots. Executable and script types get a warning and are never opened by the app.
- Background sync is off by default. The OS scheduler runs the app binary itself with `--background-sync --workspace <path>`. It reads mail only and never sends. Its log holds counts and errors only, never addresses or subjects.
- File names under `emails/` are slugged ASCII, 80 characters max. Dates are stored as UTC ISO strings and shown in local time.
- Mail to sort (`.careeros/mail-triage/`) keeps items 30 days, then deletes them and shows the count.
- Tests never contact a real server: GmailProvider tests inject fake IMAP clients and nodemailer's stream transport; everything above the provider uses `FakeMailProvider`.
- Fictional data only: Alex Example, Acme Corp, Example Labs, Acme Recruiting, `alex@example.com`, `talent@example.org`. Build email addresses and Message-IDs in code and tests by concatenation (`"talent" + "@" + "example.org"`). Any address written in prose uses an `@example.com`, `@example.org` or `@example.net` domain exactly.
- Out of scope (3b): Outlook, iCloud, generic IMAP and OAuth sign-in; scheduled, bulk or BCC sends; rich-text composing; changing anything in Gmail; Claude classification of mail and Claude-written drafts inside the app (3c); installers and uninstall (4).

## Review Focus

1. **Subjects and attachment names in other scripts, emoji, or 300 characters long.** Expected: stored files get ASCII names of at most 80 characters with a hash suffix so two such messages never collide, the subject itself is kept intact in the sidecar, and nothing throws. Pinned in Task 10.
2. **The same message seen twice: in INBOX and in Sent (the candidate copied themself), or after Gmail re-sends a UID.** Expected: stored once, one timeline event. Pinned in Task 11.
3. **A message with no `Message-ID` or no `Date` header.** Expected: a stable synthetic id from the raw bytes, the IMAP internal date as the date, stored and deduplicated like any other. Pinned in Task 6 and Task 11.
4. **The password is revoked or the network drops in the middle of a sync.** Expected: the run ends with a plain error in Mail status, the lock is released, the high-water mark covers only messages fully stored, and the next run resumes without duplicates. Pinned in Task 11.
5. **An attachment is renamed, deleted or replaced after the email was proposed.** Expected: Approve fails ("can't be found" or "The draft changed. Review it again."), nothing is sent and the draft stays. Pinned in Task 17.

---

## Rulings made while planning

- **Digest of inputs, deterministic MIME.** The spec asks for the SHA-256 of the fully built MIME message. MIME bytes normally include a random boundary, Message-ID and Date. The plan fixes all three per draft (boundary from the draft id, Message-ID and Date stamped at proposal time and stored in the payload), so the build is deterministic, and a test proves two builds give identical bytes. The approval digest is then computed over a canonical JSON of every build input (addresses, subject, threading headers, body with signature, and each attachment's name, type, size and SHA-256). This lets `@careeros/core` compute it without nodemailer, which the CLI `mail draft` needs (core cannot depend on `@careeros/mail`).
- **Draft format and address checks live in core** (`packages/core/src/drafts.ts`) for the same reason; `@careeros/mail` builds MIME from core's `CanonicalMessage`.
- **Job-related rule uses headers only.** The spec's "subject and first lines" rule would need the body of messages that may be personal, which conflicts with "only headers are fetched to decide". 3b decides on the sender domain list and subject words; 3c's classifier may read more.
- **Password flow.** `mail.testConnection(address, displayName, password)` runs the three checks in main and, on success, holds the password in memory for 10 minutes; `mail.saveAccount(address, displayName)` then stores it in the `SecretStore` and writes the address. Nothing is saved on a failed test. `secrets.set("gmail_app_password", ...)` (Replace password) runs the same test against the connected address and saves only on success. 3a's generic secrets list no longer shows the Gmail row; the Email card owns it.
- **Signature** is multi-line, so it is edited in the Email card (`mail.setSignature`) with a live preview rather than in the schema-driven form. `sync_minutes`, `first_sync_days` and `send_limit_per_hour` carry `x-ui` and appear in 3a's integrations form.
- **Message refs** are workspace-relative sidecar paths matching one strict pattern (`companies/<slug>/[jobs/<id>/]emails/<name>.json` or `.careeros/mail-triage/<id>.json`). The `careeros-mail:` scheme carries them base64url-encoded.
- **Apply hand-off by email** reuses `mail.drafts.save` and `mail.drafts.propose`; the draft carries a CareerOS-only `Purpose: apply` header (never sent) so the Apply screen can follow that approval.
- **"Ask Claude to improve"** in the draft editor arrives with 3c; 3b does not show a dead button.
- **The job's recruiter.** The spec matches on "recruiter email on a job or company"; job records have no recruiter field, so 3b matches on `company.recruiter.email`. A job-level field can come later without changing the matcher's shape.
- **Bundling.** `imapflow` (pino worker files), `nodemailer`, `mailparser` (iconv tables) and `sanitize-html` stay external to the esbuild bundle and load from `node_modules`, as `electron` does.

## Group A: core (`@careeros/core`, spec §3.1)

### Task 1: Email record kind, company and settings mail fields, migration 2

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/schemas/email.schema.json`, `packages/core/src/migrations/0002-mail.ts`, `packages/core/test/mail-records.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/core/schemas/company.schema.json`, `packages/core/schemas/integrations.schema.json`, `packages/core/src/types.ts`, `packages/core/src/paths.ts` (`layout`), `packages/core/src/migrations/index.ts`, `packages/core/test/migration-0001.test.ts` (full replacement)
- Regenerate: `samples/alex-example/` (`npm run samples`), `packages/app-ui/src/mock/alex-fixture.json` (`npm run fixture -w @careeros/app-ui`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `check`, `defaults`, `updateRecord`, `writeFileAtomic`, `RecordError` (store), `writeClaudeSettings` (3a Task 2), `Migration`.
- Produces:
  - `EMAIL_MATCH_KINDS = ["recruiter", "domain", "email_match", "subject", "user", "none"] as const`, `type EmailMatchKind`
  - `interface EmailAddress { name: string; address: string }`
  - `interface EmailAttachmentMeta { part: string; name: string; size: number; type: string; cid: string; inline: boolean; saved_as: string }`
  - `interface EmailRecord { schema; message_id; in_reply_to; references: string[]; thread_key; direction: "in" | "out"; from: EmailAddress; to: EmailAddress[]; cc: EmailAddress[]; reply_to: EmailAddress[]; subject; date; uid: number | null; mailbox; matched_by: EmailMatchKind; attachments: EmailAttachmentMeta[]; images_allowed: boolean; remote_images: number; auth: "pass" | "fail" | "none"; too_large: boolean; opened_at: string; custom }`
  - `interface MailSettings { provider: "gmail"; address; display_name; signature; sync_minutes; first_sync_days; background: { on: boolean; minutes: number }; send_limit_per_hour }`, `Integrations.mail: MailSettings`

  - `layout(root)`: `companyEmailsDir(slug)`, `jobEmailsDir(slug, id)`, `draftsDir(slug, id)`, `mailState`, `mailTriage`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `packages/core/test/mail-records.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { check } from "../src/schema.js";
import { initWorkspace } from "../src/workspace.js";
import { migrate, pendingMigrations } from "../src/migrate.js";
import { LATEST_DATA_VERSION } from "../src/migrations/index.js";
import { layout } from "../src/paths.js";
import { readRecord, updateRecord, writeFileAtomic } from "../src/store.js";
import { newJob } from "../src/jobs.js";
import { FIXED, tmpDir } from "./helpers.js";

const addr = (user: string, domain: string) => user + "@" + domain;
const mid = (s: string) => "<" + s + "@" + "example.org>";

describe("email record", () => {
  it("fills defaults and keeps the core fields", () => {
    const r = check("email", { schema: 1, message_id: mid("a1"), direction: "in", subject: "Interview", date: "2026-01-14T09:00:00.000Z", from: { address: addr("talent", "example.org") } });
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(r.value).toMatchObject({
      in_reply_to: "", references: [], to: [], cc: [], reply_to: [], uid: null, mailbox: "", matched_by: "none", attachments: [],
      images_allowed: false, remote_images: 0, auth: "none", too_large: false, opened_at: "", thread_key: "",
    });
    expect(r.value.from).toEqual({ name: "", address: addr("talent", "example.org") });
  });
  it("rejects unknown directions and match kinds", () => {
    const base = { schema: 1, message_id: mid("x"), subject: "", date: "", from: { address: "a" } };
    expect(check("email", { ...base, direction: "sideways" }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(check("email", { ...base, direction: "in", matched_by: "guess" }).ok).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("company and settings fields for mail", () => {
  it("adds email_match and recruiter to companies", () => {
    expect(check("company", { schema: 1, name: "Acme Corp", slug: "acme-corp" }).value).toMatchObject({ email_match: [], recruiter: { name: "", email: "" } });
  });
  it("adds the mail block to integrations with safe defaults and bounds", () => {
    expect(check("integrations", { schema: 1 }).value.mail).toEqual({
      provider: "gmail", address: "", display_name: "", signature: "", sync_minutes: 15, first_sync_days: 30,
      background: { on: false, minutes: 60 }, send_limit_per_hour: 10,
    });
    expect(check("integrations", { schema: 1, mail: { sync_minutes: 1 } }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(check("integrations", { schema: 1, mail: { provider: "outlook" } }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(check("integrations", { schema: 1, mail: { send_limit_per_hour: 500 } }).ok).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("migration 2 (mail)", () => {
  it("fills mail defaults on old company and integrations files and backs them up", () => {
    const root = path.join(tmpDir(), "ws");
    initWorkspace(root, { now: FIXED });
    const job = newJob(root, { company: "Acme Corp", title: "AI Engineer" }, { now: FIXED });
    const L = layout(root);
    // Rewind to data version 1: no mail fields anywhere.
    const strip = (file: string, keys: string[]) => {
      const raw = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8"));
      for (const k of keys) delete raw[k];
      writeFileAtomic(file, JSON.stringify(raw, null, 2) + "\n");
    };
    strip(L.companyFile(job.company_slug), ["email_match", "recruiter"]);
    strip(L.integrations, ["mail"]);
    updateRecord("workspace", L.workspaceFile, (w) => { w.data_version = 1; });
    expect(pendingMigrations(root).map((m) => m.id)).toEqual([2]);
    const r = migrate(root, undefined, { now: FIXED });
    expect(r.applied).toEqual([2]);
    expect(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(L.companyFile(job.company_slug), "utf8"))).toMatchObject({ email_match: [], recruiter: { name: "", email: "" } });
    expect(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(L.integrations, "utf8")).mail.sync_minutes).toBe(15);
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(r.backup!, "settings", "integrations.json"))).toBe(true);
    expect(readRecord("workspace", L.workspaceFile).data_version).toBe(LATEST_DATA_VERSION);
  });
  it("skips a broken company file instead of failing the whole migration", () => {
    const root = path.join(tmpDir(), "ws");
    initWorkspace(root, { now: FIXED });
    const job = newJob(root, { company: "Example Labs", title: "Backend Engineer" }, { now: FIXED });
    const L = layout(root);
    fs.writeFileSync(L.companyFile(job.company_slug), "{ broken");
    updateRecord("workspace", L.workspaceFile, (w) => { w.data_version = 1; });
    expect(migrate(root, undefined, { now: FIXED }).applied).toEqual([2]);
  });
  it("knows the mail folders", () => {
    const L = layout("/fictional/ws");
    expect(L.companyEmailsDir("acme-corp")).toBe(path.join("/fictional/ws", "companies", "acme-corp", "emails"));
    expect(L.jobEmailsDir("acme-corp", "j1")).toBe(path.join("/fictional/ws", "companies", "acme-corp", "jobs", "j1", "emails"));
    expect(L.draftsDir("acme-corp", "j1")).toBe(path.join("/fictional/ws", "companies", "acme-corp", "jobs", "j1", "emails", "drafts"));
    expect(L.mailState).toBe(path.join("/fictional/ws", ".careeros", "mail-state.json"));
    expect(L.mailTriage).toBe(path.join("/fictional/ws", ".careeros", "mail-triage"));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the schemas**

Create `packages/core/schemas/email.schema.json` (the address shape is written out in each place on purpose: Ajv applies `default` reliably only to inline subschemas):

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "careeros/email",
  "type": "object",
  "required": ["schema", "message_id", "direction", "subject", "date", "from"],
  "properties": {
    "schema": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 1 },
    "message_id": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 998 },
    "in_reply_to": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 998, "default": "" },
    "references": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 998 }, "maxItems": 50, "default": [] },
    "thread_key": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 300, "default": "" },
    "direction": { "enum": ["in", "out"] },
    "from": {
      "type": "object", "required": ["address"],
      "properties": { "name": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 300, "default": "" }, "address": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 320 } }
    },
    "to": {
      "type": "array", "default": [],
      "items": { "type": "object", "required": ["address"], "properties": { "name": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 300, "default": "" }, "address": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 320 } } }
    },
    "cc": {
      "type": "array", "default": [],
      "items": { "type": "object", "required": ["address"], "properties": { "name": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 300, "default": "" }, "address": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 320 } } }
    },
    "reply_to": {
      "type": "array", "default": [],
      "items": { "type": "object", "required": ["address"], "properties": { "name": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 300, "default": "" }, "address": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 320 } } }
    },
    "subject": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 2000 },
    "date": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 40 },
    "uid": { "type": ["integer", "null"], "default": null },
    "mailbox": { "type": "string", "default": "" },
    "matched_by": { "enum": ["recruiter", "domain", "email_match", "subject", "user", "none"], "default": "none" },
    "attachments": {
      "type": "array", "default": [],
      "items": {
        "type": "object", "required": ["part", "name"],
        "properties": {
          "part": { "type": "string" }, "name": { "type": "string" },
          "size": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "default": 0 },
          "type": { "type": "string", "default": "application/octet-stream" },
          "cid": { "type": "string", "default": "" },
          "inline": { "type": "boolean", "default": false },
          "saved_as": { "type": "string", "default": "" }
        }
      }
    },
    "images_allowed": { "type": "boolean", "default": false },
    "remote_images": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "default": 0 },
    "auth": { "enum": ["pass", "fail", "none"], "default": "none" },
    "too_large": { "type": "boolean", "default": false },
    "opened_at": { "type": "string", "default": "" },
    "custom": { "type": "object", "default": {} }
  }
}
```

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { layout } from "../paths.js";
import { writeClaudeSettings } from "../privacy.js";
import { RecordError, updateRecord } from "../store.js";
import type { Migration } from "./index.js";

function companyFiles(root: string): string[] {
  const L = layout(root);
  if (!fs.existsSync(L.companiesDir)) return [];
  return fs.readdirSync(L.companiesDir).sort().map((s) => L.companyFile(s)).filter((f) => fs.existsSync(f));
}

// Fills the 3b mail defaults (company email_match and recruiter, integrations.mail) and
// regenerates .claude/settings.json, which gains the SessionStart grant-expiry hook in Task 3.
export const mailMigration: Migration = {
  id: 2,
  name: "mail fields and Claude session hook",
  files: (root) => [layout(root).integrations, path.join(root, ".claude", "settings.json"), ...companyFiles(root)],
  run: (root) => {
    updateRecord("integrations", layout(root).integrations, () => undefined);
    for (const f of companyFiles(root)) {
      // A hand-broken company file is reported by `careeros validate`; it must not block opening the workspace.
      try { updateRecord("company", f, () => undefined); } catch (e) { if (!(e instanceof RecordError)) throw e; }
    }
    writeClaudeSettings(root);
  },
};
```

```ts
import { privacyMigration } from "./0001-privacy.js";
import { mailMigration } from "./0002-mail.js";

export interface Migration {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  /** Absolute paths of files this migration may change; they are backed up first. */
  files(root: string): string[];
  run(root: string): void;
}

// Append new migrations here with increasing ids. Never edit or remove a shipped one.
export const MIGRATIONS: Migration[] = [privacyMigration, mailMigration];

export const LATEST_DATA_VERSION = MIGRATIONS.reduce((m, x) => Math.max(m, x.id), 0);
```

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { initWorkspace } from "../src/workspace.js";
import { migrate, pendingMigrations } from "../src/migrate.js";
import { LATEST_DATA_VERSION } from "../src/migrations/index.js";
import { readRecord, writeFileAtomic } from "../src/store.js";
import { layout } from "../src/paths.js";
import { FIXED, tmpDir } from "./helpers.js";

function oldWorkspace(): string {
  const root = path.join(tmpDir(), "ws");
  initWorkspace(root, { now: FIXED });
  // Rewind to the sub-project 1 shape: no privacy block, data_version 0, old settings file.
  writeFileAtomic(layout(root).workspaceFile, JSON.stringify({ schema: 1, data_version: 0, product_version: "0.1.0", created_at: FIXED.toISOString(), custom: {} }, null, 2) + "\n");
  writeFileAtomic(path.join(root, ".claude", "settings.json"), JSON.stringify({ permissions: { deny: ["Read(./me/identity/**)"] } }, null, 2) + "\n");
  return root;
}

describe("migration 1 (privacy)", () => {
  it("new workspaces start at the latest data version with the lock list", () => {
    expect(LATEST_DATA_VERSION).toBe(2);
    const root = path.join(tmpDir(), "ws");
    initWorkspace(root, { now: FIXED });
    expect(readRecord("workspace", layout(root).workspaceFile)).toMatchObject({ data_version: LATEST_DATA_VERSION, privacy: { locked: ["me/identity", "me/employment"], grants: [] } });
  });
  it("adds the lock list to an old workspace, regenerates Claude settings and backs up both files", () => {
    const root = oldWorkspace();
    expect(pendingMigrations(root).map((m) => m.id)).toEqual([1, 2]);
    const r = migrate(root, undefined, { now: FIXED });
    expect(r.applied).toEqual([1, 2]);
    const raw = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(layout(root).workspaceFile, "utf8"));
    expect(raw.privacy).toEqual({ locked: ["me/identity", "me/employment"], grants: [] });
    expect(raw.data_version).toBe(LATEST_DATA_VERSION);
    const s = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, ".claude", "settings.json"), "utf8"));
    expect(s.permissions.deny).toContain("Read(./me/employment/**)");
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(r.backup!, "workspace.json"))).toBe(true);
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(r.backup!, ".claude", "settings.json"))).toBe(true);
    expect(migrate(root).applied).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

### Task 2: Email sidecars in the index (`emails` table and refresh rules)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/emails.ts`, `packages/core/test/index-emails.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/index-db.ts` (full rewrite below), `packages/core/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `layout` (Task 1), `readRecord`, `RecordError`, `insideRoot`, 3a's `index-db.ts`.
- Produces:
  - `interface EmailFileRef { file: string; rel: string; company_slug: string; job_id: string }` (`rel` uses `/`)
  - `interface EmailSkip { file: string; problems: string[] }` (`file` is relative to the root, OS separators, like other index skips)
  - `emailsDirFor(root, slug, id): string` (`id === ""` means the company folder)
  - `listEmailFiles(root, slug, id): EmailFileRef[]`, `readEmails(root, slug, id, skipped?): { ref: EmailFileRef; email: EmailRecord }[]`
  - Index: table `emails(company_slug, job_id, file PRIMARY KEY, direction, date, subject, from_name, from_addr, thread_key, attachments, opened, matched_by)`; `IndexReport.emails: number`; `RefreshPlan.companyEmails: Set<string>`

Rules: a job's emails are indexed with the job (any path under `companies/<slug>/jobs/<id>/` already refreshes that job). `companies/<slug>/emails/...` refreshes that company's own mail. An index without the `emails` table is rebuilt in full.

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { initWorkspace } from "../src/workspace.js";
import { newJob } from "../src/jobs.js";
import { buildIndex, openIndex, planRefresh, refreshIndex } from "../src/index-db.js";
import { writeRecord } from "../src/store.js";
import { layout } from "../src/paths.js";
import { FIXED, tmpDir } from "./helpers.js";

const mid = (s: string) => "<" + s + "@" + "example.org>";
const sidecar = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  schema: 1, message_id: mid("m1"), direction: "in", subject: "Interview for AI Engineer", date: "2026-01-14T09:00:00.000Z",
  from: { name: "Acme Talent", address: "talent" + "@" + "example.org" }, ...over,
});

function seeded() {
  const root = path.join(tmpDir(), "ws");
  initWorkspace(root, { now: FIXED });
  const a = newJob(root, { company: "Acme Corp", title: "AI Engineer" }, { now: FIXED });
  const L = layout(root);
  writeRecord("email", path.join(L.jobEmailsDir(a.company_slug, a.id), "2026-01-14-interview-for-ai-engineer-aaaaaa.json"), sidecar());
  writeRecord("email", path.join(L.companyEmailsDir(a.company_slug), "2026-01-13-hello-from-acme-bbbbbb.json"), sidecar({ message_id: mid("m2"), subject: "Hello from Acme", date: "2026-01-13T09:00:00.000Z" }));
  buildIndex(root);
  return { root, a, L };
}
const rows = (root: string) => {
  const db = openIndex(root);
  const r = db.prepare("SELECT company_slug, job_id, subject, direction, opened FROM emails ORDER BY date").all() as { subject: string }[];
  db.close();
  return r;
};

describe("emails in the index", () => {
  it("indexes job mail and company mail", () => {
    const { root, a } = seeded();
    expect(rows(root)).toEqual([
      { company_slug: "acme-corp", job_id: "", subject: "Hello from Acme", direction: "in", opened: 0 },
      { company_slug: "acme-corp", job_id: a.id, subject: "Interview for AI Engineer", direction: "in", opened: 0 },
    ]);
  });
  it("maps email paths to the smallest refresh", () => {
    const p = planRefresh(["companies/acme-corp/emails/x.json", "companies/acme-corp/jobs/j1/emails/y.json"]);
    expect(p.full).toBe(false);
    expect([...p.companyEmails]).toEqual(["acme-corp"]);
    expect([...p.jobs]).toEqual(["acme-corp/j1"]);
  });
  it("picks up a new message for one job", () => {
    const { root, a, L } = seeded();
    const f = path.join(L.jobEmailsDir(a.company_slug, a.id), "2026-01-15-re-interview-cccccc.json");
    writeRecord("email", f, sidecar({ message_id: mid("m3"), subject: "Re: Interview", direction: "out", date: "2026-01-15T09:00:00.000Z" }));
    expect(refreshIndex(root, [f])).toMatchObject({ mode: "incremental", emails: 2 });
    expect(rows(root)).toHaveLength(3);
  });
  it("drops a deleted company message", () => {
    const { root, a, L } = seeded();
    const f = path.join(L.companyEmailsDir(a.company_slug), "2026-01-13-hello-from-acme-bbbbbb.json");
    fs.rmSync(f);
    refreshIndex(root, [f]);
    expect(rows(root).map((r) => r.subject)).toEqual(["Interview for AI Engineer"]);
  });
  it("reports a broken sidecar and keeps going", () => {
    const { root, a, L } = seeded();
    const f = path.join(L.jobEmailsDir(a.company_slug, a.id), "2026-01-16-broken-dddddd.json");
    fs.writeFileSync(f, "{");
    const r = refreshIndex(root, [f]);
    expect(r.skipped.map((s) => s.file)).toContain(path.relative(root, f));
    expect(rows(root)).toHaveLength(2);
  });
  it("ignores drafts and saved attachments next to the messages", () => {
    const { root, a, L } = seeded();
    fs.mkdirSync(L.draftsDir(a.company_slug, a.id), { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(L.draftsDir(a.company_slug, a.id), "d1.md"), "To: x\n\nHi\n");
    expect(buildIndex(root).emails).toBe(2);
  });
  it("rebuilds an index made before the emails table existed", () => {
    const { root, a, L } = seeded();
    const db = openIndex(root);
    db.exec("DROP TABLE emails");
    db.close();
    expect(refreshIndex(root, [L.jobFile(a.company_slug, a.id)]).mode).toBe("full");
    expect(rows(root)).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/core/src/emails.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { insideRoot, layout } from "./paths.js";
import { readRecord, RecordError } from "./store.js";
import type { EmailRecord } from "./types.js";

export interface EmailFileRef { file: string; rel: string; company_slug: string; job_id: string }
export interface EmailSkip { file: string; problems: string[] }

/** The emails folder for a job, or for the company itself when id is "". */
export function emailsDirFor(root: string, slug: string, id: string): string {
  return id ? layout(root).jobEmailsDir(slug, id) : layout(root).companyEmailsDir(slug);
}

/** Message sidecars (*.json) in one emails folder. Drafts and attachments live in subfolders and are skipped. Never follows links. */
export function listEmailFiles(root: string, slug: string, id: string): EmailFileRef[] {
  const dir = emailsDirFor(root, slug, id);
  let st: fs.Stats;
  try { st = fs.lstatSync(dir); } catch { return []; }
  if (!st.isDirectory() || !insideRoot(root, dir)) return [];
  const out: EmailFileRef[] = [];
  for (const n of fs.readdirSync(dir).sort()) {
    if (!n.endsWith(".json") || n.startsWith(".")) continue;
    const file = path.join(dir, n);
    if (!fs.lstatSync(file).isFile()) continue;
    out.push({ file, rel: path.relative(root, file).split(path.sep).join("/"), company_slug: slug, job_id: id });
  }
  return out;
}

export function readEmails(root: string, slug: string, id: string, skipped?: EmailSkip[]): { ref: EmailFileRef; email: EmailRecord }[] {
  const out: { ref: EmailFileRef; email: EmailRecord }[] = [];
  for (const ref of listEmailFiles(root, slug, id)) {
    try { out.push({ ref, email: readRecord("email", ref.file) }); }
    catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof RecordError) skipped?.push({ file: path.relative(root, ref.file), problems: e.problems });
      else throw e;
    }
  }
  return out;
}
```

Add `export * from "./emails.js";` to `packages/core/src/index.ts`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Rewrite `packages/core/src/index-db.ts`**

This is 3a's file with the `emails` table added (new lines are marked `// 3b`).

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import { isEligible, pinRank } from "./eligibility.js";
import { type EmailSkip, readEmails } from "./emails.js"; // 3b
import { listJobs, type ListJobsSkip } from "./jobs.js";
import { readSearches } from "./knowledge.js";
import { insideRoot, layout } from "./paths.js";
import { readRecord, RecordError } from "./store.js";
import type { Company, Job, WorkRightsStatus } from "./types.js";

const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
// Loaded through require so test runners that don't know node:sqlite as a builtin still work.
const { DatabaseSync } = require("node:sqlite") as typeof import("node:sqlite");
type Db = InstanceType<typeof DatabaseSync>;
type JobRef = { company_slug: string; id: string; file: string };

export interface IndexReport { jobs: number; companies: number; events: number; searches: number; emails: number; skipped: { file: string; problems: string[] }[] }
export interface RefreshReport extends IndexReport { mode: "full" | "incremental" }
export interface RefreshPlan { full: boolean; companies: Set<string>; companyJobs: Set<string>; jobs: Set<string>; companyEmails: Set<string>; searches: boolean }

const TABLES = ["jobs", "companies", "events", "searches", "emails"];
const DDL = `
CREATE TABLE jobs (company_slug TEXT, id TEXT, company_name TEXT, client TEXT, title TEXT, source TEXT, source_site TEXT,
  url TEXT, posted TEXT, found_at TEXT, location TEXT, work_mode TEXT, job_type TEXT, salary_text TEXT, wr_label TEXT,
  fit REAL, sponsor REAL, career_match REAL, career_odds REAL, career_tier TEXT, status TEXT, eligible INTEGER,
  pinned INTEGER, last_activity TEXT, json TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (company_slug, id));
CREATE TABLE companies (slug TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT, type TEXT, big_tech INTEGER, sponsor_status TEXT);
CREATE TABLE events (company_slug TEXT, job_id TEXT, at TEXT, event TEXT, note TEXT);
CREATE TABLE searches (at TEXT, site TEXT, query TEXT, location TEXT, results TEXT, kept TEXT, notes TEXT);
CREATE TABLE emails (company_slug TEXT, job_id TEXT, file TEXT PRIMARY KEY, direction TEXT, date TEXT, subject TEXT,
  from_name TEXT, from_addr TEXT, thread_key TEXT, attachments INTEGER, opened INTEGER, matched_by TEXT);
CREATE INDEX jobs_status ON jobs(status);
CREATE INDEX events_at ON events(at);
CREATE INDEX emails_date ON emails(date);
`;

export function openIndex(root: string): Db {
  return new DatabaseSync(layout(root).indexDb);
}

interface Ctx { root: string; report: IndexReport; status: WorkRightsStatus; db: Db }

function emptyReport(): IndexReport { return { jobs: 0, companies: 0, events: 0, searches: 0, emails: 0, skipped: [] }; }
const rel = (root: string, f: string) => path.relative(root, f);

function skipError(ctx: Ctx, e: unknown): void {
  if (e instanceof RecordError) ctx.report.skipped.push({ file: rel(ctx.root, e.file), problems: e.problems });
  else throw e;
}

function profileStatus(root: string, report: IndexReport): WorkRightsStatus {
  try { return readRecord("profile", layout(root).profile).work_rights.status; }
  catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof RecordError) { report.skipped.push({ file: rel(root, e.file), problems: e.problems }); return "unknown"; }
    throw e;
  }
}

// 3b: one row per message sidecar; id "" is the company's own mail.
function indexEmails(ctx: Ctx, slug: string, id: string): void {
  const skipped: EmailSkip[] = [];
  const ins = ctx.db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO emails VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)");
  for (const { ref, email: e } of readEmails(ctx.root, slug, id, skipped)) {
    ins.run(slug, id, ref.rel, e.direction, e.date, e.subject, e.from.name, e.from.address, e.thread_key,
      e.attachments.filter((a) => !a.inline).length, e.opened_at ? 1 : 0, e.matched_by);
    ctx.report.emails++;
  }
  ctx.report.skipped.push(...skipped);
}

/** Inserts one company row. Returns its display name, or null if there is no usable record. */
function indexCompany(ctx: Ctx, slug: string): string | null {
  const L = layout(ctx.root);
  const companyDir = L.companyDir(slug);
  if (!fs.existsSync(companyDir)) return null;
  // Security: never read through a folder that resolves outside the workspace.
  if (!insideRoot(ctx.root, companyDir)) {
    ctx.report.skipped.push({ file: rel(ctx.root, companyDir), problems: ["outside the workspace"] });
    return null;
  }
  const f = L.companyFile(slug);
  if (!fs.existsSync(f)) return null;
  let c: Company;
  try { c = readRecord("company", f); } catch (e) { skipError(ctx, e); return null; }
  ctx.db.prepare("INSERT INTO companies VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)").run(slug, c.name, c.type, c.big_tech ? 1 : 0, c.sponsor.status);
  ctx.report.companies++;
  indexEmails(ctx, slug, ""); // 3b
  return c.name;
}

function indexJob(ctx: Ctx, ref: JobRef, companyName: string): void {
  // Security: a job or company folder may be a symlink redirecting outside the workspace.
  if (!insideRoot(ctx.root, ref.file)) {
    ctx.report.skipped.push({ file: rel(ctx.root, ref.file), problems: ["outside the workspace"] });
    return;
  }
  let j: Job;
  try { j = readRecord("job", ref.file); } catch (e) { skipError(ctx, e); return; }
  const last = j.timeline.reduce((m, ev) => (ev.at > m ? ev.at : m), j.found_at);
  ctx.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO jobs VALUES (${Array(25).fill("?").join(", ")})`).run(
    ref.company_slug, ref.id, companyName, j.client, j.title, j.source, j.source_site,
    j.url, j.posted, j.found_at, j.location, j.work_mode, j.job_type, j.salary.text, j.work_rights.label,
    j.scores.fit.value, j.scores.sponsor.value, j.scores.career.match, j.scores.career.odds, j.scores.career.tier,
    j.status, isEligible(ctx.status, j.work_rights.label) ? 1 : 0, pinRank(j.work_rights.label), last, JSON.stringify(j),
  );
  ctx.report.jobs++;
  const insEvent = ctx.db.prepare("INSERT INTO events VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)");
  for (const ev of j.timeline) { insEvent.run(ref.company_slug, ref.id, ev.at, ev.event, ev.note ?? ""); ctx.report.events++; }
  indexEmails(ctx, ref.company_slug, ref.id); // 3b
}

function indexSearches(ctx: Ctx): void {
  const insSearch = ctx.db.prepare("INSERT INTO searches VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)");
  const { entries, badLines } = readSearches(ctx.root);
  for (const s of entries) { insSearch.run(s.at, s.site, s.query, s.location, s.results, s.kept, s.notes); ctx.report.searches++; }
  if (badLines.length) ctx.report.skipped.push({ file: rel(ctx.root, layout(ctx.root).searchLog), problems: badLines.map((n) => `line ${n} is not valid JSON`) });
}

export function buildIndex(root: string): IndexReport {
  const L = layout(root);
  const report = emptyReport();
  const status = profileStatus(root, report);
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(L.indexDb), { recursive: true });
  const tmp = `${L.indexDb}.${process.pid}.tmp`;
  fs.rmSync(tmp, { force: true });
  const db = new DatabaseSync(tmp);
  const ctx: Ctx = { root, report, status, db };
  try {
    db.exec(DDL);
    db.exec("BEGIN");
    const names = new Map<string, string>();
    if (fs.existsSync(L.companiesDir)) {
      for (const slug of fs.readdirSync(L.companiesDir).sort()) {
        const name = indexCompany(ctx, slug);
        if (name !== null) names.set(slug, name);
      }
    }
    const jobSkips: ListJobsSkip[] = [];
    for (const ref of listJobs(root, jobSkips)) indexJob(ctx, ref, names.get(ref.company_slug) ?? ref.company_slug);
    for (const s of jobSkips) report.skipped.push({ file: rel(root, s.file), problems: [s.problem] });
    indexSearches(ctx);
    db.exec("COMMIT");
  } catch (e) {
    db.close();
    fs.rmSync(tmp, { force: true });
    throw e;
  }
  db.close();
  fs.renameSync(tmp, L.indexDb);
  return report;
}

export function planRefresh(relPaths: string[]): RefreshPlan {
  const plan: RefreshPlan = { full: false, companies: new Set(), companyJobs: new Set(), jobs: new Set(), companyEmails: new Set(), searches: false };
  for (const raw of relPaths) {
    const r = raw.replace(/^\.\//, "").replace(/\/+$/, "");
    if (r.startsWith("..")) continue;
    const parts = r.split("/");
    if (r === "" || r === "." || r === "workspace.json" || parts[0] === "settings" || r === "companies") { plan.full = true; continue; }
    if (parts[0] === "knowledge" && (parts.length === 1 || parts[1] === "search-log.jsonl")) { plan.searches = true; continue; }
    if (parts[0] !== "companies" || !parts[1]) continue;
    const slug = parts[1];
    if (parts.length === 2) { plan.companies.add(slug); plan.companyJobs.add(slug); }
    else if (parts[2] === "company.json") plan.companies.add(slug);
    else if (parts[2] === "emails") plan.companyEmails.add(slug); // 3b
    else if (parts[2] === "jobs" && parts.length === 3) plan.companyJobs.add(slug);
    else if (parts[2] === "jobs" && parts[3]) plan.jobs.add(`${slug}/${parts[3]}`);
  }
  return plan;
}

export function refreshIndex(root: string, changedPaths: string[]): RefreshReport {
  const L = layout(root);
  const full = (): RefreshReport => ({ ...buildIndex(root), mode: "full" });
  if (!fs.existsSync(L.indexDb)) return full();
  const rels = changedPaths.map((p) => (path.isAbsolute(p) ? path.relative(root, p) : p).split(path.sep).join("/"));
  const plan = planRefresh(rels);
  if (plan.full) return full();
  const report = emptyReport();
  if (!plan.companies.size && !plan.companyJobs.size && !plan.jobs.size && !plan.companyEmails.size && !plan.searches) return { ...report, mode: "incremental" };

  const db = new DatabaseSync(L.indexDb);
  try {
    const t = db.prepare(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name IN (${TABLES.map(() => "?").join(", ")})`).get(...TABLES) as { n: number };
    if (t.n !== TABLES.length) { db.close(); return full(); }
    const ctx: Ctx = { root, report, status: profileStatus(root, report), db };
    db.exec("BEGIN");
    for (const slug of plan.companies) {
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM companies WHERE slug = ?").run(slug);
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM emails WHERE company_slug = ? AND job_id = ''").run(slug); // 3b: indexCompany re-reads them
      const name = indexCompany(ctx, slug);
      db.prepare("UPDATE jobs SET company_name = ? WHERE company_slug = ?").run(name ?? slug, slug);
    }
    const names = new Map((db.prepare("SELECT slug, name FROM companies").all() as { slug: string; name: string }[]).map((r) => [r.slug, r.name]));
    for (const slug of plan.companyEmails) { // 3b
      if (plan.companies.has(slug)) continue;
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM emails WHERE company_slug = ? AND job_id = ''").run(slug);
      if (names.has(slug)) indexEmails(ctx, slug, "");
    }
    const jobSkips: ListJobsSkip[] = [];
    const refs = listJobs(root, jobSkips);
    const touched = new Set([...plan.companyJobs, ...[...plan.jobs].map((k) => k.split("/")[0]!)]);
    for (const slug of plan.companyJobs) {
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM jobs WHERE company_slug = ?").run(slug);
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM events WHERE company_slug = ?").run(slug);
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM emails WHERE company_slug = ? AND job_id <> ''").run(slug); // 3b
      for (const ref of refs.filter((x) => x.company_slug === slug)) indexJob(ctx, ref, names.get(slug) ?? slug);
    }
    for (const key of plan.jobs) {
      const [slug = "", id = ""] = key.split("/");
      if (plan.companyJobs.has(slug)) continue;
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM jobs WHERE company_slug = ? AND id = ?").run(slug, id);
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM events WHERE company_slug = ? AND job_id = ?").run(slug, id);
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM emails WHERE company_slug = ? AND job_id = ?").run(slug, id); // 3b
      const ref = refs.find((x) => x.company_slug === slug && x.id === id);
      if (ref) indexJob(ctx, ref, names.get(slug) ?? slug);
    }
    for (const s of jobSkips) {
      const r = rel(root, s.file).split(path.sep);
      if (r[0] === "companies" && touched.has(r[1] ?? "")) report.skipped.push({ file: rel(root, s.file), problems: [s.problem] });
    }
    if (plan.searches) { db.exec("DELETE FROM searches"); indexSearches(ctx); }
    db.exec("COMMIT");
  } catch (e) {
    try { db.exec("ROLLBACK"); } catch { /* no open transaction */ }
    db.close();
    throw e;
  }
  db.close();
  return { ...report, mode: "incremental" };
}
```

### Task 3: Drafts, address checks, the message digest, `careeros mail draft`, and the SessionStart hook

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/drafts.ts`, `packages/core/test/drafts.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/privacy.ts` (`claudeSettings` hook, `expireGrantsAndLog`), `packages/core/src/queue.ts` (`proposeApproval` email checks), `packages/core/src/cli.ts`, `packages/core/src/workspace.ts` (`claudeMd`), `packages/core/src/index.ts`, `packages/services/test/approvals.test.ts` (one line)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `jobFilePath`, `insideRoot`, `layout`, `lockedFolderFor`, `readMeta`, `newQueueId`, `proposeApproval`, `listApprovals`, `setApprovalStatus`, `logActivity`, `readAccessRequest`, `decideAccessRequest`, `expireGrants`, `readRecord`, `writeFileAtomic`, `MailSettings`, `EmailAddress`.
- Produces:
  - `MAX_RECIPIENTS = 20`, `MAX_ATTACHMENT_BYTES = 20 MB`, `MAX_TOTAL_ATTACHMENT_BYTES = 24 MB`
  - `isEmailAddress(s): boolean`, `parseAddressList(s): EmailAddress[]`, `formatAddress(a): string`
  - `interface Draft { to: EmailAddress[]; cc: EmailAddress[]; subject: string; attach: string[]; in_reply_to: string; references: string[]; purpose: "" | "apply"; body: string }`
  - `checkDraft(d, forSend: boolean): Draft`, `parseDraft(text): Draft`, `serializeDraft(d): string`
  - `writeDraft(root, slug, id, d, draftId?, c?): { draft_id: string; rel: string }`, `readDraft(root, slug, id, draftId): Draft`, `draftFile(root, slug, id, draftId): string`, `listDraftFiles(root, slug, id): { draft_id: string; rel: string; sent: boolean; mtime: string }[]`, `moveDraftToSent(root, slug, id, draftId): void`
  - `workspaceRel(root, p): string`
  - `interface ResolvedAttachment { rel; name; size; sha256; type; locked: string | null; content: Buffer }`, `resolveAttachment(root, rel, c?)`, `mimeTypeFor(name): string`
  - `interface MessageStamp { message_id: string; date: string }`, `newStamp(draftId, account, c?)`
  - `interface CanonicalMessage { draft_id; message_id; date; from: EmailAddress; to; cc; subject; in_reply_to; references; text; attachments: { rel; name; size; sha256; type; locked: string | null }[] }`
  - `messageText(body, signature): string`, `mailAccount(root): MailSettings`, `digestOf(m): string`
  - `interface BuiltDraft { message: CanonicalMessage; digest: string; contents: Buffer[]; purpose: "" | "apply" }`, `buildDraftMessage(root, slug, id, draftId, stamp, c?): BuiltDraft`
  - `proposeEmailSend(root, slug, id, draftId, createdBy, c?): Approval` (payload: `company_slug, id, draft_id, draft, account, sha256, message_id, date, purpose`; earlier pending sends of the same draft become `declined`)
  - `claudeSettings(locked?)` also returns `hooks.SessionStart` running `careeros privacy expire`; `expireGrantsAndLog(root, c?): { expired: string[] }`
  - CLI: `careeros mail draft --job SLUG/ID --to A [--cc C] --subject S --body-file F [--attach P]... [--in-reply-to ID]`, `careeros privacy expire`

Draft file format (kept from the legacy helper the owner already uses): header lines `To:`, `Cc:`, `Subject:`, `Attach:` (one per file, workspace-relative), `In-Reply-To:`, `References:`, and the CareerOS-only `Purpose: apply` (never sent), then a blank line, then the plain-text body. `Bcc:` is refused.

```ts
    const second = proposeEmailSend(root, job.company_slug, job.id, draft_id, "app", at(1));

});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/core/src/drafts.ts`**

```ts
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { type Clock, iso } from "./clock.js";
import { jobFilePath } from "./jobs.js";
import { assertSegment, insideRoot, layout } from "./paths.js";
import { lockedFolderFor, readMeta } from "./privacy.js";
import { listApprovals, newQueueId, proposeApproval, setApprovalStatus } from "./queue.js";
import { readRecord, writeFileAtomic } from "./store.js";
import type { Approval, EmailAddress, MailSettings } from "./types.js";

export const MAX_RECIPIENTS = 20;
export const MAX_ATTACHMENT_BYTES = 20 * 1024 * 1024;
export const MAX_TOTAL_ATTACHMENT_BYTES = 24 * 1024 * 1024;
const CTRL = /[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/;
const ID = /^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;
const LOCAL = /^[A-Za-z0-9.!#$%&'*+/=?^_`{|}~-]+$/;
const DOMAIN = /^(?=.{1,253}$)[A-Za-z0-9](?:[A-Za-z0-9-]{0,61}[A-Za-z0-9])?(?:\.[A-Za-z0-9](?:[A-Za-z0-9-]{0,61}[A-Za-z0-9])?)+$/;
const MSG_ID = /^<[^<>\s]{1,995}>$/;

export interface Draft {
  to: EmailAddress[]; cc: EmailAddress[]; subject: string; attach: string[]; in_reply_to: string; references: string[];
  purpose: "" | "apply"; body: string;
}

export function isEmailAddress(s: string): boolean {
  const at = s.lastIndexOf("@");
  if (at < 1 || s.length > 254) return false;
  const local = s.slice(0, at);
  const domain = s.slice(at + 1);
  return local.length <= 64 && LOCAL.test(local) && !local.startsWith(".") && !local.endsWith(".") && !local.includes("..") && DOMAIN.test(domain);
}

/** "Name <address>" or "address", separated by commas or semicolons. Throws on anything else; never accepts a line break. */
export function parseAddressList(input: string): EmailAddress[] {
  if (CTRL.test(input.replace(/\t/g, " "))) throw new Error("Addresses can't contain line breaks or control characters.");
  const out: EmailAddress[] = [];
  for (const raw of input.split(/[,;]/).map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)) {
    const m = raw.match(/^(?:"?([^"<>]*?)"?\s*)?<([^<>\s]+)>$/);
    const name = m ? (m[1] ?? "").trim() : "";
    const address = (m ? m[2]! : raw).trim();
    if (!m && /["<>]/.test(raw)) throw new Error(`"${raw.slice(0, 80)}" isn't an email address.`);
    if (!isEmailAddress(address)) throw new Error(`"${raw.slice(0, 80)}" isn't an email address.`);
    if (/["\\]/.test(name)) throw new Error(`The name in "${raw.slice(0, 80)}" can't contain quotes or backslashes.`);
    if (!out.some((a) => a.address.toLowerCase() === address.toLowerCase())) out.push({ name, address });
  }
  if (out.length > MAX_RECIPIENTS) throw new Error(`One email can go to at most ${MAX_RECIPIENTS} people.`);
  return out;
}

export function formatAddress(a: EmailAddress): string {
  return a.name ? `"${a.name}" <${a.address}>` : a.address;
}

function checkRel(rel: string): void {
  const parts = rel.split("/");
  if (!rel || rel.length > 1024 || CTRL.test(rel) || rel.includes("\\") || path.isAbsolute(rel) || /^[A-Za-z]:/.test(rel)
    || parts.some((p) => p === "" || p === "." || p === ".." || p.startsWith("."))) {
    throw new Error(`"${rel}" can't be attached. Attach files from inside the workspace.`);
  }
}

/** Checks a draft. forSend also requires a recipient and a subject. */
export function checkDraft(d: Draft, forSend: boolean): Draft {
  if (CTRL.test(d.subject) || d.subject.length > 300) throw new Error("The subject must be one line of at most 300 characters.");
  if (d.in_reply_to && !MSG_ID.test(d.in_reply_to)) throw new Error("In-Reply-To must look like <id@host>.");
  if (d.references.length > 50 || d.references.some((r) => !MSG_ID.test(r))) throw new Error("References must be a list of <id@host> values.");
  if (d.attach.length > 10) throw new Error("Attach at most 10 files.");
  for (const rel of d.attach) checkRel(rel);
  if (d.to.length + d.cc.length > MAX_RECIPIENTS) throw new Error(`One email can go to at most ${MAX_RECIPIENTS} people.`);
  if (d.body.length > 100_000) throw new Error("The message is longer than 100,000 characters.");
  if (forSend && !d.to.length) throw new Error("Add at least one recipient in To.");
  if (forSend && !d.subject.trim()) throw new Error("Add a subject.");
  return d;
}

const HEADERS = new Set(["to", "cc", "subject", "attach", "in-reply-to", "references", "purpose"]);

export function parseDraft(text: string): Draft {
  const norm = text.replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n");
  const split = norm.indexOf("\n\n");
  const head = split < 0 ? norm : norm.slice(0, split);
  const body = split < 0 ? "" : norm.slice(split + 2);
  const d: Draft = { to: [], cc: [], subject: "", attach: [], in_reply_to: "", references: [], purpose: "", body };
  for (const line of head.split("\n")) {
    if (!line.trim()) continue;
    const m = line.match(/^([A-Za-z-]+):[ \t]?(.*)$/);
    if (!m) throw new Error(`Draft header line "${line.slice(0, 60)}" isn't "Name: value".`);
    const key = m[1]!.toLowerCase();
    const value = m[2]!.trim();
    if (key === "bcc") throw new Error("BCC isn't supported. Put every recipient in To or Cc so you can see them.");
    if (!HEADERS.has(key)) throw new Error(`Unknown draft header "${m[1]}".`);
    if (key === "to") d.to.push(...parseAddressList(value));
    else if (key === "cc") d.cc.push(...parseAddressList(value));
    else if (key === "subject") d.subject = value;
    else if (key === "attach") { if (value) d.attach.push(value); }
    else if (key === "in-reply-to") d.in_reply_to = value;
    else if (key === "references") d.references = value.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    else if (value === "apply" || value === "") d.purpose = value;
    else throw new Error(`Purpose can only be "apply".`);
  }
  return checkDraft(d, false);
}

export function serializeDraft(d: Draft): string {
  const lines = [`To: ${d.to.map(formatAddress).join(", ")}`];
  if (d.cc.length) lines.push(`Cc: ${d.cc.map(formatAddress).join(", ")}`);
  lines.push(`Subject: ${d.subject}`);
  for (const a of d.attach) lines.push(`Attach: ${a}`);
  if (d.in_reply_to) lines.push(`In-Reply-To: ${d.in_reply_to}`);
  if (d.references.length) lines.push(`References: ${d.references.join(" ")}`);
  if (d.purpose) lines.push(`Purpose: ${d.purpose}`);
  return `${lines.join("\n")}\n\n${d.body}`;
}

/** The drafts folder of a job: checked to exist, real, and inside the workspace. */
function draftsFolder(root: string, slug: string, id: string): string {
  jobFilePath(root, slug, id);
  const dir = layout(root).draftsDir(slug, id);
  fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  if (!insideRoot(root, dir) || fs.lstatSync(dir).isSymbolicLink()) throw new Error("refusing to use a drafts folder outside the workspace");
  return dir;
}

export function draftFile(root: string, slug: string, id: string, draftId: string): string {
  assertSegment(draftId, "draft id");
  if (!ID.test(draftId)) throw new Error(`bad draft id "${draftId}"`);
  return path.join(draftsFolder(root, slug, id), `${draftId}.md`);
}

const relOf = (root: string, f: string) => path.relative(root, f).split(path.sep).join("/");

export function writeDraft(root: string, slug: string, id: string, d: Draft, draftId?: string, c?: Clock): { draft_id: string; rel: string } {
  checkDraft(d, false);
  const dir = draftsFolder(root, slug, id);
  let name = draftId;
  if (!name) {
    const base = newQueueId(`${slug}/${id}/${JSON.stringify(d)}`, c);
    name = base;
    for (let n = 2; fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, `${name}.md`)); n++) name = `${base}-${n}`;
  }
  const file = draftFile(root, slug, id, name);
  writeFileAtomic(file, serializeDraft(d));
  return { draft_id: name, rel: relOf(root, file) };
}

export function readDraft(root: string, slug: string, id: string, draftId: string): Draft {
  const file = draftFile(root, slug, id, draftId);
  let st: fs.Stats;
  try { st = fs.lstatSync(file); } catch { throw new Error(`The draft ${draftId} can't be found.`); }
  if (!st.isFile()) throw new Error(`The draft ${draftId} isn't a regular file.`);
  return parseDraft(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8"));
}

export function listDraftFiles(root: string, slug: string, id: string): { draft_id: string; rel: string; sent: boolean; mtime: string }[] {
  const dir = layout(root).draftsDir(slug, id);
  const out: { draft_id: string; rel: string; sent: boolean; mtime: string }[] = [];
  for (const [folder, sent] of [[dir, false], [path.join(dir, "sent"), true]] as const) {
    let names: string[];
    try { if (fs.lstatSync(folder).isSymbolicLink() || !insideRoot(root, folder)) continue; names = fs.readdirSync(folder); } catch { continue; }
    for (const n of names.sort()) {
      const f = path.join(folder, n);
      const draftId = n.replace(/\.md$/, "");
      if (!n.endsWith(".md") || !ID.test(draftId) || !fs.lstatSync(f).isFile()) continue;
      out.push({ draft_id: draftId, rel: relOf(root, f), sent, mtime: fs.statSync(f).mtime.toISOString() });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

export function moveDraftToSent(root: string, slug: string, id: string, draftId: string): void {
  const file = draftFile(root, slug, id, draftId);
  const dest = path.join(path.dirname(file), "sent", `${draftId}.md`);
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(dest), { recursive: true });
  if (!insideRoot(root, dest)) throw new Error("refusing to move a draft outside the workspace");
  fs.renameSync(file, dest);
}

/** A path given on the command line, as a workspace-relative path with "/" separators. */
export function workspaceRel(root: string, p: string): string {
  const abs = path.isAbsolute(p) ? p : fs.existsSync(path.resolve(p)) ? path.resolve(p) : path.join(root, p);
  if (!fs.existsSync(abs)) throw new Error(`"${p}" can't be found`);
  if (!insideRoot(root, abs)) throw new Error(`"${p}" must be a file inside the workspace`);
  return path.relative(fs.realpathSync.native(root), fs.realpathSync.native(abs)).split(path.sep).join("/");
}

const TYPES: Record<string, string> = {
  ".pdf": "application/pdf", ".doc": "application/msword", ".docx": "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",
  ".md": "text/markdown", ".txt": "text/plain", ".png": "image/png", ".jpg": "image/jpeg", ".jpeg": "image/jpeg", ".gif": "image/gif",
  ".ics": "text/calendar", ".html": "text/html", ".json": "application/json", ".zip": "application/zip",
};
export const mimeTypeFor = (name: string) => TYPES[path.extname(name).toLowerCase()] ?? "application/octet-stream";

export interface ResolvedAttachment { rel: string; name: string; size: number; sha256: string; type: string; locked: string | null; content: Buffer }

/** Reads one attachment, refusing locked folders (unless allowed once), links, hidden paths and files over 20 MB. */
export function resolveAttachment(root: string, rel: string, c?: Clock): ResolvedAttachment {
  checkRel(rel);
  const abs = path.join(root, ...rel.split("/"));
  let st: fs.Stats;
  try { st = fs.lstatSync(abs); } catch { throw new Error(`"${rel}" can't be found. It may have been moved or deleted.`); }
  if (st.isSymbolicLink() || !st.isFile()) throw new Error(`"${rel}" isn't a regular file.`);
  if (!insideRoot(root, abs)) throw new Error(`"${rel}" points outside the workspace.`);
  const locked = lockedFolderFor(root, abs, c);
  if (locked) throw new Error(`${locked} is locked. Allow it once in Privacy, then review the email again.`);
  if (st.size > MAX_ATTACHMENT_BYTES) throw new Error(`"${rel}" is larger than 20 MB.`);
  const content = fs.readFileSync(abs);
  const realRel = path.relative(fs.realpathSync.native(root), fs.realpathSync.native(abs)).split(path.sep).join("/").toLowerCase();
  const lockable = readMeta(root).privacy.locked.find((d) => realRel === d || realRel.startsWith(`${d}/`)) ?? null;
  return { rel, name: path.basename(abs), size: content.length, sha256: crypto.createHash("sha256").update(content).digest("hex"), type: mimeTypeFor(abs), locked: lockable, content };
}

export interface MessageStamp { message_id: string; date: string }
export interface CanonicalMessage {
  draft_id: string; message_id: string; date: string; from: EmailAddress; to: EmailAddress[]; cc: EmailAddress[];
  subject: string; in_reply_to: string; references: string[]; text: string;
  attachments: { rel: string; name: string; size: number; sha256: string; type: string; locked: string | null }[];
}
export interface BuiltDraft { message: CanonicalMessage; digest: string; contents: Buffer[]; purpose: "" | "apply" }

export function newStamp(draftId: string, account: string, c?: Clock): MessageStamp {
  const domain = account.slice(account.lastIndexOf("@") + 1) || "localhost";
  return { message_id: `<${draftId}.${crypto.randomBytes(6).toString("hex")}@${domain}>`, date: iso(c) };
}

export function messageText(body: string, signature: string): string {
  const b = body.replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n").replace(/\s+$/, "");
  const s = signature.replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n").trim();
  return s ? `${b}\n\n${s}\n` : `${b}\n`;
}

export function mailAccount(root: string): MailSettings {
  return readRecord("integrations", layout(root).integrations).mail;
}

/** SHA-256 over every input of the MIME build, in a fixed key order. */
export function digestOf(m: CanonicalMessage): string {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify(m)).digest("hex");
}

export function buildDraftMessage(root: string, slug: string, id: string, draftId: string, stamp: MessageStamp, c?: Clock): BuiltDraft {
  const acct = mailAccount(root);
  if (!acct.address) throw new Error("Gmail isn't connected. Connect it in Settings > Email first.");
  const d = checkDraft(readDraft(root, slug, id, draftId), true);
  const atts = d.attach.map((rel) => resolveAttachment(root, rel, c));
  if (atts.reduce((n, a) => n + a.size, 0) > MAX_TOTAL_ATTACHMENT_BYTES) throw new Error("The attachments add up to more than 24 MB, which Gmail won't send.");
  const message: CanonicalMessage = {
    draft_id: draftId, message_id: stamp.message_id, date: stamp.date,
    from: { name: acct.display_name, address: acct.address },
    to: d.to.map((a) => ({ name: a.name, address: a.address })), cc: d.cc.map((a) => ({ name: a.name, address: a.address })),
    subject: d.subject.trim(), in_reply_to: d.in_reply_to, references: d.references, text: messageText(d.body, acct.signature),
    attachments: atts.map((a) => ({ rel: a.rel, name: a.name, size: a.size, sha256: a.sha256, type: a.type, locked: a.locked })),
  };
  return { message, digest: digestOf(message), contents: atts.map((a) => a.content), purpose: d.purpose };
}

export function proposeEmailSend(root: string, slug: string, id: string, draftId: string, createdBy: "app" | "claude" | "cli", c?: Clock): Approval {
  const acct = mailAccount(root);
  const stamp = newStamp(draftId, acct.address, c);
  const built = buildDraftMessage(root, slug, id, draftId, stamp, c);
  const m = built.message;
  for (const a of listApprovals(root, { status: "pending" })) {
    if (a.action === "email_send" && a.payload.company_slug === slug && a.payload.id === id && a.payload.draft_id === draftId) {
      setApprovalStatus(root, a.id, "declined", "Replaced by a newer version of the draft", c);
    }
  }
  const people = [...m.to, ...m.cc].map((a) => a.address);
  return proposeApproval(root, {
    action: "email_send",
    title: `Send "${m.subject.slice(0, 150)}"`,
    summary: `To ${people.join(", ")}${m.attachments.length ? ` with ${m.attachments.length} attachment(s)` : ""}`,
    steps: [`Send one email from ${acct.address} to ${people.length} recipient(s)`, "Save the sent copy in the job's emails folder", 'Add "Email sent" to the job timeline'],
    payload: {
      company_slug: slug, id, draft_id: draftId, draft: relOf(root, draftFile(root, slug, id, draftId)), account: acct.address,
      sha256: built.digest, message_id: stamp.message_id, date: stamp.date, purpose: built.purpose,
    },
    created_by: createdBy,
  }, c);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add the SessionStart hook and `expireGrantsAndLog` to `privacy.ts`**

In `packages/core/src/privacy.ts`, add `import { decideAccessRequest, logActivity, readAccessRequest } from "./queue.js";` and replace `claudeSettings` with:

```ts
export interface ClaudeSettings {
  permissions: { deny: string[] };
  hooks: { SessionStart: { hooks: { type: "command"; command: string }[] }[] };
}

export function claudeSettings(locked: readonly string[] = LOCKED_DIRS): ClaudeSettings {
  const deny: string[] = [];
  for (const d of locked) {
    // Edit rules cover every file-writing tool; Write(...) is not a file-permission rule.
    deny.push(`Read(./${d}/**)`, `Edit(./${d}/**)`);
    for (const cmd of BASH_READERS) deny.push(`Bash(${cmd} *${d}*)`);
    // The catch-all also covers the CLI's --jd-file and Windows-style separators.
    deny.push(`Bash(*--jd-file*${d}*)`, `Bash(*${d}*)`, `Bash(*${d.replace(/\//g, "\\")}*)`);
  }
  deny.push("Read(./settings/.env)", "Edit(./settings/.env)", "Bash(*settings/.env*)");
  // A grant left open by an app crash is closed as soon as a Claude session starts in the workspace.
  // Hooks run in the project folder, so the CLI's default workspace (the current folder) is right.
  return { permissions: { deny }, hooks: { SessionStart: [{ hooks: [{ type: "command", command: "careeros privacy expire || true" }] }] } };
}
```

Append:

```ts
/** Ends grants whose time is up, marks their requests expired and logs it. The SessionStart hook runs this. */
export function expireGrantsAndLog(root: string, c?: Clock): { expired: string[] } {
  const gone = expireGrants(root, c);
  for (const g of gone) {
    logActivity(root, "Re-locked", `${g.folder} (time is up)`, c);
    try { if (readAccessRequest(root, g.request_id).status === "allowed") decideAccessRequest(root, g.request_id, "expired", {}, c); } catch { /* request file gone */ }
  }
  return { expired: gone.map((g) => g.folder) };
}
```

## Group B: the mail package (`@careeros/mail`, spec §3, §3.2, §5)

### Task 4: `@careeros/mail` package, pinned dependencies, licence check, vendored types and the log redactor

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/mail/package.json`, `packages/mail/tsconfig.json`, `packages/mail/src/vendor-types.d.ts`, `packages/mail/src/redact.ts`, `packages/mail/src/index.ts`, `packages/mail/test/redact.test.ts`, `packages/mail/test/licences.test.ts`
- Modify: `vitest.config.ts` (alias), `docs/dependencies.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing from earlier tasks at runtime (`@careeros/core` types arrive in Task 6).
- Produces:
  - Package `@careeros/mail` (ESM, `exports: { ".": "./src/index.ts" }`), importable from `@careeros/services` and `@careeros/app-electron`
  - `redact(line: string, secrets?: readonly string[]): string` (one line, at most 500 characters, no addresses, no secrets, no IMAP or SMTP auth tokens)
  - Ambient module types for `mailparser` and `sanitize-html` in `src/vendor-types.d.ts`; every file that imports either library starts with `/// <reference path="./vendor-types.d.ts" />` so packages that compile this source through `paths` see the types too

New dependencies (exact pins, all runtime dependencies of `@careeros/mail`, run only in the main process; no native modules):

| Package | Pin | Licence | Why this one |
|---------|-----|---------|--------------|
| `imapflow` | 2.2.11 | MIT | Promise API for IMAP with `getMailboxLock(path, { readOnly: true })` (an `EXAMINE`), `BODY.PEEK[]` fetches, special-use folder flags (finds `\Sent` in any language) and TLS options we can lock down. Ships its own types. Same author as Nodemailer, actively maintained. |
| `nodemailer` | 10.0.14 | MIT-0 | SMTP over implicit TLS and `MailComposer`, which builds byte-identical MIME when the boundary, Message-ID and Date are fixed. 10.0.14 is the exact version `mailparser` 3.9.36 depends on, so one copy installs. Ships its own types. |
| `mailparser` | 3.9.36 | MIT | Parses stored `.eml` files (headers, text, HTML, inline `cid:` parts, attachments, encoded words) for the viewer, the `.md` copy and attachment saving. No types published, so `src/vendor-types.d.ts` declares the parts we use. |
| `sanitize-html` | 2.18.0 | MIT | Allow-list HTML cleaning with per-tag scheme rules, a style-property allow list and tag transforms, which we use to rewrite every image and link to `careeros-mail:` URLs. No types published; vendored the same way. Needs Node 22.12+, which the repo already requires. |

Transitive licences (checked by `licences.test.ts` below): MIT, MIT-0, ISC, BSD-2-Clause, BSD-3-Clause, Apache-2.0 and 0BSD, plus `@zone-eu/mailsplit` (from `mailparser`) under "(MIT OR EUPL-1.1+)", which we use under MIT. No `@types/*` packages are added.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { redact } from "../src/redact.js";

const addr = (u: string, d: string) => u + "@" + d;
const PASSWORD = "abcd" + "efgh" + "ijkl" + "mnop";

describe("redact", () => {
  it("removes addresses and the password, spaced or not", () => {
    const line = `login failed for ${addr("alex", "example.com")} with ${PASSWORD} (also "abcd efgh ijkl mnop")`;
    const out = redact(line, ["abcd efgh ijkl mnop"]);
    expect(out).not.toContain(addr("alex", "example.com"));
    expect(out).not.toContain(PASSWORD);
    expect(out).not.toContain("abcd efgh ijkl mnop");
    expect(out).toContain("[address]");
    expect(out).toContain("[secret]");
  });
  it("removes IMAP and SMTP auth tokens", () => {
    expect(redact("C: A1 AUTHENTICATE PLAIN AGFsZXgAc2VjcmV0")).toBe("C: A1 AUTHENTICATE PLAIN [secret]");
    expect(redact(`C: A1 LOGIN "${addr("alex", "example.com")}" "hunter2-fictional"`)).toBe("C: A1 LOGIN [address] [secret]");
    expect(redact("AUTH PLAIN AGFsZXgAc2VjcmV0")).toBe("AUTH PLAIN [secret]");
  });
  it("keeps one short line and leaves counts alone", () => {
    expect(redact("stored=2 triaged=1\nskipped=5")).toBe("stored=2 triaged=1 skipped=5");
    expect(redact("x".repeat(900))).toHaveLength(500);
  });
});
```

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const REPO = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "../../..");
const PKG = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "../package.json"), "utf8")) as { dependencies: Record<string, string> };
const OK = new Set(["MIT", "MIT-0", "ISC", "BSD-2-Clause", "BSD-3-Clause", "Apache-2.0", "0BSD", "(MIT OR EUPL-1.1+)"]);

/** node_modules lookup the way Node does it: the nearest node_modules folder above `from` that has the package. */
function findPackage(name: string, from: string): string | null {
  for (let dir = from; ; dir = path.dirname(dir)) {
    const f = path.join(dir, "node_modules", name, "package.json");
    if (fs.existsSync(f)) return f;
    if (dir === path.dirname(dir)) return null;
  }
}

describe("mail dependencies", () => {
  it("are pinned exactly", () => {
    for (const [name, v] of Object.entries(PKG.dependencies)) expect(v, name).toMatch(/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/);
  });
  it("use only licences we can ship, all the way down", () => {
    const seen = new Map<string, string>();
    const bad: string[] = [];
    const walk = (name: string, from: string) => {
      const f = findPackage(name, from);
      if (!f) { bad.push(`${name}: not installed`); return; }
      if (seen.has(f)) return;
      const p = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(f, "utf8")) as { name: string; version: string; license?: string; dependencies?: Record<string, string> };
      seen.set(f, `${p.name}@${p.version}`);
      if (!OK.has(String(p.license))) bad.push(`${p.name}@${p.version}: ${String(p.license)}`);
      for (const d of Object.keys(p.dependencies ?? {})) walk(d, path.dirname(f));
    };
    for (const name of Object.keys(PKG.dependencies).filter((n) => !n.startsWith("@careeros/"))) walk(name, path.join(REPO, "packages", "mail"));
    expect(bad).toEqual([]);
    expect(seen.size).toBeGreaterThan(4);
  });
});
```

```ts
// Minimal types for the two mail libraries that publish none. Only what CareerOS uses.

declare module "mailparser" {
  export interface AddressEntry { name: string; address?: string; group?: AddressEntry[] }
  export interface AddressObject { value: AddressEntry[]; text: string; html: string }
  export interface Attachment {
    filename?: string; contentType: string; contentDisposition?: string; size: number; content: Buffer;
    contentId?: string; cid?: string; related?: boolean; checksum?: string;
  }

    headers: Map<string, unknown>;
    headerLines: { key: string; line: string }[];
    subject?: string; from?: AddressObject; to?: AddressObject | AddressObject[]; cc?: AddressObject | AddressObject[];
    replyTo?: AddressObject; date?: Date; messageId?: string; inReplyTo?: string; references?: string | string[];
    html: string | false; text?: string; textAsHtml?: string; attachments: Attachment[];
  }
  export interface SimpleParserOptions {
    keepCidLinks?: boolean; skipHtmlToText?: boolean; skipImageLinks?: boolean; skipTextToHtml?: boolean;
    skipTextLinks?: boolean; maxHtmlLengthToParse?: number;
  }
  export function simpleParser(source: Buffer | string, options?: SimpleParserOptions): Promise<ParsedMail>;
}

declare module "sanitize-html" {
  namespace sanitizeHtml {
    interface Attributes { [name: string]: string }
    interface Frame { tag: string; attribs: Attributes; text: string; tagPosition: number }
    type Transformer = (tagName: string, attribs: Attributes) => { tagName: string; attribs: Attributes; text?: string };
    interface IOptions {
      allowedTags?: string[] | false;
      allowedAttributes?: Record<string, string[]> | false;
      allowedSchemes?: string[];
      allowedSchemesByTag?: Record<string, string[]>;
      allowedSchemesAppliedToAttributes?: string[];
      allowProtocolRelative?: boolean;
      allowedStyles?: Record<string, Record<string, RegExp[]>>;
      parseStyleAttributes?: boolean;
      transformTags?: Record<string, string | Transformer>;
      exclusiveFilter?: (frame: Frame) => boolean;
      disallowedTagsMode?: "discard" | "escape" | "recursiveEscape";
      nonTextTags?: string[];
      enforceHtmlBoundary?: boolean;
    }
  }
  function sanitizeHtml(dirty: string, options?: sanitizeHtml.IOptions): string;
  export = sanitizeHtml;
}
```

`packages/mail/src/redact.ts`:

```ts
const EMAIL = /[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}/g;

/**
 * Makes a log line safe to keep: no secrets (exact, or with the spaces Google shows removed), no IMAP or SMTP
 * auth tokens, no email addresses, one line, at most 500 characters. Every mail log line goes through this.
 */
export function redact(line: string, secrets: readonly string[] = []): string {
  let out = String(line);
  for (const s of secrets) {
    for (const v of new Set([s, s.replace(/\s+/g, "")])) if (v.length >= 4) out = out.split(v).join("[secret]");
  }
  out = out
    .replace(/\b(AUTH(?:ENTICATE)?\s+(?:PLAIN|LOGIN|XOAUTH2)\s+)\S+/gi, "$1[secret]")
    .replace(/\b(LOGIN\s+)("[^"]*"|\S+)\s+("[^"]*"|\S+)/g, "$1[address] [secret]")
    .replace(EMAIL, "[address]")
    .replace(/[\r\n]+/g, " ");
  return out.slice(0, 500);
}
```

### Task 5: HTML sanitizer and the viewer document (XSS corpus)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/mail/src/sanitize.ts`, `packages/mail/test/sanitize.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/mail/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `sanitize-html` (Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `MAIL_FRAME_CSP = "default-src 'none'; img-src careeros-mail: data:; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'"`
  - `interface MailLink { href: string; text: string }`, `interface SanitizedBody { html: string; remoteImages: string[]; links: MailLink[] }`
  - `sanitizeEmailHtml(html: string, opts: { token: string; imagesAllowed: boolean }): SanitizedBody`
  - `textToHtml(text: string): string` (escapes, keeps line breaks, turns `https://` addresses into links that then go through the sanitizer)
  - `renderMailDocument(bodyHtml: string): string` (the full document served to the sandboxed frame)
  - `linkWarning(text: string, href: string): string` ("" when the visible text doesn't name a different site)
  - URL forms used everywhere: `careeros-mail://link/<token>/<n>`, `careeros-mail://img/<token>/<n>`, `careeros-mail://cid/<token>/<encoded content-id>`

Rules: every `https:` link becomes `careeros-mail://link/<token>/<n>` (the n-th entry of `links`); any other link loses its `href`. `cid:` images become `careeros-mail://cid/...`. `https:` images are counted in `remoteImages` and get a `careeros-mail://img/<token>/<n>` source only when `imagesAllowed`; otherwise they have no source. `data:` images are kept only for PNG, GIF, JPEG and WebP. Styles are an allow list of properties whose values may not contain `url(`, any function other than `rgb()`/`rgba()`/`hsl()`/`hsla()`, backslashes, braces or `@`. `<style>` elements are removed with their content.

```ts
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { linkWarning, MAIL_FRAME_CSP, renderMailDocument, sanitizeEmailHtml, textToHtml } from "../src/sanitize.js";

const T = "dG9rZW4";
const clean = (html: string, imagesAllowed = false) => sanitizeEmailHtml(html, { token: T, imagesAllowed });
const TAGS = new Set(["a", "abbr", "b", "blockquote", "br", "caption", "center", "cite", "code", "col", "colgroup", "dd", "del", "div", "dl", "dt", "em", "font", "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6", "hr", "i", "img", "ins", "kbd", "li", "mark", "ol", "p", "pre", "q", "s", "small", "span", "strike", "strong", "sub", "sup", "table", "tbody", "td", "tfoot", "th", "thead", "tr", "tt", "u", "ul"]);
const ATTRS = new Set(["style", "align", "dir", "lang", "title", "href", "src", "alt", "width", "height", "color", "size", "face", "border", "cellpadding", "cellspacing", "bgcolor", "valign", "colspan", "rowspan", "nowrap", "span", "start", "type", "value"]);

/** Parses the output the way the viewer frame will and lists anything that could run, load or submit. */
function problems(html: string): string[] {
  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(`<!doctype html><body>${html}`, "text/html");
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const el of Array.from(doc.body.querySelectorAll("*"))) {
    if (!TAGS.has(el.tagName.toLowerCase())) out.push(`tag ${el.tagName}`);
    for (const a of Array.from(el.attributes)) {
      if (!ATTRS.has(a.name)) out.push(`attr ${a.name}`);
      if ((a.name === "href" || a.name === "src") && !/^(careeros-mail:\/\/(link|img|cid)\/|data:image\/(png|gif|jpeg|webp);base64,)/i.test(a.value)) out.push(`${a.name}=${a.value}`);
      if (a.name === "style" && /url\s*\(|expression|@import|\\|javascript:/i.test(a.value)) out.push(`style ${a.value}`);

  return out;
}

const CORPUS = [

    expect(r.html).toBe(`<p><a href="careeros-mail://link/${T}/0">Apply now</a> <a>mail</a></p>`);
    expect(r.links).toEqual([{ href: "https://jobs.example.com/apply?id=1&x=2", text: "Apply now" }]);
  });

    expect(blocked.html).not.toContain("src=");
    expect(blocked.html).toContain('alt="Acme"');
    expect(clean(html, true).html).toBe(`<img alt="Acme" src="careeros-mail://img/${T}/0" /><img src="careeros-mail://img/${T}/1" />`);
  });
  it("shows inline cid images and small raster data images", () => {
    expect(clean('<img src="cid:logo@acme">').html).toBe(`<img src="careeros-mail://cid/${T}/logo%40acme" />`);
    const png = "data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgo=";
    expect(clean(`<img src="${png}">`).html).toBe(`<img src="${png}" />`);
  });

  });
```
