```ts
    const html = '<table width="600" cellpadding="0"><tr><td align="center" style="padding: 8px"><h1>Acme Corp</h1><p>Hi Alex,<br>Thanks for applying.</p></td></tr></table>';
    expect(clean(html).html).toMatchSnapshot();
  });
});

describe("plain text, documents and link warnings", () => {
  it("escapes plain text and links https addresses", () => {
    const html = textToHtml("Hi <Alex>,\nApply at https://jobs.example.com/a?b=1&c=2 today.");
    expect(html).toBe('<pre>Hi &lt;Alex&gt;,\nApply at <a href="https://jobs.example.com/a?b=1&amp;c=2">https://jobs.example.com/a?b=1&amp;c=2</a> today.</pre>');
    expect(clean(html).links[0]?.href).toBe("https://jobs.example.com/a?b=1&c=2");
  });
  it("wraps the body in a document with the frame CSP and no referrer", () => {
    const doc = renderMailDocument("<p>Hi</p>");
    expect(doc).toContain(`<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="${MAIL_FRAME_CSP}">`);
    expect(doc).toContain('<meta name="referrer" content="no-referrer">');
    expect(doc).toContain("<body><p>Hi</p></body>");
    expect(MAIL_FRAME_CSP).toBe("default-src 'none'; img-src careeros-mail: data:; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'");
  });
  it("warns when the link text names a different site than the target", () => {
    expect(linkWarning("www.acme.example.com/careers", "https://acme-careers.example.net/login")).toBe("The link text shows www.acme.example.com but it goes to acme-careers.example.net.");
    expect(linkWarning("jobs.example.com", "https://apply.jobs.example.com/x")).toBe("");
    expect(linkWarning("Apply now", "https://jobs.example.com/x")).toBe("");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/mail/src/sanitize.ts`**

```ts
/// <reference path="./vendor-types.d.ts" />
import sanitizeHtml from "sanitize-html";

export const MAIL_FRAME_CSP = "default-src 'none'; img-src careeros-mail: data:; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'";

export interface MailLink { href: string; text: string }
export interface SanitizedBody { html: string; remoteImages: string[]; links: MailLink[] }

const TAGS = [
  "a", "abbr", "b", "blockquote", "br", "caption", "center", "cite", "code", "col", "colgroup", "dd", "del", "div", "dl", "dt", "em", "font",
  "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6", "hr", "i", "img", "ins", "kbd", "li", "mark", "ol", "p", "pre", "q", "s", "small", "span", "strike",
  "strong", "sub", "sup", "table", "tbody", "td", "tfoot", "th", "thead", "tr", "tt", "u", "ul",
];
const CELL = ["align", "valign", "width", "height", "colspan", "rowspan", "bgcolor", "nowrap"];
const ATTRS: Record<string, string[]> = {
  "*": ["style", "align", "dir", "lang", "title"],
  a: ["href"], img: ["src", "alt", "width", "height"], font: ["color", "size", "face"],
  table: ["width", "height", "border", "cellpadding", "cellspacing", "bgcolor", "align"], td: CELL, th: CELL, tr: ["align", "valign", "bgcolor"],
  col: ["span", "width"], colgroup: ["span", "width"], ol: ["start", "type"], li: ["value"],
};
/** Plain words, numbers, colours and rgb()/hsl() only: no url(), no other functions, no escapes, braces or at-rules. */
const SAFE_VALUE = /^(?=[\s\S]{0,200}$)(?:[-#%,.\s\w"'/!+]|(?:rgba?|hsla?)\([\d\s.,%/]*\))*$/i;
const STYLE_PROPS = [
  "color", "background", "background-color", "font", "font-size", "font-weight", "font-style", "font-family", "text-align", "text-decoration",
  "text-transform", "line-height", "letter-spacing", "white-space", "word-break", "overflow-wrap", "vertical-align", "display",
  "width", "min-width", "max-width", "height", "min-height", "max-height", "list-style-type", "table-layout", "border-collapse", "border-spacing",
  "padding", "padding-top", "padding-right", "padding-bottom", "padding-left", "margin", "margin-top", "margin-right", "margin-bottom", "margin-left",
  "border", "border-top", "border-right", "border-bottom", "border-left", "border-color", "border-width", "border-style", "border-radius",
];
const STYLES = { "*": Object.fromEntries(STYLE_PROPS.map((p) => [p, [SAFE_VALUE]])) };
const DATA_IMAGE = /^data:image\/(?:png|gif|jpeg|webp);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/=\s]+$/i;
const SCHEME = "careeros-mail://";

function httpsUrl(s: string): string | null {
  try {
    const u = new URL(s);
    return u.protocol === "https:" && u.hostname && !u.username && !u.password ? u.href : null;
  } catch { return null; }
}

const decode = (s: string) => s.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, "").replace(/&lt;/g, "<").replace(/&gt;/g, ">").replace(/&quot;/g, '"').replace(/&#39;/g, "'").replace(/&amp;/g, "&").trim();

export function sanitizeEmailHtml(input: string, opts: { token: string; imagesAllowed: boolean }): SanitizedBody {
  const remoteImages: string[] = [];
  const links: MailLink[] = [];
  const html = sanitizeHtml(input.slice(0, 2_000_000), {
    allowedTags: TAGS,
    allowedAttributes: ATTRS,
    allowedSchemes: [],
    allowedSchemesByTag: { a: ["careeros-mail"], img: ["careeros-mail", "data"] },
    allowedSchemesAppliedToAttributes: ["href", "src", "cite"],
    allowProtocolRelative: false,
    allowedStyles: STYLES,
    parseStyleAttributes: true,
    disallowedTagsMode: "discard",
    nonTextTags: ["style", "script", "textarea", "option", "noscript", "title", "head", "template"],
    enforceHtmlBoundary: true,
    transformTags: {
      a: (_tag, attribs) => {
        const { href = "", target: _t, rel: _r, ...rest } = attribs;
        const url = httpsUrl(href.trim());
        if (!url) return { tagName: "a", attribs: rest };
        links.push({ href: url, text: "" });
        return { tagName: "a", attribs: { ...rest, href: `${SCHEME}link/${opts.token}/${links.length - 1}` } };
      },
      img: (_tag, attribs) => {
        const { src = "", srcset: _s, ...rest } = attribs;
        const s = src.trim();
        if (/^cid:/i.test(s)) return { tagName: "img", attribs: { ...rest, src: `${SCHEME}cid/${opts.token}/${encodeURIComponent(s.slice(4).replace(/^<|>$/g, ""))}` } };
        const url = httpsUrl(s);
        if (url) {
          remoteImages.push(url);
          return { tagName: "img", attribs: opts.imagesAllowed ? { ...rest, src: `${SCHEME}img/${opts.token}/${remoteImages.length - 1}` } : rest };
        }
        return { tagName: "img", attribs: DATA_IMAGE.test(s) ? { ...rest, src: s } : rest };
      },
    },
  });
  // The visible text of each rewritten link, for the "this link goes somewhere else" warning.
  const re = new RegExp(`<a[^>]*href="careeros-mail://link/${opts.token}/(\\d+)"[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*?)</a>`, "g");
  for (const m of html.matchAll(re)) {
    const link = links[Number(m[1])];
    if (link && !link.text) link.text = decode(m[2] ?? "").slice(0, 300);
  }
  return { html, remoteImages, links };
}

const esc = (s: string) => s.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;");

export function textToHtml(text: string): string {
  const body = esc(text.slice(0, 1_000_000)).replace(/https:\/\/[^\s<>"']+/g, (u) => `<a href="${u}">${u}</a>`);
  return `<pre>${body}</pre>`;
}

export function renderMailDocument(bodyHtml: string): string {
  return [
    "<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset=\"utf-8\">",
    `<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="${MAIL_FRAME_CSP}">`,
    '<meta name="referrer" content="no-referrer">',
    "<style>html{color-scheme:light}body{margin:0;padding:1rem;background:white;color:black;font:0.9375rem/1.5 system-ui,sans-serif;overflow-wrap:anywhere}",
    "img{max-width:100%;height:auto}table{max-width:100%}pre{white-space:pre-wrap;font:inherit;margin:0}a{color:LinkText}</style>",
    `</head><body>${bodyHtml}</body></html>`,
  ].join("");
}

const hostLike = (s: string): string | null => {
  const m = s.toLowerCase().match(/(?:https?:\/\/)?((?:[a-z0-9-]+\.)+[a-z]{2,})(?=$|[/:?#\s])/);
  return m ? m[1]! : null;
};

export function linkWarning(text: string, href: string): string {
  const shown = hostLike(text.trim());
  let target: string;
  try { target = new URL(href).hostname.toLowerCase(); } catch { return ""; }
  if (!shown) return "";
  const bare = shown.replace(/^www\./, "");
  if (target === shown || target === bare || target.endsWith(`.${bare}`)) return "";
  return `The link text shows ${shown} but it goes to ${target}.`;
}
```

### Task 6: Message parsing (addresses, threading, attachments, auth results, missing headers)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/mail/src/parse.ts`, `packages/mail/src/testing.ts`, `packages/mail/test/parse.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/mail/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `mailparser` (Task 4); `EmailAddress` (core, Task 1).
- Produces:
  - `interface ParsedAttachment { part: string; name: string; size: number; type: string; cid: string; inline: boolean; content: Buffer }`
  - `interface ParsedMessage { message_id: string; synthetic_id: boolean; in_reply_to: string; references: string[]; thread_key: string; from: EmailAddress; to: EmailAddress[]; cc: EmailAddress[]; reply_to: EmailAddress[]; subject: string; date: string | null; text: string; html: string; attachments: ParsedAttachment[]; auth: "pass" | "fail" | "none" }`
  - `parseMessage(raw: Buffer): Promise<ParsedMessage>`
  - `syntheticMessageId(raw: Buffer): string` (stable for the same bytes: `<32 hex>.synthetic@localhost` in angle brackets)
  - `authResult(lines: string[]): "pass" | "fail" | "none"`
  - `normalizeSubject(s: string): string` (drops any number of `Re:`, `Fwd:`, `Fw:`, `AW:`, `SV:`, `RE:` prefixes)
  - `src/testing.ts` (shipped in the package because services tests and the end-to-end fixture use it too): `addr(user, domain): string`, `ALEX`, `TALENT`, `interface RawOptions`, `rawMessage(o?: RawOptions): Buffer`

Attachment `part` numbers are the 1-based position in mailparser's attachment list. Parsing the same bytes always gives the same list, so a part number in a sidecar always finds the same attachment again.

```ts
// Fictional message builders for tests and the end-to-end fixture. Addresses are built by concatenation on purpose.
export const addr = (user: string, domain: string) => user + "@" + domain;
export const ALEX = addr("alex", "example.com");
export const TALENT = addr("talent", "example.org");

export interface RawOptions {
  from?: string; fromName?: string; to?: string; cc?: string; replyTo?: string; subject?: string;
  /** null leaves the header out. */
  date?: string | null;
  /** The part before the @ of the Message-ID; null leaves the header out. */
  id?: string | null;
  inReplyTo?: string; references?: string; auth?: string; text?: string; html?: string;
  attachments?: { name: string; type: string; content: Buffer; cid?: string }[];
  extraHeaders?: string[];
}

const b64 = (b: Buffer) => b.toString("base64").replace(/.{1,76}/g, "$&\r\n").trimEnd();

/** A small RFC 5322 message. Plain text unless html or attachments are given. */
export function rawMessage(o: RawOptions = {}): Buffer {
  const h: string[] = [];
  if (o.auth) h.push(`Authentication-Results: ${o.auth}`);
  h.push(`From: ${o.fromName ? `"${o.fromName}" ` : ""}<${o.from ?? TALENT}>`);
  h.push(`To: <${o.to ?? ALEX}>`);
  if (o.cc) h.push(`Cc: <${o.cc}>`);
  if (o.replyTo) h.push(`Reply-To: <${o.replyTo}>`);
  h.push(`Subject: ${o.subject ?? "Interview for AI Engineer"}`);
  if (o.date !== null) h.push(`Date: ${o.date ?? "Wed, 14 Jan 2026 09:00:00 +0000"}`);
  if (o.id !== null) h.push(`Message-ID: <${o.id ?? "m1"}${"@"}example.org>`);
  if (o.inReplyTo) h.push(`In-Reply-To: ${o.inReplyTo}`);
  if (o.references) h.push(`References: ${o.references}`);
  h.push(...(o.extraHeaders ?? []), "MIME-Version: 1.0");
  const text = o.text ?? "Hi Alex,\r\n\r\nCould you talk on Friday?\r\n\r\nAcme Talent";
  if (!o.html && !o.attachments?.length) {
    return Buffer.from([...h, "Content-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8", "Content-Transfer-Encoding: 8bit", "", text, ""].join("\r\n"), "utf8");
  }
  const parts: string[] = [
    "--outer", "Content-Type: multipart/alternative; boundary=\"alt\"", "",
    "--alt", "Content-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8", "Content-Transfer-Encoding: 8bit", "", text,
  ];
  if (o.html) parts.push("--alt", "Content-Type: text/html; charset=utf-8", "Content-Transfer-Encoding: 8bit", "", o.html);
  parts.push("--alt--");
  for (const a of o.attachments ?? []) {
    parts.push("--outer", `Content-Type: ${a.type}; name="${a.name}"`,
      a.cid ? `Content-Disposition: inline; filename="${a.name}"` : `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="${a.name}"`,
      ...(a.cid ? [`Content-ID: <${a.cid}>`] : []), "Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64", "", b64(a.content));
  }
  parts.push("--outer--", "");
  return Buffer.from([...h, "Content-Type: multipart/mixed; boundary=\"outer\"", "", ...parts].join("\r\n"), "utf8");
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { authResult, normalizeSubject, parseMessage, syntheticMessageId } from "../src/parse.js";
import { addr, ALEX, rawMessage, TALENT } from "../src/testing.js";

const PNG = Buffer.from("iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAQAAAC1HAwCAAAAC0lEQVR42mNkYAAAAAYAAjCB0C8AAAAASUVORK5CYII=", "base64");
const mid = (s: string) => "<" + s + "@" + "example.org>";

describe("parseMessage", () => {
  it("reads the header fields and the text", async () => {
    const p = await parseMessage(rawMessage({ fromName: "Acme Talent", cc: addr("hr", "example.net"), replyTo: addr("jobs", "example.org") }));
    expect(p).toMatchObject({
      message_id: mid("m1"), synthetic_id: false, subject: "Interview for AI Engineer", date: "2026-01-14T09:00:00.000Z",
      from: { name: "Acme Talent", address: TALENT }, to: [{ name: "", address: ALEX }], cc: [{ name: "", address: addr("hr", "example.net") }],
      reply_to: [{ name: "", address: addr("jobs", "example.org") }], auth: "none", attachments: [], html: "",
    });
    expect(p.text).toContain("Could you talk on Friday?");
    expect(p.thread_key).toBe(mid("m1"));
  });
  it("threads replies on the first reference, then In-Reply-To", async () => {
    const p = await parseMessage(rawMessage({ id: "m3", inReplyTo: mid("m2"), references: `${mid("m1")} ${mid("m2")}` }));
    expect(p).toMatchObject({ in_reply_to: mid("m2"), references: [mid("m1"), mid("m2")], thread_key: mid("m1") });
    expect((await parseMessage(rawMessage({ id: "m4", inReplyTo: mid("m2") }))).thread_key).toBe(mid("m2"));
  });
  it("lists attachments with stable part numbers and inline cid images (Review Focus 1: long, non-ASCII names)", async () => {
    const long = `${"Résumé – 面接 ".repeat(20)}.pdf`;
    const p = await parseMessage(rawMessage({
      html: '<p>Hi <img src="cid:logo@acme"></p>',
      attachments: [{ name: "logo.png", type: "image/png", content: PNG, cid: "logo@acme" }, { name: "Interview details.pdf", type: "application/pdf", content: Buffer.from("%PDF-1.4 fictional") }, { name: long, type: "application/pdf", content: Buffer.from("%PDF-1.4") }],
    }));
    expect(p.attachments.map(({ content: _c, ...a }) => a)).toEqual([
      { part: "1", name: "logo.png", size: PNG.length, type: "image/png", cid: "logo@acme", inline: true },
      { part: "2", name: "Interview details.pdf", size: 18, type: "application/pdf", cid: "", inline: false },
      { part: "3", name: long, size: 8, type: "application/pdf", cid: "", inline: false },
    ]);
    expect(p.html).toContain('src="cid:logo@acme"');
  });
  it("makes up a stable id and leaves the date empty when the headers are missing (Review Focus 3)", async () => {
    const raw = rawMessage({ id: null, date: null });
    const a = await parseMessage(raw);
    const b = await parseMessage(Buffer.from(raw));
    expect(a.synthetic_id).toBe(true);
    expect(a.message_id).toBe(syntheticMessageId(raw));
    expect(a.message_id).toMatch(/^<[0-9a-f]{32}\.synthetic@localhost>$/);
    expect(b.message_id).toBe(a.message_id);
    expect(a.date).toBeNull();
    expect(a.thread_key).toBe(a.message_id);
  });
  it("decodes encoded subjects in other scripts and strips control characters", async () => {
    const subject = "Entrevista ✓ 面接 für Alex";
    const p = await parseMessage(rawMessage({ subject: `=?UTF-8?B?${Buffer.from(subject, "utf8").toString("base64")}?=` }));
    expect(p.subject).toBe(subject);
  });
  it("reads Gmail's authentication result", async () => {
    const fail = "mx.google.com; dkim=none; spf=fail smtp.mailfrom=example.org; dmarc=fail (p=REJECT) header.from=example.org";
    expect((await parseMessage(rawMessage({ auth: fail }))).auth).toBe("fail");
    expect((await parseMessage(rawMessage({ auth: "mx.google.com; dkim=pass; spf=pass; dmarc=pass header.from=example.org" }))).auth).toBe("pass");
  });
});

describe("helpers", () => {
  it("trusts the receiving server's line over a forged one", () => {
    expect(authResult(["mx.google.com; spf=fail; dmarc=fail", "evil.example.com; dmarc=pass"])).toBe("fail");
    expect(authResult(["evil.example.com; dmarc=pass", "mx.google.com; spf=pass"])).toBe("pass");
    expect(authResult([])).toBe("none");
  });
  it("normalises reply and forward prefixes", () => {
    expect(normalizeSubject("Re: RE: Fwd: AW: Interview")).toBe("Interview");
    expect(normalizeSubject("  Interview ")).toBe("Interview");
  });
});
```

```ts
/// <reference path="./vendor-types.d.ts" />
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import { simpleParser, type AddressEntry, type AddressObject } from "mailparser";
import type { EmailAddress } from "@careeros/core";

export interface ParsedAttachment { part: string; name: string; size: number; type: string; cid: string; inline: boolean; content: Buffer }
export interface ParsedMessage {
  message_id: string; synthetic_id: boolean; in_reply_to: string; references: string[]; thread_key: string;
  from: EmailAddress; to: EmailAddress[]; cc: EmailAddress[]; reply_to: EmailAddress[]; subject: string; date: string | null;
  text: string; html: string; attachments: ParsedAttachment[]; auth: "pass" | "fail" | "none";
}

const MSG_ID = /<[^<>\s]{1,995}>/g;
const CTRL = /[\x00-\x1f\x7f]+/g;

export function syntheticMessageId(raw: Buffer): string {
  return `<${crypto.createHash("sha256").update(raw).digest("hex").slice(0, 32)}.synthetic${"@"}localhost>`;
}

function ids(v: string | string[] | undefined): string[] {
  const s = Array.isArray(v) ? v.join(" ") : v ?? "";
  return [...new Set(s.match(MSG_ID) ?? [])].slice(0, 50);
}

function addresses(v: AddressObject | AddressObject[] | undefined): EmailAddress[] {
  const out: EmailAddress[] = [];
  const walk = (list: AddressEntry[]) => {
    for (const a of list) {
      if (a.group) walk(a.group);
      else if (a.address && !out.some((x) => x.address.toLowerCase() === a.address!.toLowerCase())) {
        out.push({ name: (a.name ?? "").replace(CTRL, " ").trim().slice(0, 300), address: a.address.trim().slice(0, 320) });
      }
    }
  };
  for (const o of Array.isArray(v) ? v : v ? [v] : []) walk(o.value);
  return out.slice(0, 100);
}

/** Uses the line the receiving server (Gmail) added; a sender can forge lines further down. */
export function authResult(lines: string[]): "pass" | "fail" | "none" {
  const line = lines.find((l) => /^\s*mx\.google\.com\s*;/i.test(l)) ?? lines[0] ?? "";
  if (/\b(?:dmarc|spf)=fail\b/i.test(line)) return "fail";
  if (/\b(?:dmarc|spf)=pass\b/i.test(line)) return "pass";
  return "none";
}

export function normalizeSubject(s: string): string {
  let out = s.trim();
  for (let prev = ""; prev !== out;) { prev = out; out = out.replace(/^(?:re|fwd?|aw|sv)\s*:\s*/i, ""); }
  return out.trim();
}

export async function parseMessage(raw: Buffer): Promise<ParsedMessage> {
  const p = await simpleParser(raw, { keepCidLinks: true, skipImageLinks: true, skipTextLinks: true, skipTextToHtml: true, maxHtmlLengthToParse: 2_000_000 });
  const own = p.headers.has("message-id") ? ids(p.messageId)[0] : undefined;
  const message_id = own ?? syntheticMessageId(raw);
  const references = ids(p.references);
  const in_reply_to = ids(p.inReplyTo)[0] ?? "";
  const date = p.headers.has("date") && p.date instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(p.date.getTime()) ? p.date.toISOString() : null;
  return {
    message_id, synthetic_id: !own, in_reply_to, references, thread_key: references[0] ?? (in_reply_to || message_id),
    from: addresses(p.from)[0] ?? { name: "", address: "unknown" },
    to: addresses(p.to), cc: addresses(p.cc), reply_to: addresses(p.replyTo),
    subject: (p.subject ?? "").replace(CTRL, " ").trim().slice(0, 2000), date,
    text: p.text ?? "", html: p.html || "",
    attachments: p.attachments.map((a, i) => ({
      part: String(i + 1), name: (a.filename ?? `attachment-${i + 1}`).replace(CTRL, " ").trim().slice(0, 300), size: a.size ?? a.content.length,
      type: (a.contentType || "application/octet-stream").toLowerCase(), cid: (a.cid ?? a.contentId ?? "").replace(/^<|>$/g, ""),
      inline: a.contentDisposition === "inline" || a.related === true, content: a.content,
    })),
    auth: authResult(p.headerLines.filter((h) => h.key === "authentication-results").map((h) => h.line.replace(/^authentication-results:\s*/i, ""))),
  };
}
```

### Task 7: Matching a message to a company and job, and the job-related rule

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/mail/data/job-senders.json`, `packages/mail/src/match.ts`, `packages/mail/test/match.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/mail/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `EmailAddress`, `EmailMatchKind` (core, Task 1).
- Produces:
  - `interface JobInfo { id: string; title: string; status: string; last_activity: string }`
  - `interface CompanyInfo { slug: string; name: string; aliases: string[]; website: string; recruiter_email: string; email_match: string[]; jobs: JobInfo[] }`
  - `interface HeaderInfo { direction: "in" | "out"; from: EmailAddress; to: EmailAddress[]; cc: EmailAddress[]; subject: string }`
  - `interface MatchResult { company_slug: string; job_id: string; matched_by: EmailMatchKind }`
  - `interface JobSenders { domains: string[]; words: string[] }`, `JOB_SENDERS: JobSenders` (from `data/job-senders.json`)
  - `domainOf(address): string`, `websiteDomain(website): string`, `domainMatches(domain, base): boolean`, `coreName(name): string`
  - `counterparts(h, own?): EmailAddress[]` (the sender for incoming mail, the recipients for sent mail, never your own address)
  - `matchCompany(h, companies, own?): { company: CompanyInfo; matched_by: EmailMatchKind } | null`, `pickJob(company, subject): string`, `matchMessage(h, companies, own?): MatchResult | null`
  - `isJobRelated(h, list?): boolean`, `suggestCompany(h, companies): { company_slug: string; company_name: string; reason: string } | null`
  - `interface IgnoreList { senders: string[]; domains: string[] }`, `isIgnored(address, ignore): boolean`

Matching order (spec §3.2, first hit wins, checked rule by rule across all companies): 1 `recruiter` (the company's `recruiter.email`); 2 `email_match` (an address in the list); 3 `domain` (counterpart domain equals or is under the company website's domain; never for free-mail or job-board domains); 4 `email_match` (a domain or a whole word in the list); 5 `subject` (company name without legal suffix, or an alias, as whole words; the longest name wins). The job: the only job; else the job whose title words all appear in the subject (most words wins); else the most recent job in Applying, Applied or Interview; else "" (the company folder). Display names are never used for matching.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  coreName, counterparts, domainMatches, isIgnored, isJobRelated, matchMessage, pickJob, suggestCompany, websiteDomain,
  type CompanyInfo, type HeaderInfo,
} from "../src/match.js";
import { addr, ALEX, TALENT } from "../src/testing.js";

const job = (id: string, title: string, status = "Found", last_activity = "2026-01-12T09:00:00.000Z") => ({ id, title, status, last_activity });
const ACME: CompanyInfo = {
  slug: "acme-corp", name: "Acme Corp", aliases: [], website: "https://www.acme.example.com/careers", recruiter_email: TALENT, email_match: [],
  jobs: [job("2026-01-12-ai-engineer", "AI Engineer", "Applied")],
};
const LABS: CompanyInfo = {
  slug: "example-labs", name: "Example Labs Pty Ltd", aliases: [], website: "examplelabs.example.net", recruiter_email: "",
  email_match: [addr("hiring", "partners.example.com"), "labs-talent.example.com", "LLM"],
  jobs: [job("2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm", "Backend Engineer (LLM)"), job("2026-01-13-ml-engineer", "ML Engineer", "Interview", "2026-01-14T09:00:00.000Z"), job("2026-01-10-data-engineer", "Data Engineer", "Applying", "2026-01-11T09:00:00.000Z")],
};
const RECRUITING: CompanyInfo = { slug: "acme-recruiting", name: "Acme Recruiting", aliases: ["AR Talent"], website: "", recruiter_email: "", email_match: [], jobs: [] };
const FREE: CompanyInfo = { slug: "free-co", name: "Free Co", aliases: [], website: "https://gmail.com", recruiter_email: "", email_match: [], jobs: [] };
const ALL = [ACME, LABS, RECRUITING, FREE];
const h = (from: string, subject = "Hello", over: Partial<HeaderInfo> = {}): HeaderInfo => ({ direction: "in", from: { name: "", address: from }, to: [{ name: "", address: ALEX }], cc: [], subject, ...over });

describe("matchMessage", () => {
  it("matches the recruiter first, even when the subject names another company", () => {
    expect(matchMessage(h(TALENT, "Example Labs role"), ALL, ALEX)).toEqual({ company_slug: "acme-corp", job_id: "2026-01-12-ai-engineer", matched_by: "recruiter" });
  });
  it("matches addresses, then website domains with subdomains, then domains and words in email_match", () => {
    expect(matchMessage(h(addr("hiring", "partners.example.com")), ALL, ALEX)?.matched_by).toBe("email_match");
    expect(matchMessage(h(addr("people", "careers.acme.example.com")), ALL, ALEX)).toMatchObject({ company_slug: "acme-corp", matched_by: "domain" });
    expect(matchMessage(h(addr("people", "notacme.example.com")), ALL, ALEX)).toBeNull();
    expect(matchMessage(h(addr("x", "eu.labs-talent.example.com")), ALL, ALEX)).toMatchObject({ company_slug: "example-labs", matched_by: "email_match" });
    expect(matchMessage(h(addr("x", "elsewhere.example.org"), "Your LLM work"), ALL, ALEX)).toMatchObject({ company_slug: "example-labs", matched_by: "email_match" });
  });
  it("never matches on a free-mail or job-board domain, or on a display name", () => {
    expect(matchMessage(h(addr("someone", "gmail.com")), ALL, ALEX)).toBeNull();
    expect(matchMessage({ ...h(addr("x", "elsewhere.example.org")), from: { name: "Acme Corp Talent", address: addr("x", "elsewhere.example.org") } }, ALL, ALEX)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("matches the company name in the subject as whole words, preferring the longest name", () => {
    expect(matchMessage(h(addr("x", "elsewhere.example.org"), "Your Acme Corp application"), ALL, ALEX)).toMatchObject({ company_slug: "acme-corp", matched_by: "subject" });
    expect(matchMessage(h(addr("x", "elsewhere.example.org"), "Acme Recruiting update"), ALL, ALEX)?.company_slug).toBe("acme-recruiting");
    expect(matchMessage(h(addr("x", "elsewhere.example.org"), "News from AR Talent"), ALL, ALEX)?.company_slug).toBe("acme-recruiting");
    expect(matchMessage(h(addr("x", "elsewhere.example.org"), "Acmeville newsletter"), ALL, ALEX)).toBeNull();
    expect(matchMessage(h(addr("x", "elsewhere.example.org"), "Example Labs: next steps"), ALL, ALEX)?.company_slug).toBe("example-labs");
  });
  it("matches sent mail on its recipients", () => {
    const out = h(ALEX, "Re: Interview", { direction: "out", to: [{ name: "", address: TALENT }] });
    expect(counterparts(out, ALEX)).toEqual([{ name: "", address: TALENT }]);
    expect(matchMessage(out, ALL, ALEX)?.company_slug).toBe("acme-corp");
  });
});

describe("pickJob", () => {
  it("uses the only job, then title words, then the most recent active job, else the company", () => {
    expect(pickJob(ACME, "anything")).toBe("2026-01-12-ai-engineer");
    expect(pickJob(LABS, "Backend Engineer LLM interview")).toBe("2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm");
    expect(pickJob(LABS, "Data Engineer: next steps")).toBe("2026-01-10-data-engineer");
    expect(pickJob(LABS, "Quick question")).toBe("2026-01-13-ml-engineer");
    expect(pickJob({ ...LABS, jobs: LABS.jobs.map((j) => ({ ...j, status: "Found" })) }, "Quick question")).toBe("");
    expect(pickJob(RECRUITING, "x")).toBe("");
  });
});

describe("job-related rule and helpers", () => {
  it("knows job boards, ATS senders and job words, and leaves personal mail alone", () => {
    expect(isJobRelated(h(addr("noreply", "s.seek.com.au"), "New jobs for you"))).toBe(true);
    expect(isJobRelated(h(addr("no-reply", "greenhouse.io"), "Thanks"))).toBe(true);
    expect(isJobRelated(h(addr("x", "elsewhere.example.org"), "Your application to Contoso"))).toBe(true);
    expect(isJobRelated(h(addr("x", "elsewhere.example.org"), "Interviews this week"))).toBe(true);
    expect(isJobRelated(h(addr("mum", "example.net"), "Dinner on Sunday?"))).toBe(false);
    expect(isJobRelated(h(addr("x", "elsewhere.example.org"), "Controller update: control rolex"))).toBe(false);
  });
  it("suggests a company for mail to sort", () => {
    expect(suggestCompany(h(addr("people", "examplelabs-mail.example.org"), "Hi"), ALL)).toEqual({ company_slug: "example-labs", company_name: "Example Labs Pty Ltd", reason: "The sender's domain looks like Example Labs Pty Ltd." });
    expect(suggestCompany(h(addr("x", "elsewhere.example.org"), "Hi"), ALL)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("handles domains, websites, names and the ignore list", () => {
    expect(websiteDomain("https://www.acme.example.com/careers")).toBe("acme.example.com");
    expect(websiteDomain("not a site")).toBe("");
    expect(domainMatches("eu.jobs.example.com", "jobs.example.com")).toBe(true);
    expect(domainMatches("badjobs.example.com", "jobs.example.com")).toBe(false);
    expect(coreName("Example Labs Pty Ltd")).toBe("Example Labs");
    expect(coreName("Acme Corp")).toBe("Acme");
    const ignore = { senders: [addr("news", "example.net")], domains: ["spam.example.com"] };
    expect(isIgnored(addr("NEWS", "example.net"), ignore)).toBe(true);
    expect(isIgnored(addr("a", "eu.spam.example.com"), ignore)).toBe(true);
    expect(isIgnored(TALENT, ignore)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/mail/src/match.ts`**

```ts
import type { EmailAddress, EmailMatchKind } from "@careeros/core";
import senders from "../data/job-senders.json" with { type: "json" };

export interface JobInfo { id: string; title: string; status: string; last_activity: string }
export interface CompanyInfo { slug: string; name: string; aliases: string[]; website: string; recruiter_email: string; email_match: string[]; jobs: JobInfo[] }
export interface HeaderInfo { direction: "in" | "out"; from: EmailAddress; to: EmailAddress[]; cc: EmailAddress[]; subject: string }
export interface MatchResult { company_slug: string; job_id: string; matched_by: EmailMatchKind }
export interface JobSenders { domains: string[]; words: string[] }
export interface IgnoreList { senders: string[]; domains: string[] }

export const JOB_SENDERS: JobSenders = senders as JobSenders;
const FREEMAIL = new Set(["gmail.com", "googlemail.com", "outlook.com", "hotmail.com", "live.com", "msn.com", "yahoo.com", "icloud.com", "me.com", "mac.com", "proton.me", "protonmail.com", "aol.com", "gmx.com", "fastmail.com", "bigpond.com"]);
const STOP = new Set(["and", "the", "for", "with", "of", "in", "at", "to", "a", "an"]);
const ACTIVE = new Set(["Applying", "Applied", "Interview"]);

const lc = (s: string) => s.trim().toLowerCase();
export const domainOf = (address: string) => lc(address.slice(address.lastIndexOf("@") + 1));
export const domainMatches = (domain: string, base: string) => !!base && (lc(domain) === lc(base) || lc(domain).endsWith(`.${lc(base)}`));

export function websiteDomain(website: string): string {
  const w = website.trim();
  if (!w) return "";
  try {
    const host = new URL(/^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:\/\//i.test(w) ? w : `https://${w}`).hostname.toLowerCase();
    return host.includes(".") ? host.replace(/^www\./, "") : "";
  } catch { return ""; }
}

const LEGAL = /\s*(?:,\s*)?\b(?:pty\.?\s*ltd\.?|pty|ltd\.?|limited|inc\.?|corp\.?|corporation|llc|plc|gmbh|group|australia)\s*$/i;
export function coreName(name: string): string {
  let out = name.trim();
  for (let prev = ""; prev !== out;) { prev = out; out = out.replace(LEGAL, "").trim(); }
  return out || name.trim();
}

const escapeRe = (s: string) => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
const wholeWord = (needle: string, hay: string) =>
  needle.trim().length >= 3 && new RegExp(`(?<![\\p{L}\\p{N}])${escapeRe(needle.trim())}(?![\\p{L}\\p{N}])`, "iu").test(hay);

const isBoard = (domain: string) => JOB_SENDERS.domains.some((d) => domainMatches(domain, d));

export function counterparts(h: HeaderInfo, own = ""): EmailAddress[] {
  const list = h.direction === "in" ? [h.from] : [...h.to, ...h.cc];
  return list.filter((a) => !own || lc(a.address) !== lc(own));
}

export function matchCompany(h: HeaderInfo, companies: CompanyInfo[], own = ""): { company: CompanyInfo; matched_by: EmailMatchKind } | null {
  const people = counterparts(h, own).map((a) => lc(a.address));
  const domains = people.map(domainOf);
  const rules: [EmailMatchKind, (c: CompanyInfo) => boolean][] = [
    ["recruiter", (c) => !!c.recruiter_email && people.includes(lc(c.recruiter_email))],
    ["email_match", (c) => c.email_match.some((m) => m.includes("@") && people.includes(lc(m)))],
    ["domain", (c) => {
      const d = websiteDomain(c.website);
      return !!d && !FREEMAIL.has(d) && !isBoard(d) && domains.some((x) => domainMatches(x, d));
    }],
    ["email_match", (c) => c.email_match.some((m) => !m.includes("@") && (m.includes(".") ? domains.some((x) => domainMatches(x, m)) : wholeWord(m, h.subject)))],
  ];
  for (const [kind, test] of rules) {
    const hit = companies.find(test);
    if (hit) return { company: hit, matched_by: kind };
  }
  const named = companies
    .map((c) => ({ c, len: Math.max(...[coreName(c.name), ...c.aliases].filter((n) => wholeWord(n, h.subject)).map((n) => n.length), -1) }))
    .filter((x) => x.len > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => b.len - a.len);
  return named[0] ? { company: named[0].c, matched_by: "subject" } : null;
}

const titleWords = (title: string) => [...new Set(title.toLowerCase().split(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]+/u).filter((w) => w.length >= 2 && !STOP.has(w)))];

export function pickJob(c: CompanyInfo, subject: string): string {
  if (c.jobs.length === 1) return c.jobs[0]!.id;
  const byTitle = c.jobs
    .map((j) => ({ j, words: titleWords(j.title) }))
    .filter((x) => x.words.length && x.words.every((w) => new RegExp(`(?<![\\p{L}\\p{N}])${escapeRe(w)}(?![\\p{L}\\p{N}])`, "iu").test(subject)))
    .sort((a, b) => b.words.length - a.words.length || b.j.last_activity.localeCompare(a.j.last_activity));
  if (byTitle[0]) return byTitle[0].j.id;
  const active = c.jobs.filter((j) => ACTIVE.has(j.status)).sort((a, b) => b.last_activity.localeCompare(a.last_activity));
  return active[0]?.id ?? "";
}

export function matchMessage(h: HeaderInfo, companies: CompanyInfo[], own = ""): MatchResult | null {
  const m = matchCompany(h, companies, own);
  return m ? { company_slug: m.company.slug, job_id: pickJob(m.company, h.subject), matched_by: m.matched_by } : null;
}

export function isJobRelated(h: HeaderInfo, list: JobSenders = JOB_SENDERS): boolean {
  if (counterparts(h).some((a) => list.domains.some((d) => domainMatches(domainOf(a.address), d)))) return true;
  return list.words.some((w) => new RegExp(`(?<![\\p{L}\\p{N}])${escapeRe(w)}s?(?![\\p{L}\\p{N}])`, "iu").test(h.subject));
}

export function suggestCompany(h: HeaderInfo, companies: CompanyInfo[]): { company_slug: string; company_name: string; reason: string } | null {
  const domain = domainOf(h.from.address).replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");
  for (const c of companies) {
    const squashed = coreName(c.name).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");
    if (squashed.length >= 4 && domain.includes(squashed)) return { company_slug: c.slug, company_name: c.name, reason: `The sender's domain looks like ${c.name}.` };
  }
  for (const c of companies) {
    if (wholeWord(coreName(c.name), `${h.subject} ${h.from.name}`)) return { company_slug: c.slug, company_name: c.name, reason: `The email mentions ${c.name}.` };
  }
  return null;
}

export function isIgnored(address: string, ignore: IgnoreList): boolean {
  const a = lc(address);
  return ignore.senders.some((s) => lc(s) === a) || ignore.domains.some((d) => domainMatches(domainOf(a), d));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/mail/src/mime.ts`**

```ts
import type { CanonicalMessage } from "@careeros/core";
import MailComposer from "nodemailer/lib/mail-composer";

/**
 * Builds the exact message SMTP sends. The boundary comes from the draft id and the Message-ID and Date were
 * stamped when the email was proposed, so the bytes depend only on the inputs core hashes for the approval.
 */
export async function buildMime(m: CanonicalMessage, contents: Buffer[]): Promise<Buffer> {
  const options = {
    from: { name: m.from.name, address: m.from.address },
    to: m.to.map((a) => ({ name: a.name, address: a.address })),
    cc: m.cc.length ? m.cc.map((a) => ({ name: a.name, address: a.address })) : undefined,
    subject: m.subject,
    text: m.text,
    messageId: m.message_id,
    date: new Date(m.date),
    inReplyTo: m.in_reply_to || undefined,
    references: m.references.length ? m.references : undefined,
    attachments: m.attachments.map((a, i) => ({ filename: a.name, content: contents[i], contentType: a.type })),
    baseBoundary: `careeros-${m.draft_id}`,
    textEncoding: "quoted-printable",
    newline: "\r\n",
    disableFileAccess: true,
    disableUrlAccess: true,
  };
  // MailComposer's published option type lags its runtime options (baseBoundary, newline); the object above is the contract.
  return new MailComposer(options as never).compile().build();
}

export function envelopeFor(m: CanonicalMessage): { from: string; to: string[] } {
  return { from: m.from.address, to: [...m.to, ...m.cc].map((a) => a.address) };
}
```

### Task 9: `MailProvider`, the Gmail provider, a fake provider, and remote image fetching

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/mail/src/provider.ts`, `packages/mail/src/fake.ts`, `packages/mail/src/images.ts`, `packages/mail/test/provider.test.ts`, `packages/mail/test/fake.test.ts`, `packages/mail/test/images.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/mail/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `imapflow`, `nodemailer` (Task 4); `parseMessage` (Task 6); `redact` (Task 4); `EmailAddress` (core).
- Produces:
  - `GMAIL = { imap: { host: "imap.gmail.com", port: 993 }, smtp: { host: "smtp.gmail.com", port: 465 } }`, `GMAIL_HOSTS: string[]`
  - `interface MailAccount { address: string; password: string; displayName: string }`
  - `interface MailboxInfo { path: string; uidValidity: string; uidNext: number; exists: number }`
  - `interface HeaderSummary { uid: number; size: number; internalDate: string; messageId: string; from: EmailAddress[]; to: EmailAddress[]; cc: EmailAddress[]; subject: string }`
  - `interface MailSession { open(which: "inbox" | "sent"): Promise<MailboxInfo>; headers(range: { sinceUid: number } | { since: Date }): Promise<HeaderSummary[]>; body(uid: number): Promise<Buffer>; close(): Promise<void> }` (read-only by construction: there is no way to change flags, move or delete)
  - `interface ProviderCheck { id: "imap" | "sent" | "smtp"; ok: boolean; message: string; fix: string }`
  - `interface MailProvider { connect(): Promise<MailSession>; test(): Promise<ProviderCheck[]>; send(envelope: { from: string; to: string[] }, raw: Buffer): Promise<{ accepted: string[] }> }`
  - `type MailErrorKind = "auth" | "app-password-needed" | "imap-off" | "no-sent" | "network" | "tls" | "host" | "other"`, `class MailError extends Error { kind; fix }`, `explainMailError(e: unknown): MailError`
  - `interface ImapClient`, `interface SmtpClient`, `interface GmailDeps { imap?; smtp?; allowedHosts? }`, `class GmailProvider implements MailProvider` with `imapOptions()` and `smtpOptions()`
  - `interface FakeMessage { uid: number; raw: Buffer; internalDate?: string }`, `class FakeMailProvider implements MailProvider` (fields `inbox`, `sentBox` (`{ uidValidity: string; messages: FakeMessage[] }`), `calls: string[]`, `sent: { envelope; raw }[]`, `failWith: MailErrorKind | null`, `failBodyAt: number | null`; methods `add(box, raw, internalDate?)`), `fakeProviderFromFile(file: string, onSend?: (raw: Buffer) => void): FakeMailProvider`
  - `IMAGE_TYPES`, `MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 5 MB`, `type Fetcher`, `fetchRemoteImage(url, fetcher, opts?): Promise<{ type: string; body: Buffer }>`

Rulings: implicit TLS only (`secure: true`, ports 993 and 465), `rejectUnauthorized: true`, TLS 1.2 minimum, `servername` set; imapflow's logger is off (it would log addresses); a host outside `allowedHosts` is refused before any socket opens. IMAP `UID N:*` returns the last message even when its UID is below N, so headers below the asked UID are dropped. A failing check names the fix in plain words. Accounts where Google blocks App Passwords (work, school or Advanced Protection) fail with "auth"; the fix text says email can't be connected for that account yet (open question 1, resolved: no fallback in 3b).

`packages/mail/test/provider.test.ts`:

```ts
import nodemailer from "nodemailer";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { explainMailError, GmailProvider, MailError, type ImapClient, type SmtpClient } from "../src/provider.js";
import { ALEX, rawMessage } from "../src/testing.js";

const ACCOUNT = { address: ALEX, password: "abcd" + "efgh" + "ijkl" + "mnop", displayName: "Alex Example" };
type Msg = { uid: number; raw: Buffer; date: string };

function fakeImap(opts: { messages?: Msg[]; sentPath?: string | null; connectError?: unknown; log?: string[] } = {}): ImapClient {
  const log = opts.log ?? [];
  const msgs = opts.messages ?? [];
  const client: ImapClient = {
    mailbox: false,
    connect: async () => { log.push("connect"); if (opts.connectError) throw opts.connectError; },
    logout: async () => { log.push("logout"); },
    close: () => { log.push("close"); },
    list: async () => [{ path: "INBOX", specialUse: "\\Inbox" }, ...(opts.sentPath === null ? [] : [{ path: opts.sentPath ?? "[Gmail]/Sent Mail", specialUse: "\\Sent" }])],
    getMailboxLock: async (path, o) => {
      log.push(`lock ${path} readOnly=${o.readOnly}`);
      client.mailbox = { path, uidValidity: 7n, uidNext: 100, exists: msgs.length };
      return { release: () => log.push(`release ${path}`) };
    },
    search: async (q) => { log.push(`search since ${q.since?.toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`); return msgs.map((m) => m.uid); },
    fetch: async function* (range) {
      log.push(`fetch ${range}`);
      // Real servers answer "N:*" with the last message even when its UID is below N.
      for (const m of msgs) yield { uid: m.uid, size: m.raw.length, internalDate: new Date(m.date), envelope: { subject: `Subject ${m.uid}`, messageId: `<id${m.uid}${"@"}example.org>`, from: [{ name: "Acme Talent", address: ALEX }], to: [], cc: [] } };
    },
    fetchOne: async (uid, q) => { log.push(`fetchOne ${uid} ${Object.keys(q).join(",")}`); return { source: msgs.find((m) => String(m.uid) === uid)?.raw }; },
    on: () => client,
  };
  return client;
}
const okSmtp = (log: string[] = []): SmtpClient => ({ verify: async () => { log.push("verify"); return true; }, sendMail: async () => ({ accepted: [] }), close: () => { log.push("smtp close"); } });

describe("GmailProvider options", () => {
  it("uses implicit TLS with certificate checks on the two Gmail hosts only", () => {
    const p = new GmailProvider(ACCOUNT);
    expect(p.imapOptions()).toMatchObject({ host: "imap.gmail.com", port: 993, secure: true, logger: false, auth: { user: ALEX, pass: ACCOUNT.password }, tls: { rejectUnauthorized: true, minVersion: "TLSv1.2", servername: "imap.gmail.com" } });
    expect(p.smtpOptions()).toMatchObject({ host: "smtp.gmail.com", port: 465, secure: true, logger: false, disableFileAccess: true, disableUrlAccess: true, tls: { rejectUnauthorized: true, minVersion: "TLSv1.2" } });
  });
  it("refuses a host outside the allowed list before connecting", async () => {
    const log: string[] = [];
    const p = new GmailProvider(ACCOUNT, { imap: () => fakeImap({ log }), allowedHosts: ["smtp.gmail.com"] });
    await expect(p.connect()).rejects.toThrow(/may only connect to smtp\.gmail\.com/);
    expect(log).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("GmailProvider sessions", () => {
  const msgs: Msg[] = [3, 5, 6].map((uid) => ({ uid, raw: rawMessage({ id: `m${uid}` }), date: "2026-01-14T09:00:00.000Z" }));
  it("opens mailboxes read-only, finds Sent by its flag, and peeks bodies", async () => {
    const log: string[] = [];
    const s = await new GmailProvider(ACCOUNT, { imap: () => fakeImap({ messages: msgs, sentPath: "[Gmail]/Gesendet", log }) }).connect();
    expect(await s.open("inbox")).toEqual({ path: "INBOX", uidValidity: "7", uidNext: 100, exists: 3 });
    expect((await s.open("sent")).path).toBe("[Gmail]/Gesendet");
    expect((await s.body(5)).equals(msgs[1]!.raw)).toBe(true);
    await s.close();
    expect(log).toContain("lock INBOX readOnly=true");
    expect(log).toContain("lock [Gmail]/Gesendet readOnly=true");
    expect(log).toContain("fetchOne 5 source");
    expect(log.at(-1)).toBe("logout");
  });
  it("drops UIDs below the high-water mark that the server returns anyway", async () => {
    const s = await new GmailProvider(ACCOUNT, { imap: () => fakeImap({ messages: msgs }) }).connect();
    await s.open("inbox");
    expect((await s.headers({ sinceUid: 5 })).map((x) => x.uid)).toEqual([5, 6]);
    expect((await s.headers({ sinceUid: 7 })).map((x) => x.uid)).toEqual([]);
    const all = await s.headers({ since: new Date("2025-12-16T00:00:00.000Z") });
    expect(all.map((x) => x.uid)).toEqual([3, 5, 6]);
    expect(all[0]).toMatchObject({ messageId: "<id3" + "@" + "example.org>", subject: "Subject 3", internalDate: "2026-01-14T09:00:00.000Z", from: [{ name: "Acme Talent", address: ALEX }] });
  });
});

describe("GmailProvider test connection", () => {
  it("passes all three checks without sending anything", async () => {
    const log: string[] = [];
    const checks = await new GmailProvider(ACCOUNT, { imap: () => fakeImap({ log }), smtp: () => okSmtp(log) }).test();
    expect(checks.map((c) => [c.id, c.ok])).toEqual([["imap", true], ["sent", true], ["smtp", true]]);
    expect(checks[2]!.message).toContain("Nothing was sent");
    expect(log).toEqual(["connect", "logout", "verify", "smtp close"]);
  });
  it("explains each failure with its fix", async () => {
    const auth = Object.assign(new Error("Command failed"), { authenticationFailed: true, responseText: "[AUTHENTICATIONFAILED] Invalid credentials (Failure)" });
    const smtpAuth = Object.assign(new Error("Invalid login: 535-5.7.8 Username and Password not accepted"), { code: "EAUTH" });
    const checks = await new GmailProvider(ACCOUNT, { imap: () => fakeImap({ connectError: auth }), smtp: () => ({ ...okSmtp(), verify: async () => { throw smtpAuth; } }) }).test();
    expect(checks.map((c) => [c.id, c.ok])).toEqual([["imap", false], ["sent", false], ["smtp", false]]);
    expect(checks[0]!.message).toBe("Gmail didn't accept the address and app password.");
    expect(checks[0]!.fix).toMatch(/16 letters/);
    expect(checks[1]!.message).toMatch(/Not checked/);
    const noSent = await new GmailProvider(ACCOUNT, { imap: () => fakeImap({ sentPath: null }), smtp: () => okSmtp() }).test();
    expect(noSent[1]).toMatchObject({ id: "sent", ok: false, message: "Gmail's Sent folder wasn't found." });
  });
});

describe("explainMailError", () => {
  it("turns library errors into plain words", () => {
    expect(explainMailError(Object.assign(new Error("x"), { responseText: "[ALERT] Application-specific password required" })).kind).toBe("app-password-needed");
    expect(explainMailError(Object.assign(new Error("x"), { responseText: "[ALERT] Your account is not enabled for IMAP use." })).kind).toBe("imap-off");
    expect(explainMailError(Object.assign(new Error("getaddrinfo ENOTFOUND imap.gmail.com"), { code: "ENOTFOUND" })).kind).toBe("network");
    expect(explainMailError(Object.assign(new Error("self-signed certificate in certificate chain"), { code: "SELF_SIGNED_CERT_IN_CHAIN" })).kind).toBe("tls");
    const other = explainMailError(new Error(`Weird ${ALEX} reply`));
    expect(other.kind).toBe("other");
    expect(other.message).not.toContain(ALEX);
    expect(explainMailError(new MailError("no-sent", "x")).kind).toBe("no-sent");
  });
});

describe("sending", () => {
  it("passes the exact bytes to SMTP (captured by nodemailer's stream transport)", async () => {
    const raw = rawMessage({ from: ALEX, id: "s1" });
    let captured: Buffer | null = null;
    const smtp = (): SmtpClient => {
      const t = nodemailer.createTransport({ streamTransport: true, buffer: true });
      return { verify: async () => true, close: () => t.close(), sendMail: async (m) => { const info = await t.sendMail(m); captured = info.message as Buffer; return info as never; } };
    };
    const r = await new GmailProvider(ACCOUNT, { smtp }).send({ from: ALEX, to: ["talent" + "@" + "example.org"] }, raw);
    expect(captured!.equals(raw)).toBe(true);
    expect(r.accepted).toEqual(["talent" + "@" + "example.org"]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { FakeMailProvider, fakeProviderFromFile } from "../src/fake.js";
import { rawMessage } from "../src/testing.js";

describe("FakeMailProvider", () => {
  it("serves headers above a UID or since a date, and bodies, and records calls", async () => {
    const f = new FakeMailProvider();
    f.add("inbox", rawMessage({ id: "a" }), "2026-01-10T09:00:00.000Z");
    f.add("inbox", rawMessage({ id: "b", subject: "Second" }), "2026-01-14T09:00:00.000Z");
    const s = await f.connect();
    expect(await s.open("inbox")).toMatchObject({ path: "INBOX", uidValidity: "1", uidNext: 3, exists: 2 });
    expect((await s.headers({ sinceUid: 2 })).map((h) => h.subject)).toEqual(["Second"]);
    expect((await s.headers({ since: new Date("2026-01-12T00:00:00.000Z") })).map((h) => h.uid)).toEqual([2]);
    expect((await s.body(1)).toString()).toContain("Message-ID: <a");
    await s.close();
    expect(f.calls).toEqual(["connect", "open inbox", "headers inbox from uid 2", "headers inbox since 2026-01-12", "body inbox 1", "close"]);
  });
  it("leaves the message id empty when the header is missing, like an IMAP envelope", async () => {
    const f = new FakeMailProvider();
    f.add("inbox", rawMessage({ id: null }));
    const s = await f.connect();
    await s.open("inbox");
    expect((await s.headers({ sinceUid: 1 }))[0]?.messageId).toBe("");
  });
  it("fails the way the real provider does when told to", async () => {
    const f = new FakeMailProvider();
    f.failWith = "auth";
    await expect(f.connect()).rejects.toMatchObject({ kind: "auth" });
    expect((await f.test())[0]).toMatchObject({ id: "imap", ok: false });
  });
  it("loads an end-to-end fixture file and reports sends", async () => {
    const file = path.join(fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "careeros-fake-")), "mail.json");
    fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify({ inbox: [{ raw: rawMessage({ id: "e1" }).toString("utf8"), internalDate: "2026-01-14T09:00:00.000Z" }], sent: [] }));
    const seen: Buffer[] = [];
    const f = fakeProviderFromFile(file, (raw) => seen.push(raw));
    expect(f.inbox.messages).toHaveLength(1);
    await f.send({ from: "a", to: ["b"] }, Buffer.from("x"));
    expect(seen.map(String)).toEqual(["x"]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { fetchRemoteImage, MAX_IMAGE_BYTES, type Fetcher } from "../src/images.js";

const PNG = Buffer.from("iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAQAAAC1HAwCAAAAC0lEQVR42mNkYAAAAAYAAjCB0C8AAAAASUVORK5CYII=", "base64");
const fetcher = (routes: Record<string, () => Response>): Fetcher & { seen: { url: string; init: unknown }[] } => {
  const seen: { url: string; init: unknown }[] = [];
  const f = (async (url: string, init: unknown) => { seen.push({ url, init }); const r = routes[url]; if (!r) throw new Error("no route"); return r(); }) as Fetcher & { seen: typeof seen };
  f.seen = seen;
  return f;
};

describe("fetchRemoteImage", () => {
  it("fetches an https image without cookies or referrer", async () => {
    const f = fetcher({ "https://images.example.com/logo.png": () => new Response(PNG, { headers: { "content-type": "image/png" } }) });
    const r = await fetchRemoteImage("https://images.example.com/logo.png", f);
    expect(r).toEqual({ type: "image/png", body: PNG });
    expect(f.seen[0]!.init).toMatchObject({ redirect: "manual", credentials: "omit", referrerPolicy: "no-referrer" });
  });
  it("follows https redirects only", async () => {
    const f = fetcher({
      "https://t.example.com/a": () => new Response(null, { status: 302, headers: { location: "https://images.example.com/logo.png" } }),
      "https://images.example.com/logo.png": () => new Response(PNG, { headers: { "content-type": "image/png" } }),
      "https://t.example.com/b": () => new Response(null, { status: 302, headers: { location: "http://images.example.com/x.png" } }),
    });
    expect((await fetchRemoteImage("https://t.example.com/a", f)).type).toBe("image/png");
    await expect(fetchRemoteImage("https://t.example.com/b", f)).rejects.toThrow(/https/);
  });
  it("refuses http, SVG, non-images and anything over 5 MB", async () => {
    const f = fetcher({
      "https://images.example.com/x.svg": () => new Response("<svg/>", { headers: { "content-type": "image/svg+xml" } }),
      "https://images.example.com/page": () => new Response("<html>", { headers: { "content-type": "text/html" } }),
      "https://images.example.com/big.png": () => new Response(Buffer.alloc(MAX_IMAGE_BYTES + 1), { headers: { "content-type": "image/png" } }),
    });
    await expect(fetchRemoteImage("http://images.example.com/x.png", f)).rejects.toThrow(/https/);
    await expect(fetchRemoteImage("https://images.example.com/x.svg", f)).rejects.toThrow(/isn't an image/);
    await expect(fetchRemoteImage("https://images.example.com/page", f)).rejects.toThrow(/isn't an image/);
    await expect(fetchRemoteImage("https://images.example.com/big.png", f)).rejects.toThrow(/larger than 5 MB/);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/mail/src/provider.ts`**

```ts
import { ImapFlow } from "imapflow";
import nodemailer from "nodemailer";
import type { EmailAddress } from "@careeros/core";
import { redact } from "./redact.js";

export const GMAIL = { imap: { host: "imap.gmail.com", port: 993 }, smtp: { host: "smtp.gmail.com", port: 465 } } as const;
export const GMAIL_HOSTS: string[] = [GMAIL.imap.host, GMAIL.smtp.host];

export interface MailAccount { address: string; password: string; displayName: string }
export interface MailboxInfo { path: string; uidValidity: string; uidNext: number; exists: number }
export interface HeaderSummary { uid: number; size: number; internalDate: string; messageId: string; from: EmailAddress[]; to: EmailAddress[]; cc: EmailAddress[]; subject: string }
export interface MailSession {
  open(which: "inbox" | "sent"): Promise<MailboxInfo>;
  headers(range: { sinceUid: number } | { since: Date }): Promise<HeaderSummary[]>;
  body(uid: number): Promise<Buffer>;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}
export interface ProviderCheck { id: "imap" | "sent" | "smtp"; ok: boolean; message: string; fix: string }
export interface MailProvider {
  connect(): Promise<MailSession>;
  test(): Promise<ProviderCheck[]>;
  send(envelope: { from: string; to: string[] }, raw: Buffer): Promise<{ accepted: string[] }>;
}

export type MailErrorKind = "auth" | "app-password-needed" | "imap-off" | "no-sent" | "network" | "tls" | "host" | "other";
export class MailError extends Error {
  constructor(readonly kind: MailErrorKind, message: string, readonly fix = "") { super(message); this.name = "MailError"; }
}

const TEXT: Record<Exclude<MailErrorKind, "other">, [string, string]> = {
  auth: ["Gmail didn't accept the address and app password.",
    "Check the address, then create a new app password and paste all 16 letters. If Google's App Passwords page says the setting isn't available for your account (some work, school or Advanced Protection accounts), email can't be connected yet. Keep using Gmail directly for now."],
  "app-password-needed": ["Gmail needs an app password, not your normal password.", "Create an app password named CareerOS on Google's App Passwords page and paste its 16 letters."],
  "imap-off": ["IMAP is turned off in Gmail.", "In Gmail, open Settings > See all settings > Forwarding and POP/IMAP and turn on IMAP."],
  "no-sent": ["Gmail's Sent folder wasn't found.", "In Gmail settings, open Labels and tick \"Show in IMAP\" for Sent Mail."],
  network: ["CareerOS couldn't reach Gmail.", "Check your internet connection and try again."],
  tls: ["The secure connection to Gmail couldn't be checked.", "Check the computer's date and time. Some office or school networks inspect secure traffic; try another network."],
  host: ["CareerOS only connects to Gmail's own servers.", ""],
};
const NETWORK_CODES = new Set(["ENOTFOUND", "ECONNREFUSED", "ETIMEDOUT", "ECONNRESET", "EAI_AGAIN", "EHOSTUNREACH", "ENETUNREACH", "ETIMEOUT", "ESOCKET", "ECONNECTION", "EPIPE", "NoConnection"]);

export function explainMailError(e: unknown): MailError {
  if (e instanceof MailError) return e;
  const o = (e ?? {}) as { message?: string; responseText?: string; response?: string; code?: string; authenticationFailed?: boolean };
  const text = `${o.message ?? String(e)} ${o.responseText ?? ""} ${o.response ?? ""}`;
  const code = String(o.code ?? "");
  const make = (k: Exclude<MailErrorKind, "other">) => new MailError(k, TEXT[k][0], TEXT[k][1]);
  if (/application-specific password required|5\.7\.9/i.test(text)) return make("app-password-needed");
  if (/not enabled for IMAP|IMAP access is disabled/i.test(text)) return make("imap-off");
  if (o.authenticationFailed || code === "EAUTH" || /AUTHENTICATIONFAILED|Invalid credentials|Username and Password not accepted|\b535\b/i.test(text)) return make("auth");
  if (/certificate|CERT_|ERR_TLS|self[- ]signed|unable to verify/i.test(`${text} ${code}`)) return make("tls");
  if (NETWORK_CODES.has(code) || /timed? ?out|socket hang up|connection closed/i.test(text)) return make("network");
  return new MailError("other", `Gmail returned an error: ${redact(text.trim().split("\n")[0] ?? "").slice(0, 200)}`, "Try again in a few minutes.");
}

interface Envelope { date?: Date; subject?: string; messageId?: string; from?: { name?: string; address?: string }[]; to?: { name?: string; address?: string }[]; cc?: { name?: string; address?: string }[] }
/** The part of imapflow's client CareerOS uses. It has no flag, move, copy, append or delete methods on purpose. */
export interface ImapClient {
  mailbox: false | { path: string; uidValidity: bigint; uidNext: number; exists: number };
  connect(): Promise<void>;
  logout(): Promise<void>;
  close(): void;
  list(): Promise<{ path: string; specialUse?: string }[]>;
  getMailboxLock(path: string, opts: { readOnly: boolean }): Promise<{ release(): void }>;
  search(q: { since?: Date }, opts: { uid: true }): Promise<number[] | false>;
  fetch(range: string, q: { uid: true; envelope: true; size: true; internalDate: true }, opts: { uid: true }): AsyncIterable<{ uid: number; size?: number; internalDate?: Date | string; envelope?: Envelope }>;
  fetchOne(uid: string, q: { source: true }, opts: { uid: true }): Promise<{ source?: Buffer } | false>;
  on(event: "error", fn: (e: Error) => void): unknown;
}
export interface SmtpClient {
  verify(): Promise<unknown>;
  sendMail(m: { envelope: { from: string; to: string[] }; raw: Buffer }): Promise<{ accepted?: unknown[] }>;
  close(): void;
}
export interface ImapOptions {
  host: string; port: number; secure: true; auth: { user: string; pass: string }; logger: false; emitLogs: false; disableAutoIdle: true;
  connectionTimeout: number; greetingTimeout: number; socketTimeout: number; tls: { rejectUnauthorized: true; minVersion: "TLSv1.2"; servername: string };
}
export interface SmtpOptions {
  host: string; port: number; secure: true; auth: { user: string; pass: string }; logger: false; debug: false;
  connectionTimeout: number; greetingTimeout: number; socketTimeout: number; disableFileAccess: true; disableUrlAccess: true;
  tls: { rejectUnauthorized: true; minVersion: "TLSv1.2"; servername: string };
}
export interface GmailDeps { imap?: (o: ImapOptions) => ImapClient; smtp?: (o: SmtpOptions) => SmtpClient; allowedHosts?: string[] }

const toAddr = (list: Envelope["from"]): EmailAddress[] =>
  (list ?? []).filter((a) => a.address).map((a) => ({ name: (a.name ?? "").replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]+/g, " ").trim(), address: a.address! }));

class ImapSession implements MailSession {
  private lock: { release(): void } | null = null;
  constructor(private c: ImapClient) {}

  async open(which: "inbox" | "sent"): Promise<MailboxInfo> {
    this.lock?.release();
    this.lock = null;
    let path = "INBOX";
    if (which === "sent") {
      const sent = (await this.c.list()).find((b) => b.specialUse === "\\Sent");
      if (!sent) throw new MailError("no-sent", TEXT["no-sent"][0], TEXT["no-sent"][1]);
      path = sent.path;
    }
    // readOnly: true makes imapflow send EXAMINE, so nothing in the mailbox can change.
    this.lock = await this.c.getMailboxLock(path, { readOnly: true });
    const mb = this.c.mailbox;
    if (!mb) throw new MailError("other", `Gmail didn't open ${path}.`);
    return { path, uidValidity: String(mb.uidValidity), uidNext: mb.uidNext, exists: mb.exists };
  }

  async headers(range: { sinceUid: number } | { since: Date }): Promise<HeaderSummary[]> {
    let uids: string;
    if ("sinceUid" in range) uids = `${range.sinceUid}:*`;
    else {
      const found = await this.c.search({ since: range.since }, { uid: true });
      if (!found || !found.length) return [];
      uids = found.join(",");
    }
    const min = "sinceUid" in range ? range.sinceUid : 0;
    const out: HeaderSummary[] = [];
    for await (const m of this.c.fetch(uids, { uid: true, envelope: true, size: true, internalDate: true }, { uid: true })) {
      if (m.uid < min) continue; // "N:*" returns the last message even when its UID is below N
      const d = m.internalDate ? new Date(m.internalDate) : m.envelope?.date;
      out.push({
        uid: m.uid, size: m.size ?? 0, internalDate: d && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? d.toISOString() : "",
        messageId: m.envelope?.messageId ?? "", subject: m.envelope?.subject ?? "",
        from: toAddr(m.envelope?.from), to: toAddr(m.envelope?.to), cc: toAddr(m.envelope?.cc),
      });
    }
    return out.sort((a, b) => a.uid - b.uid);
  }

  async body(uid: number): Promise<Buffer> {
    // imapflow fetches `source` with BODY.PEEK[], which never sets \Seen.
    const m = await this.c.fetchOne(String(uid), { source: true }, { uid: true });
    if (!m || !m.source) throw new MailError("other", `Message ${uid} is no longer in Gmail.`);
    return m.source;
  }

  async close(): Promise<void> {
    this.lock?.release();
    this.lock = null;
    try { await this.c.logout(); } catch { this.c.close(); }
  }
}

const ok = (id: ProviderCheck["id"], message: string): ProviderCheck => ({ id, ok: true, message, fix: "" });
const failed = (id: ProviderCheck["id"], e: MailError): ProviderCheck => ({ id, ok: false, message: e.message, fix: e.fix });

export class GmailProvider implements MailProvider {
  private allowed: string[];
  constructor(private account: MailAccount, private deps: GmailDeps = {}) {
    this.allowed = deps.allowedHosts ?? GMAIL_HOSTS;
  }

  private assertHost(host: string): void {
    if (!this.allowed.includes(host)) throw new MailError("host", `Mail may only connect to ${this.allowed.join(" and ")}.`);
  }

  imapOptions(): ImapOptions {
    const { host, port } = GMAIL.imap;
    return {
      host, port, secure: true, auth: { user: this.account.address, pass: this.account.password }, logger: false, emitLogs: false, disableAutoIdle: true,
      connectionTimeout: 15_000, greetingTimeout: 15_000, socketTimeout: 60_000, tls: { rejectUnauthorized: true, minVersion: "TLSv1.2", servername: host },
    };
  }

  smtpOptions(): SmtpOptions {
    const { host, port } = GMAIL.smtp;
    return {
      host, port, secure: true, auth: { user: this.account.address, pass: this.account.password }, logger: false, debug: false,
      connectionTimeout: 15_000, greetingTimeout: 15_000, socketTimeout: 60_000, disableFileAccess: true, disableUrlAccess: true,
      tls: { rejectUnauthorized: true, minVersion: "TLSv1.2", servername: host },
    };
  }

  private imap(): ImapClient {
    this.assertHost(GMAIL.imap.host);
    const c = (this.deps.imap ?? ((o) => new ImapFlow(o) as unknown as ImapClient))(this.imapOptions());
    c.on("error", () => undefined); // a dropped socket surfaces as a rejected call; never crash the process
    return c;
  }

  private smtp(): SmtpClient {
    this.assertHost(GMAIL.smtp.host);
    return (this.deps.smtp ?? ((o) => nodemailer.createTransport(o) as unknown as SmtpClient))(this.smtpOptions());
  }

  async connect(): Promise<MailSession> {
    const c = this.imap();
    try { await c.connect(); } catch (e) { throw explainMailError(e); }
    return new ImapSession(c);
  }

  async test(): Promise<ProviderCheck[]> {
    const checks: ProviderCheck[] = [];
    let c: ImapClient | null = null;
    try {
      c = this.imap();
      await c.connect();
      checks.push(ok("imap", "Signed in to Gmail."));
      const sent = (await c.list()).find((b) => b.specialUse === "\\Sent");
      checks.push(sent ? ok("sent", `Found your Sent folder (${sent.path}).`) : failed("sent", new MailError("no-sent", TEXT["no-sent"][0], TEXT["no-sent"][1])));
    } catch (e) {
      checks.push(failed("imap", explainMailError(e)));
      checks.push({ id: "sent", ok: false, message: "Not checked, because signing in failed.", fix: "" });
    } finally {
      if (c) { try { await c.logout(); } catch { c.close(); } }
    }
    try {
      const t = this.smtp();
      try { await t.verify(); } finally { t.close(); }
      checks.push(ok("smtp", "Gmail will accept mail you send from CareerOS. Nothing was sent."));
    } catch (e) {
      checks.push(failed("smtp", explainMailError(e)));
    }
    return checks;
  }

  async send(envelope: { from: string; to: string[] }, raw: Buffer): Promise<{ accepted: string[] }> {
    const t = this.smtp();
    try {
      const r = await t.sendMail({ envelope, raw });
      const accepted = (r.accepted ?? envelope.to).map(String);
      if (!accepted.length) throw new MailError("other", "Gmail refused every recipient.", "Check the addresses and try again.");
      return { accepted };
    } catch (e) {
      throw explainMailError(e);
    } finally {
      t.close();
    }
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/mail/src/fake.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import { parseMessage } from "./parse.js";
import { explainMailError, MailError, type HeaderSummary, type MailboxInfo, type MailErrorKind, type MailProvider, type MailSession, type ProviderCheck } from "./provider.js";

export interface FakeMessage { uid: number; raw: Buffer; internalDate?: string }
interface Box { uidValidity: string; messages: FakeMessage[] }

/** An in-memory Gmail for tests and end-to-end runs. Like the real session, it can only read. */
export class FakeMailProvider implements MailProvider {
  inbox: Box = { uidValidity: "1", messages: [] };
  sentBox: Box = { uidValidity: "1", messages: [] };
  readonly calls: string[] = [];
  readonly sent: { envelope: { from: string; to: string[] }; raw: Buffer }[] = [];
  failWith: MailErrorKind | null = null;
  /** A body fetch for this UID fails with a network error (a connection that drops mid-sync). */
  failBodyAt: number | null = null;

  constructor(private onSend?: (raw: Buffer) => void) {}

  add(box: "inbox" | "sent", raw: Buffer, internalDate = "2026-01-14T09:00:00.000Z"): FakeMessage {
    const b = box === "inbox" ? this.inbox : this.sentBox;
    const m = { uid: (b.messages.at(-1)?.uid ?? 0) + 1, raw, internalDate };
    b.messages.push(m);
    return m;
  }

  private fail(): void {
    if (this.failWith) throw explainMailError(this.failWith === "auth" ? Object.assign(new Error("x"), { authenticationFailed: true }) : this.failWith === "network" ? Object.assign(new Error("x"), { code: "ECONNRESET" }) : new MailError(this.failWith, `fake ${this.failWith}`));
  }

  async connect(): Promise<MailSession> {
    this.calls.push("connect");
    this.fail();
    let which: "inbox" | "sent" = "inbox";
    const box = () => (which === "inbox" ? this.inbox : this.sentBox);
    const session: MailSession = {
      open: async (w): Promise<MailboxInfo> => {
        which = w;
        this.calls.push(`open ${w}`);
        const b = box();
        return { path: w === "inbox" ? "INBOX" : "[Gmail]/Sent Mail", uidValidity: b.uidValidity, uidNext: (b.messages.at(-1)?.uid ?? 0) + 1, exists: b.messages.length };
      },
      headers: async (range): Promise<HeaderSummary[]> => {
        this.calls.push("sinceUid" in range ? `headers ${which} from uid ${range.sinceUid}` : `headers ${which} since ${range.since.toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`);
        const picked = box().messages.filter((m) => ("sinceUid" in range ? m.uid >= range.sinceUid : (m.internalDate ?? "") >= range.since.toISOString()));
        const out: HeaderSummary[] = [];
        for (const m of picked) {
          const p = await parseMessage(m.raw);
          out.push({ uid: m.uid, size: m.raw.length, internalDate: m.internalDate ?? "", messageId: p.synthetic_id ? "" : p.message_id, from: [p.from], to: p.to, cc: p.cc, subject: p.subject });
        }
        return out;
      },
      body: async (uid) => {
        this.calls.push(`body ${which} ${uid}`);
        if (this.failBodyAt === uid) throw explainMailError(Object.assign(new Error("socket hang up"), { code: "ECONNRESET" }));
        const m = box().messages.find((x) => x.uid === uid);
        if (!m) throw new MailError("other", `Message ${uid} is no longer in Gmail.`);
        return m.raw;
      },
      close: async () => { this.calls.push("close"); },
    };
    return session;
  }

  async test(): Promise<ProviderCheck[]> {
    this.calls.push("test");
    try { this.fail(); } catch (e) {
      const m = e as MailError;
      return [{ id: "imap", ok: false, message: m.message, fix: m.fix }, { id: "sent", ok: false, message: "Not checked, because signing in failed.", fix: "" }, { id: "smtp", ok: false, message: m.message, fix: m.fix }];
    }
    return [
      { id: "imap", ok: true, message: "Signed in to Gmail.", fix: "" },
      { id: "sent", ok: true, message: "Found your Sent folder ([Gmail]/Sent Mail).", fix: "" },
      { id: "smtp", ok: true, message: "Gmail will accept mail you send from CareerOS. Nothing was sent.", fix: "" },
    ];
  }

  async send(envelope: { from: string; to: string[] }, raw: Buffer): Promise<{ accepted: string[] }> {
    this.calls.push("send");
    this.fail();
    this.sent.push({ envelope, raw });
    this.onSend?.(raw);
    return { accepted: envelope.to };
  }
}

/** Reads `{ inbox: [{ raw, internalDate? }], sent: [...] }` (raw as UTF-8 text) for end-to-end runs. */
export function fakeProviderFromFile(file: string, onSend?: (raw: Buffer) => void): FakeMailProvider {
  const data = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8")) as { inbox?: { raw: string; internalDate?: string }[]; sent?: { raw: string; internalDate?: string }[] };
  const f = new FakeMailProvider(onSend);
  for (const m of data.inbox ?? []) f.add("inbox", Buffer.from(m.raw, "utf8"), m.internalDate);
  for (const m of data.sent ?? []) f.add("sent", Buffer.from(m.raw, "utf8"), m.internalDate);
  return f;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `packages/mail/src/images.ts`**

```ts
export const IMAGE_TYPES = ["image/png", "image/jpeg", "image/gif", "image/webp", "image/avif", "image/bmp"];
export const MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;

export interface FetchInit {
  redirect: "manual"; credentials: "omit"; referrerPolicy: "no-referrer"; cache: "no-store";
  headers: Record<string, string>; signal: AbortSignal;
}
export type Fetcher = (url: string, init: FetchInit) => Promise<Response>;

const https = (u: string): URL => {
  let url: URL;
  try { url = new URL(u); } catch { throw new Error("Images load over https only."); }
  if (url.protocol !== "https:" || url.username || url.password) throw new Error("Images load over https only.");
  return url;
};

/** Fetches one remote image for "Load images": https only (redirects too), no cookies or referrer, no SVG, at most 5 MB. */
export async function fetchRemoteImage(url: string, fetcher: Fetcher, opts: { maxRedirects?: number; timeoutMs?: number } = {}): Promise<{ type: string; body: Buffer }> {
  let current = https(url).href;
  const ctl = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => ctl.abort(), opts.timeoutMs ?? 15_000);
  try {
    for (let hop = 0; hop <= (opts.maxRedirects ?? 3); hop++) {
      const res = await fetcher(current, {
        redirect: "manual", credentials: "omit", referrerPolicy: "no-referrer", cache: "no-store",
        headers: { Accept: IMAGE_TYPES.join(",") }, signal: ctl.signal,
      });
      if (res.status >= 300 && res.status < 400) {
        const loc = res.headers.get("location");
        if (!loc) throw new Error("The image moved without saying where.");
        current = https(new URL(loc, current).href).href;
        continue;
      }
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`The image couldn't load (${res.status}).`);
      const type = (res.headers.get("content-type") ?? "").split(";")[0]!.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!IMAGE_TYPES.includes(type)) throw new Error("That address isn't an image CareerOS will show.");
      if (Number(res.headers.get("content-length") ?? 0) > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES) throw new Error("The image is larger than 5 MB.");
      const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
      let size = 0;
      const reader = res.body?.getReader();
      while (reader) {
        const { done, value } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        size += value.byteLength;
        if (size > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES) { await reader.cancel(); throw new Error("The image is larger than 5 MB."); }
        chunks.push(Buffer.from(value));
      }
      return { type, body: Buffer.concat(chunks) };
    }
    throw new Error("The image redirected too many times.");
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}
```

### Task 10: Storing messages (`.eml`, sidecar, untrusted `.md`), Mail to sort, attachments, moves and refs

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/mail/src/store.ts`, `packages/mail/test/store.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/mail/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: core `careerosDir`, `emailsDirFor`, `readEmails`, `listJobs`, `jobFilePath`, `layout`, `insideRoot`, `readRecord`, `writeRecord`, `updateRecord`, `writeFileAtomic`, `uniquePath`, `iso`, `Clock`, `EmailRecord`, `EmailMatchKind`; `ParsedMessage`, `normalizeSubject`, `syntheticMessageId` (Task 6); `HeaderSummary` (Task 9); `sanitizeEmailHtml` (Task 5).
- Produces:
  - `MAIL_REF: RegExp` (same pattern as the contract's `MAIL_REF_PATTERN` in Task 13)
  - `interface MailPaths { ref; json; eml; md; dir; base; company_slug; job_id; triage: boolean }`, `resolveMailRef(root, ref): MailPaths`
  - `asciiSlug(s, max): string`, `baseNameFor(date, subject, messageId): string` (at most 74 characters, so every file name stays within 80), `safeFileName(name): string` (at most 80), `isRisky(name, type): boolean`
  - `type StoreTarget = { company_slug: string; job_id: string } | "triage"`
  - `interface StoreInput { raw: Buffer | null; parsed: ParsedMessage | null; header: HeaderSummary; mailbox: string; direction: "in" | "out"; matched_by: EmailMatchKind; tooLarge: boolean }`
  - `storeMessage(root, target, input, c?): { ref: string; email: EmailRecord }` (writes `.eml`, then `.md`, then the `.json` sidecar last)
  - `UNTRUSTED_NOTE: string`, `renderMarkdown(email, text): string`, `markdownBody(md: string): string`
  - `readStored(root, ref): { paths: MailPaths; email: EmailRecord; raw: Buffer | null }`, `updateStored(root, ref, fn): EmailRecord`, `deleteStored(root, ref): void`
  - `moveMessage(root, ref, to: { company_slug: string; job_id: string }): string` (new ref; `matched_by` becomes `user`)
  - `saveAttachmentFile(root, ref, part, name, content, c?): string` (workspace-relative path of the saved copy)
  - `listTriage(root): { id: string; ref: string; email: EmailRecord }[]`, `purgeTriage(root, now, days?): number`
  - `pruneRaw(root, before: Date): { removed: number; bytes: number }`, `rawBytes(root): number`
  - `knownMessageIds(root): Map<string, string>` (Message-ID to ref, over jobs, companies and Mail to sort)

Layout: job mail in `companies/<slug>/jobs/<id>/emails/<base>.{eml,json,md}`, company mail in `companies/<slug>/emails/`, Mail to sort in `.careeros/mail-triage/`, saved attachments in the same `emails/attachments/` folder. Every folder is checked to be real (not a link) and inside the workspace before anything is written.

```ts
  });
  it("removes old raw copies but keeps the record and text", async () => {
    const { root, job } = ws();
    const s = await store(root, { company_slug: job.company_slug, job_id: job.id });
    const before = rawBytes(root);
    expect(before).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(pruneRaw(root, new Date("2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z"))).toEqual({ removed: 0, bytes: 0 });
    expect(pruneRaw(root, new Date("2026-02-01T00:00:00.000Z"))).toEqual({ removed: 1, bytes: before });
    expect(readStored(root, s.ref).raw).toBeNull();
    expect(fs.existsSync(resolveMailRef(root, s.ref).md)).toBe(true);
    expect(rawBytes(root)).toBe(0);
  });
  it("knows every stored Message-ID", async () => {
    const { root, job } = ws();
    const a = await store(root, { company_slug: job.company_slug, job_id: job.id }, rawMessage({ id: "k1" }));
    const b = await store(root, "triage", rawMessage({ id: "k2", from: addr("noreply", "seek.com.au") }));
    expect(knownMessageIds(root)).toEqual(new Map([[mid("k1"), a.ref], [mid("k2"), b.ref]]));
  });
});
```
