```ts
import { layout, refreshIndex, updateRecord } from "@careeros/core";
import { ALEX, parseMessage, storeMessage, type StoreTarget } from "@careeros/mail";
import type { ServiceContext } from "../src/context.js";

export const ACME = ["acme-corp", "2026-01-12-ai-engineer"] as const;
export const LABS = ["example-labs", "2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm"] as const;
export const GOOD_PASSWORD = "abcdefghijklmnop";

/** Connects the fictional account directly (the wizard path is tested in mail-account.test.ts). */
export function connectMail(ctx: ServiceContext, root: string): void {
  updateRecord("integrations", layout(root).integrations, (i) => { i.mail.address = ALEX; i.mail.display_name = "Alex Example"; i.mail.signature = "Alex Example\nAI Engineer"; });
  ctx.host.secrets.set("gmail_app_password", GOOD_PASSWORD);
}

export async function storeRaw(root: string, target: StoreTarget, raw: Buffer, o: { direction?: "in" | "out"; uid?: number } = {}): Promise<string> {
  const parsed = await parseMessage(raw);
  const s = storeMessage(root, target, {
    raw, parsed, mailbox: "INBOX", direction: o.direction ?? "in", matched_by: target === "triage" ? "none" : "recruiter", tooLarge: false,
    header: { uid: o.uid ?? 1, size: raw.length, internalDate: parsed.date ?? "2026-01-14T09:00:00.000Z", messageId: parsed.message_id, from: [parsed.from], to: parsed.to, cc: parsed.cc, subject: parsed.subject },
  });
  refreshIndex(root, [s.ref]);
  return s.ref;
}
```

```ts
  const board = await storeRaw(x.root, "triage", rawMessage({ id: "t2", from: SEEK, subject: "New jobs" }), { uid: 5 });

}

      expect((await mailAsset(ctx, u)).status, u).toBe(404);

});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/services/src/mail-read.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import {
  addEvent, buildDraftMessage, checkDraft, formatAddress, jobFilePath, layout, listApprovals, listDraftFiles, listJobs, mailAccount, messageText,
  mimeTypeFor, newJob, parseAddressList, parseDraft, proposeEmailSend, readDraft, readMeta, readRecord, updateRecord, writeDraft,
  type Clock, type Draft, type EmailRecord, type MessageStamp,
} from "@careeros/core";
import {
  companyIndex, deleteStored, domainMatches, domainOf, isIgnored, isRisky, linkWarning, listTriage, MAIL_FRAME_CSP, markdownBody, moveMessage,
  parseMessage, pruneRaw, readMailState, readStored, renderMailDocument, sanitizeEmailHtml, saveAttachmentFile, suggestCompany, textToHtml,
  updateStored, writeMailState, type MailPaths, type ParsedMessage,
} from "@careeros/mail";
import {
  mailRefToken, parseMailUrl, type ApprovalView, type DraftDetail, type DraftInput, type DraftStatus, type DraftView, type EmailPreview, type MailList,
  type MailMessageView, type MailNextUp, type MailQuery, type MailRow, type MailTarget, type PruneResult, type TriageItem,
} from "@careeros/contract";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import { mailHost } from "./mail-account.js";
import { approvalView, query, touch } from "./read.js";
import { isSafeLink } from "./secrets.js";

const TOO_LARGE = "This email is larger than 25 MB, so only its headers were saved. Open it in Gmail to read it.";

// ---- list ------------------------------------------------------------------
interface EmailIndexRow {
  file: string; company_slug: string; job_id: string; direction: "in" | "out"; date: string; subject: string; from_name: string; from_addr: string;
  thread_key: string; attachments: number; opened: number; matched_by: string; company_name: string; job_title: string;
}
const LIST_SQL = `SELECT e.file, e.company_slug, e.job_id, e.direction, e.date, e.subject, e.from_name, e.from_addr, e.thread_key, e.attachments,
  e.opened, e.matched_by, COALESCE(c.name, e.company_slug) AS company_name, COALESCE(j.title, '') AS job_title
  FROM emails e LEFT JOIN companies c ON c.slug = e.company_slug LEFT JOIN jobs j ON j.company_slug = e.company_slug AND j.id = e.job_id`;

export function allMailRows(root: string): MailRow[] {
  return query<EmailIndexRow>(root, LIST_SQL).map((r): MailRow => ({
    ref: r.file, company_slug: r.company_slug, company_name: r.company_name, job_id: r.job_id, job_title: r.job_title, direction: r.direction,
    date: r.date, subject: r.subject, from: { name: r.from_name, address: r.from_addr }, thread_key: r.thread_key, attachments: r.attachments,
    opened: r.opened === 1, matched_by: r.matched_by,
  })).sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date) || a.ref.localeCompare(b.ref));
}

export function mailList(root: string, q: MailQuery): MailList {
  const text = q.text?.trim().toLowerCase();
  const rows = allMailRows(root).filter((r) =>
    (!q.company_slug || r.company_slug === q.company_slug) &&
    (!q.id || r.job_id === q.id || (q.includeCompany === true && r.job_id === "")) &&
    (!q.direction || r.direction === q.direction) &&
    (!q.unread || (!r.opened && r.direction === "in")) &&
    (!q.attachments || r.attachments > 0) &&
    (!text || [r.subject, r.from.name, r.from.address, r.company_name, r.job_title].some((s) => s.toLowerCase().includes(text))));
  const offset = q.offset ?? 0;
  return { rows: rows.slice(offset, offset + (q.limit ?? 200)), total: rows.length };
}

export function mailNextUp(root: string): MailNextUp {
  const unread = allMailRows(root).filter((r) => r.direction === "in" && !r.opened);
  return { unread: unread.slice(0, 10), unreadCount: unread.length, drafts: allDrafts(root) };
}

// ---- one message -----------------------------------------------------------
function jobNames(root: string, slug: string, id: string): { company_name: string; job_title: string } {
  let company_name = slug;
  let job_title = "";
  try { company_name = readRecord("company", layout(root).companyFile(slug)).name; } catch { /* company file missing */ }
  if (id) { try { job_title = readRecord("job", layout(root).jobFile(slug, id)).title; } catch { /* job missing */ } }
  return { company_name, job_title };
}

const claimedDomain = (name: string): string => {
  const at = name.match(/@([a-z0-9.-]+\.[a-z]{2,})/i);
  if (at) return at[1]!.toLowerCase();
  const d = name.match(/\b((?:[a-z0-9-]+\.)+[a-z]{2,})\b/i);
  return d ? d[1]!.toLowerCase() : "";
};

function warningsFor(e: EmailRecord): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  const real = domainOf(e.from.address);
  if (e.auth === "fail") out.push(`Gmail couldn't confirm this email really came from ${real}. Be careful with its links and attachments.`);
  const claimed = claimedDomain(e.from.name);
  if (claimed && !domainMatches(real, claimed) && !domainMatches(claimed, real)) out.push(`The sender's name shows ${claimed}, but the email came from ${e.from.address}.`);
  if (e.too_large) out.push(TOO_LARGE);
  return out;
}

export function messageView(root: string, ref: string): MailMessageView {
  const { paths, email: e, raw } = readStored(root, ref);
  const names = paths.triage ? { company_name: "", job_title: "" } : jobNames(root, paths.company_slug, paths.job_id);
  return {
    ref, company_slug: paths.company_slug, company_name: names.company_name, job_id: paths.job_id, job_title: names.job_title, triage: paths.triage,
    direction: e.direction, from: e.from, to: e.to, cc: e.cc, reply_to: e.reply_to, subject: e.subject, date: e.date, message_id: e.message_id,
    references: e.references, thread_key: e.thread_key, auth: e.auth, warnings: warningsFor(e), images_allowed: e.images_allowed,
    remote_images: e.remote_images, too_large: e.too_large, has_raw: raw !== null, opened_at: e.opened_at,
    attachments: e.attachments.filter((a) => !a.inline).map((a) => ({ part: a.part, name: a.name, size: a.size, type: a.type, inline: a.inline, saved_rel: a.saved_as, risky: isRisky(a.name, a.type) })),
  };
}

export function getMessage(ctx: ServiceContext, ref: string): MailMessageView {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const { email } = readStored(root, ref);
  if (!email.opened_at && email.direction === "in") {
    updateStored(root, ref, (e) => { e.opened_at = ctx.now().toISOString(); });
    touch(root, ref);
  }
  return messageView(root, ref);
}

export function loadImages(ctx: ServiceContext, ref: string): MailMessageView {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  updateStored(root, ref, (e) => { e.images_allowed = true; });
  return messageView(root, ref);
}

const parsedCache = new Map<string, ParsedMessage>();
async function parsedFor(paths: MailPaths, raw: Buffer): Promise<ParsedMessage> {
  const key = `${paths.eml}:${raw.length}:${fs.statSync(paths.eml).mtimeMs}`;
  const hit = parsedCache.get(key);
  if (hit) return hit;
  const p = await parseMessage(raw);
  parsedCache.set(key, p);
  if (parsedCache.size > 8) parsedCache.delete(parsedCache.keys().next().value!);
  return p;
}

export async function saveAttachment(ctx: ServiceContext, ref: string, part: string): Promise<MailMessageView> {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const s = readStored(root, ref);
  if (!s.raw) throw new Error("The original email isn't stored any more, so its attachments can't be saved. Open it in Gmail.");
  const a = (await parsedFor(s.paths, s.raw)).attachments.find((x) => x.part === part);
  if (!a) throw new Error("That attachment isn't in this email.");
  saveAttachmentFile(root, ref, part, a.name, a.content, ctx.clock());
  touch(root, ref);
  return messageView(root, ref);
}

export function moveTo(ctx: ServiceContext, ref: string, target: MailTarget): MailMessageView {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  if (target.id) jobFilePath(root, target.company_slug, target.id);
  const { email } = readStored(root, ref);
  const to = moveMessage(root, ref, { company_slug: target.company_slug, job_id: target.id ?? "" });
  if (target.id) addEvent(root, target.company_slug, target.id, email.direction === "in" ? "Email received" : "Email sent", email.subject.slice(0, 200), ctx.clock());
  touch(root, ref, to);
  return messageView(root, to);
}

export function prune(ctx: ServiceContext, months: number): PruneResult {
  return pruneRaw(ctx.requireRoot(), new Date(ctx.now().getTime() - months * 30 * 86_400_000));
}

// ---- careeros-mail: assets -------------------------------------------------
export interface AssetResponse { status: number; headers: Record<string, string>; body: Buffer | string }
const NOT_FOUND: AssetResponse = { status: 404, headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8" }, body: "Not found" };
const SAFE_IMAGE = /^image\/(png|jpeg|gif|webp)$/;
const PLAIN = { "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff", "Cache-Control": "no-store" };

export async function mailAsset(ctx: ServiceContext, url: string): Promise<AssetResponse> {
  const u = parseMailUrl(url);
  const root = ctx.root;
  if (!u || !root) return NOT_FOUND;
  let s: ReturnType<typeof readStored>;
  try { s = readStored(root, u.ref); } catch { return NOT_FOUND; }
  const parsed = s.raw ? await parsedFor(s.paths, s.raw) : null;
  const html = parsed
    ? parsed.html || textToHtml(parsed.text)
    : textToHtml(s.email.too_large ? TOO_LARGE : markdownBody(fs.existsSync(s.paths.md) ? fs.readFileSync(s.paths.md, "utf8") : ""));
  const clean = sanitizeEmailHtml(html, { token: mailRefToken(u.ref), imagesAllowed: s.email.images_allowed });
  if (u.kind === "msg") {
    return { status: 200, headers: { "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8", "Content-Security-Policy": MAIL_FRAME_CSP, "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer", ...PLAIN }, body: renderMailDocument(clean.html) };
  }
  if (u.kind === "cid") {
    const a = parsed?.attachments.find((x) => x.cid === u.arg && SAFE_IMAGE.test(x.type));
    return a ? { status: 200, headers: { "Content-Type": a.type, ...PLAIN }, body: a.content } : NOT_FOUND;
  }
  if (u.kind === "img") {
    if (!s.email.images_allowed) return { status: 403, headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8" }, body: "Images are blocked for this email." };
    const src = clean.remoteImages[Number(u.arg)];
    if (!src || !ctx.host.mail) return NOT_FOUND;
    try {
      const img = await ctx.host.mail.fetchImage(src);
      return { status: 200, headers: { "Content-Type": img.type, ...PLAIN }, body: img.body };
    } catch { return NOT_FOUND; }
  }
  const link = clean.links[Number(u.arg)];
  if (link && isSafeLink(link.href)) {
    const note = linkWarning(link.text, link.href);
    void ctx.host.shell.openExternal(link.href, note || undefined);
  }
  return { status: 204, headers: {}, body: "" };
}

// ---- Mail to sort ----------------------------------------------------------
const triageRef = (id: string) => `.careeros/mail-triage/${id}.json`;

export function triageItems(ctx: ServiceContext): TriageItem[] {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const companies = companyIndex(root);
  return listTriage(root).map((t) => ({
    id: t.id, ref: t.ref, from: t.email.from, subject: t.email.subject, date: t.email.date,
    suggestion: suggestCompany({ direction: t.email.direction, from: t.email.from, to: t.email.to, cc: t.email.cc, subject: t.email.subject }, companies),
  }));
}

export function assignTriage(ctx: ServiceContext, id: string, target: MailTarget, addMatch: boolean): TriageItem[] {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const ref = triageRef(id);
  const { email } = readStored(root, ref);
  if (target.id) jobFilePath(root, target.company_slug, target.id);
  const to = moveMessage(root, ref, { company_slug: target.company_slug, job_id: target.id ?? "" });
  if (addMatch && email.direction === "in") {
    const a = email.from.address.toLowerCase();
    updateRecord("company", layout(root).companyFile(target.company_slug), (c) => {
      if (!c.email_match.some((m) => m.toLowerCase() === a)) c.email_match = [...c.email_match, a].slice(-50);
    });
  }
  if (target.id) addEvent(root, target.company_slug, target.id, email.direction === "in" ? "Email received" : "Email sent", email.subject.slice(0, 200), ctx.clock());
  touch(root, to, `companies/${target.company_slug}`);
  return triageItems(ctx);
}

export function createCompanyFromTriage(ctx: ServiceContext, id: string, name: string, jobTitle: string): TriageItem[] {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  readStored(root, triageRef(id));
  const j = newJob(root, { company: name.trim(), title: jobTitle.trim(), source: "pasted" }, ctx.clock());
  touch(root, `companies/${j.company_slug}`);
  return assignTriage(ctx, id, { company_slug: j.company_slug, id: j.id }, true);
}

export function ignoreTriage(ctx: ServiceContext, id: string, scope: "sender" | "domain"): TriageItem[] {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const { email } = readStored(root, triageRef(id));
  const st = readMailState(root);
  const value = scope === "sender" ? email.from.address.toLowerCase() : domainOf(email.from.address);
  const list = scope === "sender" ? st.ignore.senders : st.ignore.domains;
  if (!list.includes(value)) list.push(value);
  writeMailState(root, st);
  for (const t of listTriage(root)) if (isIgnored(t.email.from.address, st.ignore)) deleteStored(root, t.ref);
  return triageItems(ctx);
}

// ---- drafts and previews ---------------------------------------------------
export function toDraft(input: DraftInput): Draft {
  return checkDraft({
    to: parseAddressList(input.to), cc: parseAddressList(input.cc), subject: input.subject, attach: input.attach,
    in_reply_to: input.in_reply_to, references: input.references, purpose: input.purpose, body: input.body,
  }, false);
}

export function toInput(d: Draft): DraftInput {
  return { to: d.to.map(formatAddress).join(", "), cc: d.cc.map(formatAddress).join(", "), subject: d.subject, body: d.body, attach: d.attach, in_reply_to: d.in_reply_to, references: d.references, purpose: d.purpose };
}

export function draftViews(root: string, slug: string, id: string): DraftView[] {
  const names = jobNames(root, slug, id);
  const proposals = listApprovals(root).filter((a) => a.action === "email_send" && a.payload.company_slug === slug && a.payload.id === id);
  return listDraftFiles(root, slug, id).map((f): DraftView => {
    let subject = "(this draft can't be read)";
    let to = "";
    try {
      const d = parseDraft(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, ...f.rel.split("/")), "utf8"));
      subject = d.subject;
      to = d.to.map((a) => a.address).join(", ");
    } catch { /* shown as unreadable */ }
    const a = proposals.filter((x) => x.payload.draft_id === f.draft_id).at(-1);
    const status: DraftStatus = f.sent ? "sent" : a?.status === "pending" || a?.status === "approved" ? "waiting" : a?.status === "failed" ? "failed" : "draft";
    return {
      draft_id: f.draft_id, company_slug: slug, id, company_name: names.company_name, job_title: names.job_title, rel: f.rel, subject, to, status,
      updated: f.mtime, approval_id: a?.id ?? "", note: a?.status === "failed" ? a.result_note : "",
    };
  }).sort((x, y) => y.updated.localeCompare(x.updated) || x.draft_id.localeCompare(y.draft_id));
}

export function allDrafts(root: string): DraftView[] {
  return listJobs(root).flatMap((j) => draftViews(root, j.company_slug, j.id)).sort((x, y) => y.updated.localeCompare(x.updated));
}

const lockedDir = (root: string, rel: string): string | null => {
  const r = rel.toLowerCase();
  return readMeta(root).privacy.locked.find((d) => r === d || r.startsWith(`${d}/`)) ?? null;
};

export function emailPreview(root: string, slug: string, id: string, draftId: string, stamp: MessageStamp, expected?: string, c?: Clock): EmailPreview {
  const names = jobNames(root, slug, id);
  const job = { company_slug: slug, id, title: names.job_title, company_name: names.company_name };
  try {
    const b = buildDraftMessage(root, slug, id, draftId, stamp, c);
    const m = b.message;
    const current = expected === undefined || b.digest === expected;
    return {
      account: m.from.address, from: m.from, to: m.to, cc: m.cc, subject: m.subject, text: m.text, in_reply_to: m.in_reply_to,
      attachments: m.attachments.map((a) => ({ rel: a.rel, name: a.name, size: a.size, type: a.type, locked: a.locked })),
      locked: m.attachments.some((a) => a.locked !== null), current, problem: current ? "" : "The draft changed. Review it again.", job,
    };
  } catch (e) {
    const acct = mailAccount(root);
    let d: Draft | null = null;
    try { d = readDraft(root, slug, id, draftId); } catch { d = null; }
    const attachments = (d?.attach ?? []).map((rel) => ({ rel, name: rel.split("/").pop() ?? rel, size: 0, type: mimeTypeFor(rel), locked: lockedDir(root, rel) }));
    return {
      account: acct.address, from: { name: acct.display_name, address: acct.address }, to: d?.to ?? [], cc: d?.cc ?? [], subject: d?.subject ?? "",
      text: d ? messageText(d.body, acct.signature) : "", in_reply_to: d?.in_reply_to ?? "", attachments, locked: attachments.some((a) => a.locked !== null),
      current: false, problem: (e as Error).message.split("\n")[0]!, job,
    };
  }
}

export function withEmail(root: string, v: ApprovalView, c?: Clock): ApprovalView {
  if (v.action !== "email_send" || v.status !== "pending") return v;
  const p = v.payload;
  try {
    return { ...v, email: emailPreview(root, String(p.company_slug), String(p.id), String(p.draft_id), { message_id: String(p.message_id), date: String(p.date) }, String(p.sha256), c) };
  } catch { return v; }
}

export function draftDetail(ctx: ServiceContext, slug: string, id: string, draftId: string): DraftDetail {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const view = draftViews(root, slug, id).find((v) => v.draft_id === draftId);
  if (!view) throw new Error(`The draft ${draftId} can't be found.`);
  const draft = parseDraft(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, ...view.rel.split("/")), "utf8"));
  const preview = view.status === "sent" ? null : emailPreview(root, slug, id, draftId, { message_id: "<preview>", date: ctx.now().toISOString() }, undefined, ctx.clock());
  return { view, draft: toInput(draft), preview };
}

export function saveDraft(ctx: ServiceContext, slug: string, id: string, draftId: string, input: DraftInput): DraftDetail {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  if (draftId && listDraftFiles(root, slug, id).some((f) => f.draft_id === draftId && f.sent)) throw new Error("This email was already sent. Start a new draft.");
  const w = writeDraft(root, slug, id, toDraft(input), draftId || undefined, ctx.clock());
  return draftDetail(ctx, slug, id, w.draft_id);
}

export function proposeDraft(ctx: ServiceContext, slug: string, id: string, draftId: string): ApprovalView {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  return withEmail(root, approvalView(proposeEmailSend(root, slug, id, draftId, "app", ctx.clock())), ctx.clock());
}

export const mailReadHandlers = {
  "mail.list": (ctx: ServiceContext, q: MailQuery) => mailList(ctx.requireRoot(), q),
  "mail.get": getMessage,
  "mail.loadImages": loadImages,
  "mail.saveAttachment": saveAttachment,
  "mail.move": moveTo,
  "mail.prune": prune,
  "mail.triage.list": triageItems,
  "mail.triage.assign": assignTriage,
  "mail.triage.createCompany": createCompanyFromTriage,
  "mail.triage.ignore": ignoreTriage,
  "mail.drafts.all": (ctx: ServiceContext) => allDrafts(ctx.requireRoot()),
  "mail.drafts.list": (ctx: ServiceContext, slug: string, id: string) => draftViews(ctx.requireRoot(), slug, id),
  "mail.drafts.get": draftDetail,
  "mail.drafts.save": saveDraft,
  "mail.drafts.propose": proposeDraft,
};
```

### Task 16: Services: the sync timer, Sync now, and background sync (settings, OS schedule, the headless run)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/services/src/mail-sync.ts`, `packages/services/test/mail-sync.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/services/src/runtime.ts` (`Runtime.syncMailSoon`, `Runtime.resolveMailAsset`; installs the timer), `packages/services/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `syncMail`, `redact`, `type SyncResult` (mail); `mailHost`, `mailSettings`, `currentAccount`, `syncInfo`, `accountHooks`, `mailStatus` (Task 14); `mailAsset`, `type AssetResponse` (Task 15); core `refreshIndex`, `updateRecord`, `layout`, `logActivity`, `readMeta`; contract `BackgroundStatus`, `MailStatus`.
- Produces:
  - `runSync(ctx): Promise<SyncResult | null>` (null when nothing ran: no account, or a sync already running in this process)
  - `installMailTimer(ctx, opts?: { firstMs?: number; soonMs?: number }): void` (first sync 5 s after a workspace opens, then every `sync_minutes`; registers "sync soon" in `accountHooks`)
  - `syncMailSoon(ctx): void` (debounced; used after saving an account and when the computer wakes)
  - `syncNow(ctx): Promise<MailStatus>`, `backgroundStatus(ctx): Promise<BackgroundStatus>`, `setBackground(ctx, on: boolean, minutes: number): Promise<BackgroundStatus>`
  - `runBackgroundSync(host: Host, workspace: string): Promise<{ code: number; line: string }>`
  - `mailSyncHandlers` (keys `mail.syncNow`, `mail.background.status`, `mail.background.set`)
  - `Runtime` gains `syncMailSoon(): void` and `resolveMailAsset(url: string): Promise<AssetResponse>`

Rulings: the app timer, Sync now and the background job all go through `syncMail`, whose lock file in user data keeps runs from overlapping across processes; in one process, a second request while one is running returns the current status and starts nothing. A log line holds counts and the error sentence only, passed through `redact` with the password, so it never holds an address or a secret. The background job reads the password from the OS store; when that store is locked (a Mac before first unlock, a Linux session without a keyring) it writes one line saying so and exits 0, so the OS doesn't retry in a loop. Background sync is per user and off by default; turning it on needs a connected account.

```ts
import { layout, readRecord, updateRecord } from "@careeros/core";

}

    expect(mail.logs.join("\n")).not.toContain(ALEX);

    expect(s.last_ok).toBe("2026-01-15T09:00:00.000Z");
    expect(R["mail.list"](ctx, {}).total).toBe(1);
  });
  it("runs one sync at a time in a process", async () => {
    const { ctx, root, mail } = setup();
    connectMail(ctx, root);
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([runSync(ctx), runSync(ctx)]);
    expect([a, b].filter((r) => r !== null)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(mail.fake.calls.filter((c) => c === "connect")).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

describe("the app timer", () => {
  it("syncs soon after an account is saved, shows the next sync, and stops when the workspace closes", async () => {
    const { ctx, root, mail } = setup();
    installMailTimer(ctx, { firstMs: 20, soonMs: 20 });
    // The first tick finds no account and does nothing.
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 60));
    expect(mail.fake.calls).not.toContain("connect");
    expect(syncInfo(ctx).next).not.toBe("");
    connectMail(ctx, root);
    // saveAccount calls this hook; connectMail writes the settings directly, so call it here.
    accountHooks.get(ctx)!();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(mail.fake.calls).toContain("connect"), { timeout: 3000, interval: 20 });
    ctx.setRoot(null);
    expect(syncInfo(ctx).next).toBe("");
  });
});

describe("background sync", () => {
  it("installs a per-user schedule only for a connected account, and removes it", async () => {
    const { ctx, root, scheduler } = setup();
    await expect(S["mail.background.set"](ctx, true, 60)).rejects.toThrow("Connect Gmail in Settings > Email first.");
    connectMail(ctx, root);
    const on = await S["mail.background.set"](ctx, true, 120);
    expect(on).toMatchObject({ supported: true, on: true, minutes: 120, installed: true });
    expect(scheduler.installed).toEqual({ program: "/fictional/CareerOS", args: ["--background-sync", "--workspace", root], minutes: 120 });
    expect(readRecord("integrations", layout(root).integrations).mail.background).toEqual({ on: true, minutes: 120 });
    const off = await S["mail.background.set"](ctx, false, 120);
    expect(off).toMatchObject({ on: false, installed: false });
    expect(scheduler.installed).toBeNull();
  });
  it("says when the system can't schedule it", async () => {
    const { ctx, root } = setup({ supported: false });
    connectMail(ctx, root);
    expect(await S["mail.background.status"](ctx)).toMatchObject({ supported: false, on: false });
    await expect(S["mail.background.set"](ctx, true, 60)).rejects.toThrow("Background sync isn't available on this system.");
  });
  it("runs headless: syncs, logs one line and exits 0", async () => {
    const { ctx, root, mail, host } = setup();
    connectMail(ctx, root);
    updateRecord("integrations", layout(root).integrations, (i) => { i.mail.background.on = true; });
    const r = await runBackgroundSync(host, root);
    expect(r.code).toBe(0);
    expect(r.line).toMatch(/sync stored=1 sorted=0 skipped=1/);
    expect(mail.logs.at(-1)).toBe(r.line);
  });
  it("does nothing when background sync is off, the folder isn't a workspace, or the password store is locked", async () => {
    const { ctx, root, mail, host } = setup();
    connectMail(ctx, root);
    expect(await runBackgroundSync(host, root)).toMatchObject({ code: 0, line: expect.stringMatching(/Background sync is off/) });
    updateRecord("integrations", layout(root).integrations, (i) => { i.mail.background.on = true; });
    expect(await runBackgroundSync(host, "/fictional/missing")).toMatchObject({ code: 1, line: expect.stringMatching(/isn't a CareerOS workspace/) });
    const locked = fakeHost({ mail, secrets: memorySecrets(false) });
    expect(await runBackgroundSync(locked, root)).toMatchObject({ code: 0, line: expect.stringMatching(/password store is locked/) });
    expect(mail.fake.calls).not.toContain("connect");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/services/src/mail-sync.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import { layout, logActivity, refreshIndex, updateRecord } from "@careeros/core";
import { redact, syncMail, type SyncResult } from "@careeros/mail";
import type { BackgroundStatus, MailStatus } from "@careeros/contract";
import { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import type { Host } from "./host.js";
import { accountHooks, currentAccount, mailHost, mailSettings, mailStatus, syncInfo } from "./mail-account.js";

const NOT_CONNECTED = "Connect Gmail in Settings > Email first.";
const soonTimers = new WeakMap<ServiceContext, { ms: number; handle?: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> }>();

function summary(at: Date, r: SyncResult): string {
  const base = `${at.toISOString()} sync stored=${r.stored} sorted=${r.triaged} skipped=${r.skipped} duplicates=${r.duplicates} too_large=${r.too_large} capped=${r.capped ? "yes" : "no"}`;
  return r.error ? `${base} error=${r.error}` : base;
}

/** Folders whose index rows change when these stored emails appear (the emails and their job's timeline). */
function changedPaths(touched: string[]): string[] {
  const out = new Set<string>(touched);
  for (const ref of touched) {
    const m = ref.match(/^(companies\/[^/]+\/jobs\/[^/]+)\//);
    if (m) out.add(m[1]!);
  }
  return [...out];
}

export async function runSync(ctx: ServiceContext): Promise<SyncResult | null> {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const host = mailHost(ctx);
  const account = currentAccount(ctx);
  const info = syncInfo(ctx);
  if (!account || info.syncing) return null;
  info.syncing = true;
  ctx.emit("mail");
  try {
    const m = mailSettings(root);
    const now = ctx.now();
    const r = await syncMail({ root, provider: host.provider(account), account: account.address, now, firstSyncDays: m.first_sync_days, lockFile: host.lockFile });
    if (r.touched.length) { try { refreshIndex(root, changedPaths(r.touched)); } catch { /* the next read rebuilds the index */ } }
    host.log(redact(summary(now, r), [account.password]));
    return r;
  } finally {
    info.syncing = false;
    ctx.emit("mail", "jobs", "home");
  }
}

export function syncMailSoon(ctx: ServiceContext): void {
  const t = soonTimers.get(ctx);
  if (!t || !ctx.root || !ctx.host.mail) return;
  if (t.handle) clearTimeout(t.handle);
  t.handle = setTimeout(() => { t.handle = undefined; void runSync(ctx).catch(() => undefined); }, t.ms);
}

/** Syncs while a workspace is open: first after firstMs, then every sync_minutes (read again before each wait). */
export function installMailTimer(ctx: ServiceContext, opts: { firstMs?: number; soonMs?: number } = {}): void {
  if (!ctx.host.mail) return;
  soonTimers.set(ctx, { ms: opts.soonMs ?? 2000 });
  accountHooks.set(ctx, () => syncMailSoon(ctx));
  ctx.onOpen((root) => {
    let handle: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    let stopped = false;
    const info = syncInfo(ctx);
    const arm = (ms: number) => {
      if (stopped) return;
      info.next = new Date(ctx.now().getTime() + ms).toISOString();
      handle = setTimeout(async () => {
        try { if (ctx.root === root) await runSync(ctx); } catch { /* shown through last_error */ }
        let minutes = 15;
        try { minutes = mailSettings(root).sync_minutes; } catch { /* keep the default */ }
        arm(minutes * 60_000);
      }, ms);
    };
    arm(opts.firstMs ?? 5000);
    return () => {
      stopped = true;
      if (handle) clearTimeout(handle);
      const soon = soonTimers.get(ctx);
      if (soon?.handle) { clearTimeout(soon.handle); soon.handle = undefined; }
      info.next = "";
    };
  });
}

export async function syncNow(ctx: ServiceContext): Promise<MailStatus> {
  mailHost(ctx);
  if (!currentAccount(ctx)) throw new Error(NOT_CONNECTED);
  await runSync(ctx);
  return mailStatus(ctx);
}

export async function backgroundStatus(ctx: ServiceContext): Promise<BackgroundStatus> {
  const m = mailSettings(ctx.requireRoot());
  const s = ctx.host.scheduler;
  const st = s?.supported ? await s.status() : { installed: false, detail: "Background sync isn't available on this system." };
  return { supported: !!s?.supported, on: m.background.on, minutes: m.background.minutes, installed: st.installed, detail: st.detail, log: ctx.host.mail?.readLog().slice(-20) ?? [] };
}

export async function setBackground(ctx: ServiceContext, on: boolean, minutes: number): Promise<BackgroundStatus> {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const host = mailHost(ctx);
  const s = ctx.host.scheduler;
  if (on) {
    if (!currentAccount(ctx)) throw new Error(NOT_CONNECTED);
    if (!s?.supported) throw new Error("Background sync isn't available on this system.");
    await s.install({ ...host.backgroundCommand(root), minutes });
  } else if (s?.supported) {
    await s.remove();
  }
  updateRecord("integrations", layout(root).integrations, (i) => { i.mail.background = { on, minutes }; });
  logActivity(root, on ? "Background mail sync on" : "Background mail sync off", on ? `every ${minutes} minutes` : "", ctx.clock());
  return backgroundStatus(ctx);
}

/** The `--background-sync` run: no window, one log line, exit code 0 unless the workspace is unusable. */
export async function runBackgroundSync(host: Host, workspace: string): Promise<{ code: number; line: string }> {
  const ctx = new ServiceContext(host);
  const at = ctx.now().toISOString();
  const done = (code: number, text: string) => {
    const line = redact(text.startsWith(at) ? text : `${at} ${text}`);
    host.mail?.log(line);
    return { code, line };
  };
  if (!host.mail) return done(1, "Email isn't available in this version of the app.");
  if (!fs.existsSync(layout(workspace).workspaceFile)) return done(1, "The background sync folder isn't a CareerOS workspace. Turn background sync off and on again in Settings > Email.");
  ctx.root = workspace;
  let m;
  try { m = mailSettings(workspace); } catch { return done(1, "The workspace settings can't be read."); }
  if (!m.background.on) return done(0, "Background sync is off. Nothing to do.");
  if (!m.address) return done(0, "Gmail isn't connected. Nothing to do.");
  if (!host.secrets.available() || !host.secrets.has("gmail_app_password")) {
    return done(0, "The password store is locked, so mail wasn't synced. CareerOS will try again next time.");
  }
  const account = currentAccount(ctx)!;
  const r = await syncMail({ root: workspace, provider: host.mail.provider(account), account: account.address, now: ctx.now(), firstSyncDays: m.first_sync_days, lockFile: host.mail.lockFile });
  if (r.touched.length) { try { refreshIndex(workspace, changedPaths(r.touched)); } catch { /* rebuilt on next open */ } }
  const line = redact(summary(new Date(at), r), [account.password]);
  host.mail.log(line);
  return { code: 0, line };
}

export const mailSyncHandlers = {
  "mail.syncNow": syncNow,
  "mail.background.status": backgroundStatus,
  "mail.background.set": setBackground,
};
```

`layout(root).workspaceFile` is the path of `workspace.json` (core 3a `layout`). The headless run writes its own line through `done` only on the early exits; a real run writes exactly one summary line.

### Task 17: Services: sending an approved email (hash check, once only, hourly cap, sent copy)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/services/src/mail-send.ts`, `packages/services/test/mail-send.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/services/src/approvals.ts` (`execute` runs `email_send`; the refusal goes), `packages/services/test/approvals.test.ts` (the 3a email test), `packages/services/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: core `buildDraftMessage`, `addEvent`, `logActivity`, `readActivity`, `moveDraftToSent`, `type Approval`; mail `buildMime`, `envelopeFor`, `parseMessage`, `storeMessage`; services `mailHost`, `mailSettings`, `currentAccount` (Task 14), `touch`, `jobDirRel`.
- Produces: `sendEmailApproval(ctx, a: Approval): Promise<string>` (the approval's result note); `sentInLastHour(root, now): number`.

Rulings: the approval is claimed on disk (`pending -> approved`) before anything else, as 3a does for every action, so a double click, a second window or a retried IPC call sends nothing twice. Before sending, services rebuild the message with the stamp saved in the proposal and compare the digest; any change to the draft, the signature, the display name or the bytes of any attachment fails the approval with "The draft changed. Review it again." and sends nothing. An attachment that was deleted, moved or locked again also fails it, with the reason. Once the SMTP server has accepted the message, nothing after that can fail the approval: saving the sent copy, the timeline event and moving the draft are each tried, and a problem with one is added to the result note. The hourly cap counts "Email sent" activity entries in the last 60 minutes.

```ts
const input = (over = {}) => ({ to: `Acme Talent <${TALENT}>`, cc: "", subject: "Application for AI Engineer", body: "Hi,\n\nPlease find my resume attached.\n\nRegards,\nAlex", attach: [RESUME], in_reply_to: "", references: [], purpose: "apply" as const, ...over });

function setup() {

  };

}

describe("approving an email send", () => {

    expect(readRecord("job", layout(root).jobFile(...ACME)).status).not.toBe("Applied");

    expect(mail.fake.sent).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("stops at the hourly limit", async () => {

    await AP["approvals.approve"](ctx, propose().approval.id);

    expect(await AP["approvals.approve"](ctx, second.approval.id)).toMatchObject({

    });
    expect(mail.fake.sent).toHaveLength(1);

  it("fails cleanly when Gmail refuses or the account changed", async () => {
    const { ctx, root, mail, propose } = setup();
    const a = propose();
    mail.fake.failWith = "network";

    expect(await AP["approvals.approve"](ctx, b.approval.id)).toMatchObject({ status: "failed", result_note: "This email was prepared for another Gmail account. Propose it again." });
    expect(mail.fake.sent).toEqual([]);
    expect(R["mail.list"](ctx, { direction: "out" }).total).toBe(0);
    expect(listApprovals(root).filter((x) => x.status === "failed")).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/services/src/mail-send.ts`**

```ts
import { addEvent, buildDraftMessage, logActivity, moveDraftToSent, readActivity, type Approval } from "@careeros/core";
import { buildMime, envelopeFor, parseMessage, storeMessage } from "@careeros/mail";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import { currentAccount, mailHost, mailSettings } from "./mail-account.js";
import { jobDirRel, touch } from "./read.js";

const HOUR = 3_600_000;

export function sentInLastHour(root: string, now: Date): number {
  const since = now.getTime() - HOUR;
  return readActivity(root, 1000).filter((e) => e.event === "Email sent" && Date.parse(e.at) > since).length;
}

/** Runs one approved email_send. Throws (failing the approval) only before the message leaves. */
export async function sendEmailApproval(ctx: ServiceContext, a: Approval): Promise<string> {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const host = mailHost(ctx);
  const p = a.payload as Record<string, unknown>;
  const slug = String(p.company_slug ?? "");
  const id = String(p.id ?? "");
  const draftId = String(p.draft_id ?? "");
  const account = currentAccount(ctx);
  if (!account) throw new Error("Gmail isn't connected. Connect it in Settings > Email, then propose the email again.");
  if (account.address.toLowerCase() !== String(p.account ?? "").toLowerCase()) throw new Error("This email was prepared for another Gmail account. Propose it again.");

  const built = buildDraftMessage(root, slug, id, draftId, { message_id: String(p.message_id ?? ""), date: String(p.date ?? "") }, ctx.clock());
  if (built.digest !== p.sha256) throw new Error("The draft changed. Review it again.");

  const limit = mailSettings(root).send_limit_per_hour;
  const recent = sentInLastHour(root, ctx.now());
  if (recent >= limit) {
    throw new Error(`CareerOS has sent ${recent} email${recent === 1 ? "" : "s"} in the last hour, the most your settings allow. Try again later or raise the limit in Settings > Email.`);
  }

  const m = built.message;
  const raw = await buildMime(m, built.contents);
  await host.provider(account).send(envelopeFor(m), raw);

  // The message has left. From here on, problems are reported in the note, never as a failed approval.
  const count = m.to.length + m.cc.length;
  const problems: string[] = [];
  try {
    const parsed = await parseMessage(raw);
    const s = storeMessage(root, { company_slug: slug, job_id: id }, {
      raw, parsed, mailbox: "Sent", direction: "out", matched_by: "user", tooLarge: false,
      header: { uid: 0, size: raw.length, internalDate: m.date, messageId: m.message_id, from: [m.from], to: m.to, cc: m.cc, subject: m.subject },
    }, ctx.clock());
    touch(root, s.ref);
  } catch { problems.push("the sent copy couldn't be saved (Gmail has it in Sent)"); }
  try { addEvent(root, slug, id, "Email sent", m.subject.slice(0, 200), ctx.clock()); } catch { problems.push("the timeline couldn't be updated"); }
  logActivity(root, "Email sent", m.subject.slice(0, 200), ctx.clock());
  try { moveDraftToSent(root, slug, id, draftId); } catch { problems.push("the draft couldn't be moved to sent"); }
  touch(root, jobDirRel(slug, id));
  const note = `Sent to ${count} recipient${count === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
  return problems.length ? `${note}, but ${problems.join(" and ")}` : note;
}
```

`storeMessage` dedupes nothing by itself; the next sync sees Gmail's own Sent copy with the same Message-ID and skips it (`knownMessageIds`, Task 11).

### Task 18: Wiring: `mail` in the bridge, validators, dispatcher, change areas, preload and MockBridge

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-ui/src/mock/mail-mock.ts`, `packages/app-ui/test/mock-mail.test.ts`, `packages/contract/test/mail-methods.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/contract/src/methods.ts`, `packages/contract/src/bridge.ts`, `packages/contract/test/bridge.test.ts`, `packages/services/src/dispatch.ts`, `packages/services/src/watcher.ts`, `packages/services/test/watcher.test.ts`, `packages/services/src/mail-account.ts`, `packages/services/src/mail-read.ts`, `packages/services/src/mail-sync.ts` (each handler table gains `satisfies Partial<HandlerTable>`), `packages/app-electron/src/preload.cts`, `packages/app-ui/src/mock/MockBridge.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every mail handler table (Tasks 14-16), contract mail types and `MAIL_REF_PATTERN`, `mailUrlFor` (Task 13).
- Produces:
  - `METHODS` gains the 23 `mail.*` methods listed below, with validators `mailRef`, `mailPart`, `triageId`, `draftId`, `draftIdOrNew`, `mailTarget`, `mailQuery`, `draftInput` (exported from `methods.ts`)
  - `CareerosBridge.mail` (shape below) and the local `CareerosBridge.mailUrl(ref): string`; `Transport.mailUrl(ref): string`
  - `MUTATION_AREAS` mail entries; `areasFor` maps `emails/` paths and `.careeros/mail-*` to `"mail"`
  - `createMockMail(h: MockMailHooks): MockMail` with `api: CareerosBridge["mail"]`, `nextUp(): MailNextUp`, `withEmail(a): ApprovalView`, `sendApproved(a): string`, `applyEmail(slug, id): string`; `MockOptions.mailConnected?: boolean` (default true); `MOCK_GOOD_PASSWORD = "abcdefghijklmnop"`

Ruling: the renderer passes the app password exactly once, to `mail.testConnection`, and it never comes back in any reply. MockBridge records that argument as `"<redacted>"` like it does for `secrets.set`.

In `packages/contract/test/bridge.test.ts`, replace the body of `"routes every bridge call to exactly the METHODS list"` with a recursive walk and the new local function:

```ts
  it("routes every bridge call to exactly the METHODS list", async () => {
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    const bridge = createRemoteBridge({
      call: async (m: MethodName) => { seen.add(m); return null; },
      onChange: () => () => undefined, pathForFile: () => null, fileUrl: (r) => `careeros-file://ws/${r}`, mailUrl: (r) => `careeros-mail://msg/${r}`,
    });
    const walk = async (group: Record<string, unknown>) => {
      for (const v of Object.values(group)) {
        if (typeof v === "function") await (v as (...a: unknown[]) => Promise<unknown>)();
        else if (typeof v === "object" && v !== null) await walk(v as Record<string, unknown>);
      }
    };
    for (const [ns, group] of Object.entries(bridge)) {
      if (typeof group !== "object" || group === null) continue;
      expect(ns).toMatch(/^[a-z]+$/);
      await walk(group as Record<string, unknown>);
    }
    expect([...seen].sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(METHODS).sort());
    expect(bridge.fileUrl("learning/a b.md")).toBe("careeros-file://ws/learning/a b.md");
    expect(bridge.mailUrl("x")).toBe("careeros-mail://msg/x");
  });
```

```ts
import { METHODS } from "@careeros/contract";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MOCK_GOOD_PASSWORD } from "../src/mock/mail-mock";
import { MockBridge } from "../src/mock/MockBridge";

const ADDRESS = "alex" + "@" + "example.com";

describe("MockBridge mail", () => {
  it("implements every contract method", () => {
    const b = new MockBridge() as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
    for (const m of Object.keys(METHODS)) {
      const fn = m.split(".").reduce<unknown>((o, k) => (o as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.[k], b);
      expect(typeof fn, m).toBe("function");
    }
  });
  it("connects only after a passing test and never records the password", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge({ mailConnected: false });
    expect((await b.mail.status()).connected).toBe(false);
    expect((await b.mail.testConnection(ADDRESS, "Alex Example", "zzzz yyyy xxxx wwww")).ok).toBe(false);
    await expect(b.mail.saveAccount(ADDRESS, "Alex Example")).rejects.toThrow(/Test the connection first/);
    expect((await b.mail.testConnection(ADDRESS, "Alex Example", MOCK_GOOD_PASSWORD)).ok).toBe(true);
    expect(await b.mail.saveAccount(ADDRESS, "Alex Example")).toMatchObject({ connected: true, address: ADDRESS });
    expect(JSON.stringify(b.calls)).not.toContain(MOCK_GOOD_PASSWORD);
  });
  it("lists mail, marks it read and keeps images blocked until asked", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    const list = await b.mail.list({});
    expect(list.total).toBe(3);
    const first = list.rows[0]!;
    expect(first.opened).toBe(false);
    const v = await b.mail.get(first.ref);
    expect(v.images_allowed).toBe(false);
    expect((await b.mail.loadImages(first.ref)).images_allowed).toBe(true);
    expect((await b.mail.list({ unread: true })).rows.map((r) => r.ref)).not.toContain(first.ref);
    expect(b.mailUrl(first.ref)).toMatch(/^careeros-mail:\/\/msg\//);
  });
  it("sends a proposed draft only on approve", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    const d = await b.mail.drafts.save("acme-corp", "2026-01-12-ai-engineer", "", {
      to: "talent" + "@" + "example.org", cc: "", subject: "Re: Interview", body: "Friday works.", attach: [], in_reply_to: "", references: [], purpose: "",
    });
    expect(d.preview?.text).toBe("Friday works.\n\nAlex Example\nAI Engineer\n");
    const a = await b.mail.drafts.propose("acme-corp", "2026-01-12-ai-engineer", d.view.draft_id);
    expect((await b.approvals.list()).find((x) => x.id === a.id)?.email?.subject).toBe("Re: Interview");
    expect((await b.mail.list({ direction: "out" })).total).toBe(1);
    expect(await b.approvals.approve(a.id)).toMatchObject({ status: "done", result_note: "Sent to 1 recipient" });
    expect((await b.mail.list({ direction: "out" })).total).toBe(2);
    expect((await b.mail.drafts.list("acme-corp", "2026-01-12-ai-engineer")).find((x) => x.draft_id === d.view.draft_id)?.status).toBe("sent");
    expect((await b.jobs.get("acme-corp", "2026-01-12-ai-engineer")).job.timeline.at(-1)?.event).toBe("Email sent");
  });
  it("sorts mail: assign with always-match, and ignore", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    const [first, second] = await b.mail.triage.list();
    expect(first!.suggestion?.company_slug).toBe("example-labs");
    const left = await b.mail.triage.assign(first!.id, { company_slug: "example-labs", id: "2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm" }, true);
    expect(left.map((i) => i.id)).toEqual([second!.id]);
    expect(await b.mail.triage.ignore(second!.id, "domain")).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("counts unread mail on Home", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    expect((await b.home.summary()).unreadMail).toBe(2);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write `packages/app-ui/src/mock/mail-mock.ts`**

```ts
import {
  mailUrlFor, type ApprovalView, type BackgroundStatus, type CareerosBridge, type ChangeArea, type DraftDetail, type DraftInput, type DraftView,
  type EmailPreview, type MailAddressView, type MailList, type MailMessageView, type MailNextUp, type MailQuery, type MailRow, type MailStatus,
  type MailTarget, type MailTestResult, type TriageItem,
} from "@careeros/contract";

export const MOCK_GOOD_PASSWORD = "abcdefghijklmnop";
const at = (user: string, domain: string) => user + "@" + domain;
const ALEX = at("alex", "example.com");
const TALENT = at("talent", "example.org");
const EMAIL = /^[^\s@<>"]+@[^\s@<>"]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$/;
const ACME = { slug: "acme-corp", id: "2026-01-12-ai-engineer", company: "Acme Corp", title: "AI Engineer" };
const LABS = { slug: "example-labs", id: "2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm", company: "Example Labs", title: "Backend Engineer (LLM)" };

export interface MockMailHooks {
  rec<T>(method: string, args: unknown[], fn: () => T): Promise<T>;
  emit(...areas: ChangeArea[]): void;
  now(): string;
  jobName(slug: string, id: string): { company: string; title: string; apply_via: string };
  event(slug: string, id: string, event: string, note: string): void;
  connected: boolean;
}
export interface MockMail {
  api: CareerosBridge["mail"];
  nextUp(): MailNextUp;
  withEmail(a: ApprovalView): ApprovalView;
  sendApproved(a: ApprovalView): string;
  applyEmail(slug: string, id: string): string;
}

interface Msg { view: MailMessageView }
interface Draft { draft_id: string; slug: string; id: string; input: DraftInput; status: DraftView["status"]; updated: string; approval_id: string; note: string }

const refFor = (slug: string, id: string, base: string) => `companies/${slug}/${id ? `jobs/${id}/` : ""}emails/${base}.json`;

function message(o: {
  base: string; job: typeof ACME; direction?: "in" | "out"; from: MailAddressView; subject: string; date: string; auth?: "pass" | "fail" | "none";
  warnings?: string[]; remote?: number; attachments?: MailMessageView["attachments"]; opened?: string;
}): Msg {
  return {
    view: {
      ref: refFor(o.job.slug, o.job.id, o.base), company_slug: o.job.slug, company_name: o.job.company, job_id: o.job.id, job_title: o.job.title,
      triage: false, direction: o.direction ?? "in", from: o.from, to: [{ name: "Alex Example", address: ALEX }], cc: [], reply_to: [],
      subject: o.subject, date: o.date, message_id: `<${o.base}@example.org>`, references: [], thread_key: `<${o.base}@example.org>`,
      auth: o.auth ?? "pass", warnings: o.warnings ?? [], images_allowed: false, remote_images: o.remote ?? 0, too_large: false, has_raw: true,
      attachments: o.attachments ?? [], opened_at: o.opened ?? "",
    },
  };
}

function seed(): { messages: Msg[]; triage: TriageItem[]; drafts: Draft[] } {
  const spoofFrom = at("x", "evil.example.net");
  return {
    messages: [
      message({
        base: "2026-01-14-interview-for-ai-engineer-a1b2c3", job: ACME, from: { name: "Acme Talent", address: TALENT }, subject: "Interview for AI Engineer",
        date: "2026-01-14T09:00:00.000Z", remote: 1,
        attachments: [
          { part: "2", name: "Interview details.pdf", size: 18_000, type: "application/pdf", inline: false, saved_rel: "", risky: false },
          { part: "3", name: "setup.exe", size: 2, type: "application/octet-stream", inline: false, saved_rel: "", risky: true },
        ],
      }),
      message({
        base: "2026-01-13-urgent-confirm-your-details-d4e5f6", job: LABS, from: { name: `Example Labs (${at("talent", "examplelabs.example.net")})`, address: spoofFrom },
        subject: "Urgent: confirm your details", date: "2026-01-13T09:00:00.000Z", auth: "fail",
        warnings: [
          "Gmail couldn't confirm this email really came from evil.example.net. Be careful with its links and attachments.",
          `The sender's name shows examplelabs.example.net, but the email came from ${spoofFrom}.`,
        ],
      }),
      message({
        base: "2026-01-12-re-ai-engineer-application-0a0b0c", job: ACME, direction: "out", from: { name: "Alex Example", address: ALEX },
        subject: "Re: AI Engineer application", date: "2026-01-12T10:00:00.000Z", opened: "2026-01-12T10:00:00.000Z",
      }),
    ],
    triage: [
      { id: "2026-01-14-your-application-e1e2e3", ref: ".careeros/mail-triage/2026-01-14-your-application-e1e2e3.json", from: { name: "Example Labs People", address: at("people", "examplelabs-mail.example.org") }, subject: "Your application", date: "2026-01-14T08:00:00.000Z", suggestion: { company_slug: LABS.slug, company_name: LABS.company, reason: "The subject names Example Labs." } },
      { id: "2026-01-14-new-jobs-for-you-f1f2f3", ref: ".careeros/mail-triage/2026-01-14-new-jobs-for-you-f1f2f3.json", from: { name: "Job alerts", address: at("alerts", "jobs.example.net") }, subject: "New jobs for you", date: "2026-01-14T07:00:00.000Z", suggestion: null },
    ],
    drafts: [{
      draft_id: "20260114t120000-aaaaaa", slug: LABS.slug, id: LABS.id, status: "draft", updated: "2026-01-14T12:00:00.000Z", approval_id: "", note: "",
      input: { to: at("hiring", "examplelabs.example.net"), cc: "", subject: "Following up", body: "Hi,\n\nI wanted to follow up on my application.\n\nRegards,\nAlex", attach: [], in_reply_to: "", references: [], purpose: "" },
    }],
  };
}

function parseList(s: string): MailAddressView[] {
  return s.split(",").map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean).map((x) => {
    const m = x.match(/^(.*)<([^>]+)>$/);
    const name = m ? m[1]!.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, "") : "";
    const address = (m ? m[2]! : x).trim();
    if (!EMAIL.test(address)) throw new Error(`"${address.slice(0, 80)}" isn't an email address.`);
    return { name, address };
  });
}

export function createMockMail(h: MockMailHooks): MockMail {
  const s = seed();
  let connected = h.connected;
  let address = connected ? ALEX : "";
  let displayName = "Alex Example";
  let signature = "Alex Example\nAI Engineer";
  let staged = "";
  let lastSync = connected ? "2026-01-15T08:45:00.000Z" : "";
  let lastError = "";
  let background = { on: false, minutes: 60 };
  let seq = 0;
  const ignored: string[] = [];

  const status = (): MailStatus => ({
    available: true, connected, address, display_name: displayName, signature, password_set: connected, secure_storage: true, syncing: false,
    last_sync: lastSync, last_ok: lastSync, next_sync: connected ? "2026-01-15T09:15:00.000Z" : "", last_error: lastError,
    counts: { stored: 2, triaged: 2, skipped: 14, duplicates: 0, too_large: 0 }, purged: 0, sync_minutes: 15, first_sync_days: 30,
    send_limit_per_hour: 10, cloud_folder: "", raw_bytes: s.messages.filter((m) => m.view.has_raw).length * 24_000, triage_count: s.triage.length,
  });
  const row = (v: MailMessageView): MailRow => ({
    ref: v.ref, company_slug: v.company_slug, company_name: v.company_name, job_id: v.job_id, job_title: v.job_title, direction: v.direction,
    date: v.date, subject: v.subject, from: v.from, thread_key: v.thread_key, attachments: v.attachments.length, opened: !!v.opened_at, matched_by: "recruiter",
  });
  const find = (ref: string): Msg => {
    const m = s.messages.find((x) => x.view.ref === ref);
    if (!m) throw new Error("This email can't be found. It may have been moved or deleted.");
    return m;
  };
  const rows = () => s.messages.map((m) => row(m.view)).sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date));
  const list = (q: MailQuery): MailList => {
    const t = q.text?.toLowerCase();
    const r = rows().filter((x) => (!q.company_slug || x.company_slug === q.company_slug) && (!q.id || x.job_id === q.id || (!!q.includeCompany && !x.job_id))
      && (!q.direction || x.direction === q.direction) && (!q.unread || (!x.opened && x.direction === "in")) && (!q.attachments || x.attachments > 0)
      && (!t || [x.subject, x.from.name, x.from.address, x.company_name].some((y) => y.toLowerCase().includes(t))));
    return { rows: r.slice(q.offset ?? 0, (q.offset ?? 0) + (q.limit ?? 200)), total: r.length };
  };
  const draftView = (d: Draft): DraftView => {
    const j = h.jobName(d.slug, d.id);
    return {
      draft_id: d.draft_id, company_slug: d.slug, id: d.id, company_name: j.company, job_title: j.title, rel: `companies/${d.slug}/jobs/${d.id}/emails/drafts/${d.draft_id}.md`,
      subject: d.input.subject, to: d.input.to, status: d.status, updated: d.updated, approval_id: d.approval_id, note: d.note,
    };
  };
  const findDraft = (slug: string, id: string, draftId: string): Draft => {
    const d = s.drafts.find((x) => x.slug === slug && x.id === id && x.draft_id === draftId);
    if (!d) throw new Error(`The draft ${draftId} can't be found.`);
    return d;
  };
  const preview = (d: Draft, current = true): EmailPreview => {
    const j = h.jobName(d.slug, d.id);
    const sig = signature.trim();
    const body = d.input.body.replace(/\s+$/, "");
    return {
      account: address, from: { name: displayName, address }, to: parseList(d.input.to), cc: parseList(d.input.cc), subject: d.input.subject,
      text: sig ? `${body}\n\n${sig}\n` : `${body}\n`, in_reply_to: d.input.in_reply_to,
      attachments: d.input.attach.map((rel) => ({ rel, name: rel.split("/").pop() ?? rel, size: 2048, type: "application/pdf", locked: rel.startsWith("me/identity/") ? "me/identity" : null })),
      locked: d.input.attach.some((rel) => rel.startsWith("me/identity/")), current, problem: current ? "" : "The draft changed. Review it again.",
      job: { company_slug: d.slug, id: d.id, title: j.title, company_name: j.company },
    };
  };
  const detail = (d: Draft): DraftDetail => ({ view: draftView(d), draft: { ...d.input }, preview: d.status === "sent" ? null : preview(d) });
  const approvals: ApprovalView[] = [];
  const triageLeft = () => s.triage.filter((t) => !ignored.some((x) => t.from.address.toLowerCase() === x || t.from.address.toLowerCase().endsWith(`@${x}`)));
  const background_ = (): BackgroundStatus => ({ supported: true, on: background.on, minutes: background.minutes, installed: background.on, detail: background.on ? "Scheduled (mock)." : "Not scheduled.", log: [] });
  const requireConnected = () => { if (!connected) throw new Error("Connect Gmail in Settings > Email first."); };

  const api: CareerosBridge["mail"] = {
    status: () => h.rec("mail.status", [], status),
    testConnection: (addr: string, name: string, password: string) => h.rec("mail.testConnection", [addr, name, "<redacted>"], (): MailTestResult => {
      if (!EMAIL.test(addr.trim())) throw new Error(`"${addr.slice(0, 80)}" isn't an email address.`);
      const pw = password.replace(/\s+/g, "");
      if (!/^[A-Za-z]{16}$/.test(pw)) throw new Error("An app password is 16 letters. Copy it again from Google's App Passwords page.");
      const ok = pw.toLowerCase() === MOCK_GOOD_PASSWORD;
      staged = ok ? addr.trim().toLowerCase() : "";
      const fail = { ok: false, message: "Gmail didn't accept the address and app password.", fix: "Check the address, then create a new app password and paste all 16 letters." };
      return {
        ok,
        checks: ok
          ? [{ id: "imap", label: "Sign in to Gmail", ok: true, message: "Signed in to Gmail.", fix: "" }, { id: "sent", label: "Find the Sent folder", ok: true, message: "Found your Sent folder ([Gmail]/Sent Mail).", fix: "" }, { id: "smtp", label: "Sign in to send", ok: true, message: "Gmail will accept mail you send from CareerOS. Nothing was sent.", fix: "" }]
          : [{ id: "imap", label: "Sign in to Gmail", ...fail }, { id: "sent", label: "Find the Sent folder", ok: false, message: "Not checked, because signing in failed.", fix: "" }, { id: "smtp", label: "Sign in to send", ...fail }],
      };
    }),
    saveAccount: (addr: string, name: string) => h.rec("mail.saveAccount", [addr, name], () => {
      if (!staged || staged !== addr.trim().toLowerCase()) throw new Error("Test the connection first. CareerOS saves the account only after a passing test.");
      staged = ""; connected = true; address = addr.trim(); displayName = name.trim(); lastError = "";
      h.emit("mail", "settings", "home");
      return status();
    }),
    disconnect: () => h.rec("mail.disconnect", [], () => { connected = false; address = ""; background = { ...background, on: false }; h.emit("mail", "settings", "home"); return status(); }),
    setSignature: (text: string) => h.rec("mail.setSignature", [text], () => { signature = text.replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n").replace(/\s+$/, ""); h.emit("mail", "settings"); return status(); }),
    syncNow: () => h.rec("mail.syncNow", [], () => { requireConnected(); lastSync = h.now(); h.emit("mail", "jobs", "home"); return status(); }),
    list: (q: MailQuery) => h.rec("mail.list", [q], () => list(q)),
    get: (ref: string) => h.rec("mail.get", [ref], () => {
      const m = find(ref);
      if (!m.view.opened_at && m.view.direction === "in") { m.view.opened_at = h.now(); h.emit("home"); }
      return m.view;
    }),
    loadImages: (ref: string) => h.rec("mail.loadImages", [ref], () => { const m = find(ref); m.view.images_allowed = true; h.emit("mail"); return m.view; }),
    saveAttachment: (ref: string, part: string) => h.rec("mail.saveAttachment", [ref, part], () => {
      const m = find(ref);
      const a = m.view.attachments.find((x) => x.part === part);
      if (!a) throw new Error("That attachment isn't in this email.");
      const dir = ref.slice(0, ref.lastIndexOf("/"));
      a.saved_rel = `${dir}/attachments/${a.name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9.]+/g, "-")}`;
      h.emit("mail", "jobs");
      return m.view;
    }),
    move: (ref: string, target: MailTarget) => h.rec("mail.move", [ref, target], () => {
      const m = find(ref);
      const j = h.jobName(target.company_slug, target.id ?? "");
      const base = ref.slice(ref.lastIndexOf("/") + 1, -5);
      Object.assign(m.view, { ref: refFor(target.company_slug, target.id ?? "", base), company_slug: target.company_slug, job_id: target.id ?? "", company_name: j.company, job_title: target.id ? j.title : "" });
      if (target.id) h.event(target.company_slug, target.id, "Email received", m.view.subject);
      h.emit("mail", "jobs", "home");
      return m.view;
    }),
    prune: (months: number) => h.rec("mail.prune", [months], () => {
      const cutoff = new Date(new Date(h.now()).getTime() - months * 30 * 86_400_000).toISOString();
      const old = s.messages.filter((m) => m.view.has_raw && m.view.date < cutoff);
      for (const m of old) m.view.has_raw = false;
      h.emit("mail");
      return { removed: old.length, bytes: old.length * 24_000 };
    }),
    triage: {
      list: () => h.rec("mail.triage.list", [], triageLeft),
      assign: (id: string, target: MailTarget, addMatch: boolean) => h.rec("mail.triage.assign", [id, target, addMatch], () => {
        const i = s.triage.findIndex((t) => t.id === id);
        if (i < 0) throw new Error("This email can't be found. It may have been moved or deleted.");
        const [t] = s.triage.splice(i, 1);
        const j = h.jobName(target.company_slug, target.id ?? "");
        const m = message({ base: t!.id, job: { slug: target.company_slug, id: target.id ?? "", company: j.company, title: target.id ? j.title : "" }, from: t!.from, subject: t!.subject, date: t!.date });
        s.messages.push(m);
        if (target.id) h.event(target.company_slug, target.id, "Email received", t!.subject);
        h.emit("mail", "jobs", "home");
        return triageLeft();
      }),
      createCompany: (id: string, name: string, jobTitle: string) => h.rec("mail.triage.createCompany", [id, name, jobTitle], () => {
        const i = s.triage.findIndex((t) => t.id === id);
        if (i < 0) throw new Error("This email can't be found. It may have been moved or deleted.");
        s.triage.splice(i, 1);
        h.emit("mail", "jobs", "home", "sponsors");
        return triageLeft();
      }),
      ignore: (id: string, scope: "sender" | "domain") => h.rec("mail.triage.ignore", [id, scope], () => {
        const t = s.triage.find((x) => x.id === id);
        if (!t) throw new Error("This email can't be found. It may have been moved or deleted.");
        const a = t.from.address.toLowerCase();
        ignored.push(scope === "sender" ? a : a.slice(a.indexOf("@") + 1));
        s.triage = triageLeft();
        h.emit("mail");
        return triageLeft();
      }),
    },
    drafts: {
      all: () => h.rec("mail.drafts.all", [], () => s.drafts.map(draftView).sort((a, b) => b.updated.localeCompare(a.updated))),
      list: (slug: string, id: string) => h.rec("mail.drafts.list", [slug, id], () => s.drafts.filter((d) => d.slug === slug && d.id === id).map(draftView)),
      get: (slug: string, id: string, draftId: string) => h.rec("mail.drafts.get", [slug, id, draftId], () => detail(findDraft(slug, id, draftId))),
      save: (slug: string, id: string, draftId: string, input: DraftInput) => h.rec("mail.drafts.save", [slug, id, draftId, input], () => {
        parseList(input.to); parseList(input.cc);
        if (draftId) {
          const d = findDraft(slug, id, draftId);
          if (d.status === "sent") throw new Error("This email was already sent. Start a new draft.");
          Object.assign(d, { input: { ...input }, updated: h.now() });
          return detail(d);
        }
        const d: Draft = { draft_id: `20260115t090000-mock${String(++seq).padStart(2, "0")}`, slug, id, input: { ...input }, status: "draft", updated: h.now(), approval_id: "", note: "" };
        s.drafts.push(d);
        h.emit("mail");
        return detail(d);
      }),
      propose: (slug: string, id: string, draftId: string) => h.rec("mail.drafts.propose", [slug, id, draftId], () => {
        requireConnected();
        const d = findDraft(slug, id, draftId);
        const p = preview(d);
        const people = [...p.to, ...p.cc].map((x) => x.address);
        const a: ApprovalView = {
          id: `20260115t090000-mail${String(++seq).padStart(2, "0")}`, action: "email_send", title: `Send "${d.input.subject}"`, summary: `To ${people.join(", ")}`,
          steps: [`Send one email from ${address} to ${people.length} recipient(s)`, "Save the sent copy in the job's emails folder", 'Add "Email sent" to the job timeline'],
          payload: { company_slug: slug, id, draft_id: draftId, purpose: d.input.purpose }, created_at: h.now(), created_by: "app", status: "pending", decided_at: "", result_note: "",
          email: p,
        };
        approvals.push(a);
        d.status = "waiting"; d.approval_id = a.id;
        h.emit("mail", "approvals", "home");
        return a;
      }),
    },
    background: {
      status: () => h.rec("mail.background.status", [], background_),
      set: (on: boolean, minutes: number) => h.rec("mail.background.set", [on, minutes], () => { if (on) requireConnected(); background = { on, minutes }; h.emit("mail"); return background_(); }),
    },
  };

  return {
    api,
    /** The approvals this mock created; MockBridge merges them into approvals.list. */
    get approvals() { return approvals; },
    nextUp: () => {
      const unread = rows().filter((r) => r.direction === "in" && !r.opened);
      return { unread: unread.slice(0, 10), unreadCount: unread.length, drafts: s.drafts.map(draftView) };
    },
    withEmail: (a) => {
      if (a.action !== "email_send" || a.status !== "pending") return a;
      const d = s.drafts.find((x) => x.approval_id === a.id);
      return d ? { ...a, email: preview(d) } : a;
    },
    sendApproved: (a) => {
      const d = s.drafts.find((x) => x.approval_id === a.id);
      if (!d) throw new Error("The draft can't be found.");
      const p = preview(d);
      const j = h.jobName(d.slug, d.id);
      s.messages.push(message({ base: `${d.draft_id}-sent`, job: { slug: d.slug, id: d.id, company: j.company, title: j.title }, direction: "out", from: p.from, subject: p.subject, date: h.now(), opened: h.now() }));
      d.status = "sent";
      h.event(d.slug, d.id, "Email sent", p.subject);
      const n = p.to.length + p.cc.length;
      return `Sent to ${n} recipient${n === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
    },
    applyEmail: (slug, id) => {
      const v = h.jobName(slug, id).apply_via.replace(/^mailto:/i, "");
      return EMAIL.test(v) ? v : "";
    },
  } as MockMail & { approvals: ApprovalView[] };
}
```

## Group D: screens (`@careeros/app-ui`, spec §4)

### Task 19: Settings > Connections > Email (wizard, test, save, signature, background sync)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-ui/src/components/MailSetup.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/test/mail-setup.test.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Settings.tsx` (Email card; `SecretsPanel` drops the Gmail password), `packages/app-ui/test/settings.test.tsx` (two secrets tests), `packages/app-ui/src/styles/app.css`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `mail.status/testConnection/saveAccount/disconnect/setSignature/syncNow/background.status/background.set`, `secrets.set`, `links.open` (bridge); `useLoad`, `useAction`, `errorText`; brand `Button`, `Card`, `Dialog`, `Input`, `Pill`, `Select`, `Text`, `Textarea`.
- Produces: `MailSetup()`, `GOOGLE_LINKS` (`twoStep`, `appPasswords`, `imap`), `formatWhen(iso: string, now?: Date): string` (exported from `MailSetup.tsx`, reused by the Mail screen in Task 20).

Rulings: the password field is cleared as soon as the test finishes, pass or fail, so the renderer holds it for one call only. "Save and connect" appears only after a passing test and saves the tested address; changing the address after a test hides it again. The wizard's links go through `links.open`, which shows the full address before opening the browser. Replacing the password lives here, not in the generic secrets list.

`packages/app-ui/test/mail-setup.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MOCK_GOOD_PASSWORD } from "../src/mock/mail-mock";
import { MockBridge } from "../src/mock/MockBridge";
import { SettingsScreen } from "../src/screens/Settings";
import { renderApp } from "./render";

const ADDRESS = "alex" + "@" + "example.com";

async function openEmail(bridge: MockBridge) {
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  renderApp(<SettingsScreen />, { bridge });
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("tab", { name: "Connections" }));
  return { user, card: await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Email" }) };
}

describe("Email setup", () => {
  it("walks through the steps, tests, then saves only after a pass", async () => {
    const bridge = new MockBridge({ mailConnected: false });
    const { user, card } = await openEmail(bridge);
    await user.click(within(card).getByRole("button", { name: "Open Google's App Passwords page" }));
    expect(bridge.opened).toEqual(["https://myaccount.google.com/apppasswords"]);
    await user.type(within(card).getByLabelText("Gmail address"), ADDRESS);
    await user.type(within(card).getByLabelText("Your name, as people will see it"), "Alex Example");
    const pw = within(card).getByLabelText("App password (16 letters)");
    expect(pw).toHaveAttribute("type", "password");
    expect(within(card).queryByRole("button", { name: "Save and connect" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.type(pw, MOCK_GOOD_PASSWORD);
    await user.click(within(card).getByRole("button", { name: "Test connection" }));
    const results = await within(card).findByRole("list", { name: "Connection test" });
    expect(within(results).getAllByText("Passed")).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(within(results).getByText("Gmail will accept mail you send from CareerOS. Nothing was sent.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(pw).toHaveValue("");
    await user.click(within(card).getByRole("button", { name: "Save and connect" }));
    expect(await within(card).findByText(`Connected as ${ADDRESS}`)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(card).queryByLabelText("App password (16 letters)")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(JSON.stringify(bridge.calls)).not.toContain(MOCK_GOOD_PASSWORD);
  });
  it("shows each failed check with its fix and offers no save", async () => {
    const { user, card } = await openEmail(new MockBridge({ mailConnected: false }));
    await user.type(within(card).getByLabelText("Gmail address"), ADDRESS);
    await user.type(within(card).getByLabelText("App password (16 letters)"), "zzzz yyyy xxxx wwww");
    await user.click(within(card).getByRole("button", { name: "Test connection" }));
    const results = await within(card).findByRole("list", { name: "Connection test" });
    expect(within(results).getAllByText("Gmail didn't accept the address and app password.")).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(within(results).getAllByText(/paste all 16 letters/)).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(within(card).queryByRole("button", { name: "Save and connect" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it("explains a password that isn't an app password, without contacting Gmail", async () => {
    const bridge = new MockBridge({ mailConnected: false });
    const { user, card } = await openEmail(bridge);
    await user.type(within(card).getByLabelText("Gmail address"), ADDRESS);
    await user.type(within(card).getByLabelText("App password (16 letters)"), "my normal password");
    await user.click(within(card).getByRole("button", { name: "Test connection" }));
    expect(await within(card).findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("An app password is 16 letters.");
  });
  it("hides Save again when the address changes after a test", async () => {
    const { user, card } = await openEmail(new MockBridge({ mailConnected: false }));
    const address = within(card).getByLabelText("Gmail address");
    await user.type(address, ADDRESS);
    await user.type(within(card).getByLabelText("App password (16 letters)"), MOCK_GOOD_PASSWORD);
    await user.click(within(card).getByRole("button", { name: "Test connection" }));
    await within(card).findByRole("button", { name: "Save and connect" });
    await user.type(address, "x");
    expect(within(card).queryByRole("button", { name: "Save and connect" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it("edits the signature with a preview, and turns background sync on with the OS status", async () => {
    const bridge = new MockBridge();
    const { user, card } = await openEmail(bridge);
    const sig = await within(card).findByLabelText("Signature");
    await user.clear(sig);
    await user.type(sig, "Alex Example{Enter}Senior AI Engineer");
    expect(within(card).getByLabelText("Signature preview")).toHaveTextContent("Senior AI Engineer");
    await user.click(within(card).getByRole("button", { name: "Save signature" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(bridge.calls.find((c) => c.method === "mail.setSignature")?.args).toEqual(["Alex Example\nSenior AI Engineer"]));
    await user.click(within(card).getByRole("switch", { name: "Sync in the background" }));
    expect(await within(card).findByText("Scheduled (mock).")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(bridge.calls.find((c) => c.method === "mail.background.set")?.args).toEqual([true, 60]);
  });
  it("disconnects after a confirmation", async () => {
    const bridge = new MockBridge();
    const { user, card } = await openEmail(bridge);
    await user.click(await within(card).findByRole("button", { name: "Disconnect" }));
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole("dialog")).getByRole("button", { name: "Disconnect Gmail" }));
    expect(await within(card).findByLabelText("Gmail address")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```
