- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/app-ui/src/components/MailSetup.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button, Card, Dialog, Input, Pill, Select, Text, Textarea } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { MailStatus, MailTestResult } from "@careeros/contract";
import { errorText, useAction, useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { Loading } from "./States";

export const GOOGLE_LINKS = {
  twoStep: "https://myaccount.google.com/signinoptions/twosv",
  appPasswords: "https://myaccount.google.com/apppasswords",
  imap: "https://mail.google.com/mail/u/0/#settings/fwdandpop",
} as const;
const MINUTES = [15, 30, 60, 120, 240, 720, 1440];

export function formatWhen(iso: string, now = new Date()): string {
  if (!iso) return "never";
  const t = new Date(iso);
  const mins = Math.round((now.getTime() - t.getTime()) / 60_000);
  if (mins >= 0 && mins < 1) return "just now";
  if (mins >= 1 && mins < 60) return `${mins} min ago`;
  if (mins < 0 && mins > -60) return `in ${-mins} min`;
  return t.toLocaleString(undefined, { day: "numeric", month: "short", hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit" });
}

function Wizard({ onSaved }: { onSaved: () => void }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const [address, setAddress] = useState("");
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [result, setResult] = useState<MailTestResult | null>(null);
  const [tested, setTested] = useState("");
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState("");

  const test = async () => {
    setProblem("");
    setResult(null);
    const pw = password;
    setPassword("");
    try {
      const r = await bridge.mail.testConnection(address.trim(), name.trim(), pw);
      setResult(r);
      setTested(r.ok ? address.trim() : "");
    } catch (e) { setProblem(errorText(e)); setTested(""); }
  };
  const save = () => run("save", async () => { await bridge.mail.saveAccount(address.trim(), name.trim()); onSaved(); }, "Gmail connected");
  const canSave = !!result?.ok && tested === address.trim();

  return (
    <div className="stack">
      <ol className="wizard-steps">
        <li>
          <Text role="body">Turn on 2-Step Verification for your Google account.</Text>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => void bridge.links.open(GOOGLE_LINKS.twoStep)}>Open Google's 2-Step Verification page</Button>
        </li>
        <li>
          <Text role="body">Create an app password named CareerOS. Google shows 16 letters once. Copy them.</Text>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => void bridge.links.open(GOOGLE_LINKS.appPasswords)}>Open Google's App Passwords page</Button>
        </li>
        <li>
          <Text role="body">Check that IMAP is on in Gmail's settings.</Text>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => void bridge.links.open(GOOGLE_LINKS.imap)}>Open Gmail's IMAP settings</Button>
        </li>
      </ol>
      <form className="stack" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); void test(); }}>
        <Input label="Gmail address" type="email" autoComplete="off" spellCheck={false} value={address} onChange={(e) => { setAddress(e.target.value); setResult(null); }} />
        <Input label="Your name, as people will see it" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
        <Input label="App password (16 letters)" type="password" autoComplete="off" spellCheck={false} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          help="CareerOS keeps it in your computer's secure storage and never shows it again." />
        {problem ? <p role="alert" className="field__error">{problem}</p> : null}
        <div className="row">
          <Button type="submit" variant={canSave ? "secondary" : "primary"} disabled={!address.trim() || !password}>Test connection</Button>
          {canSave ? <Button variant="primary" loading={busy === "save"} onClick={() => void save()}>Save and connect</Button> : null}
        </div>
      </form>
      {result ? (
        <ul className="list-plain" aria-label="Connection test">
          {result.checks.map((c) => (
            <li key={c.id} className="check-line">
              <Pill tone={c.ok ? "success" : "danger"}>{c.ok ? "Passed" : "Failed"}</Pill>
              <div>
                <Text role="label">{c.label}</Text>
                <Text role="body-sm">{c.message}</Text>
                {c.fix ? <Text role="body-sm" muted>{c.fix}</Text> : null}
              </div>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

function Connected({ status, reload }: { status: MailStatus; reload: () => void }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const [signature, setSignature] = useState(status.signature);
  const [replacement, setReplacement] = useState("");
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(false);
  const bg = useLoad((b) => b.mail.background.status(), [], ["mail"]);

  const replace = () => run("replace", async () => { const v = replacement; setReplacement(""); await bridge.secrets.set("gmail_app_password", v); reload(); }, "Password replaced");
  const disconnect = () => run("disconnect", async () => { setConfirm(false); await bridge.mail.disconnect(); reload(); }, "Gmail disconnected");
  const saveSignature = () => run("signature", async () => { await bridge.mail.setSignature(signature); reload(); }, "Signature saved");
  const setBackground = (on: boolean, minutes: number) => run("background", async () => { await bridge.mail.background.set(on, minutes); bg.reload(); });

  return (
    <div className="stack">
      <div className="row row--between">
        <div>
          <Text role="heading-2">Connected as {status.address}</Text>
          <Text role="body-sm" muted>Last sync {formatWhen(status.last_sync)}{status.last_error ? "" : `. ${status.counts.stored} new job emails, ${status.counts.triaged} to sort.`}</Text>
        </div>
        <div className="row">
          <Button loading={busy === "sync"} onClick={() => void run("sync", async () => { await bridge.mail.syncNow(); reload(); }, "Mail synced")}>Sync now</Button>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setConfirm(true)}>Disconnect</Button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {status.last_error ? <Text role="body-sm" className="field__error">{status.last_error}</Text> : null}
      {status.cloud_folder ? (
        <Text role="body-sm" muted>Your workspace is in {status.cloud_folder}. Saved emails and attachments sync there too, so treat that account as private.</Text>
      ) : null}

      <div className="row row--top">
        <div className="grow">
          <Input label="Replace the app password" type="password" autoComplete="off" spellCheck={false} value={replacement} onChange={(e) => setReplacement(e.target.value)}
            help="CareerOS tests the new password first and keeps the old one if it fails." />
        </div>
        <Button disabled={!replacement} loading={busy === "replace"} onClick={() => void replace()}>Replace password</Button>
      </div>

      <div className="split">
        <Textarea label="Signature" rows={4} value={signature} onChange={(e) => setSignature(e.target.value)} help="Added to the end of every email you send from CareerOS." />
        <div className="field">
          <span className="field__label">Preview</span>
          <pre className="mail-preview" aria-label="Signature preview">{`Hi,\n\n…\n\n${signature.trim()}`}</pre>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div><Button disabled={signature === status.signature} loading={busy === "signature"} onClick={() => void saveSignature()}>Save signature</Button></div>

      {bg.data ? (
        <div className="stack">
          <div className="row">
            <button type="button" role="switch" className="switch" aria-checked={bg.data.on} aria-label="Sync in the background" disabled={!bg.data.supported || busy === "background"}
              onClick={() => void setBackground(!bg.data!.on, bg.data!.minutes)} />
            <Text role="body">Sync in the background, even when CareerOS is closed</Text>
            <Select label="Every" hideLabel value={String(bg.data.minutes)} disabled={!bg.data.supported}
              onChange={(e) => void setBackground(bg.data!.on, Number(e.target.value))}
              options={MINUTES.map((m) => ({ value: String(m), label: m < 60 ? `Every ${m} minutes` : m === 60 ? "Every hour" : `Every ${m / 60} hours` }))} />
          </div>
          <Text role="body-sm" muted>{bg.data.detail}</Text>
          {bg.data.log.length ? <pre className="mail-preview" aria-label="Background sync log">{bg.data.log.join("\n")}</pre> : null}
        </div>
      ) : <Loading />}

      <Dialog open={confirm} onClose={() => setConfirm(false)} title="Disconnect Gmail?"
        actions={<><Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setConfirm(false)}>Cancel</Button><Button variant="danger" loading={busy === "disconnect"} onClick={() => void disconnect()}>Disconnect Gmail</Button></>}>
        <Text role="body">CareerOS removes the app password and stops syncing. Emails already saved in your workspace stay. To cut access completely, also delete the app password on Google's App Passwords page.</Text>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
}

export function MailSetup() {
  const status = useLoad((b) => b.mail.status(), [], ["mail", "settings"]);
  const s = status.data;
  return (
    <Card className="stack" aria-label="Email">
      <Text role="heading-2">Email</Text>
      {!s ? <Loading /> : !s.available ? (
        <Text role="body" muted>Email isn't available in this version of the app.</Text>
      ) : !s.secure_storage ? (
        <Text role="body">Secure storage isn't available on this computer, so Gmail can't be connected. CareerOS never keeps the password in a plain file.</Text>
      ) : s.connected ? <Connected status={s} reload={status.reload} /> : <Wizard onSaved={status.reload} />}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

The problem line is a plain paragraph with `role="alert"` because `Text` uses its `role` prop for typography. `Button` has a `"danger"` variant in the brand set (3a Task 11). `Card` passes `aria-label` through to its `section`, which gives it the `region` role.

### Task 20: Mail screen, message viewer, nav item and the mail status footer

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-ui/src/components/MessageViewer.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Mail.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/test/mail-screen.test.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/app-ui/src/router.ts` (`mail` screen), `packages/app-ui/src/screens/registry.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/Shell.tsx` (nav item, command, footer), `packages/app-ui/test/shell.test.tsx` (footer line), `packages/app-ui/src/styles/app.css`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `mail.status/syncNow/list/get/loadImages/saveAttachment/move/prune`, `mail.triage.*`, `mail.drafts.all`, `mailUrl`, `files.reveal`, `jobs.list` (bridge); `formatWhen` (Task 19); `fmtDateTime`, `bytes`, `plural` (3a `format.ts`).
- Produces:
  - `MessageViewer({ mailRef, onReply?, showMove? }: { mailRef: string; onReply?: (v: MailMessageView) => void; showMove?: boolean })`
  - `useJobTargets(): { company_slug: string; company_name: string; id: string; title: string }[]` (exported from `MessageViewer.tsx`; reused by Task 21)
  - `MailScreen()`; route `{ screen: "mail" }` (`#/mail`)

Rulings: the body is shown only inside `<iframe sandbox="">` loaded from `bridge.mailUrl(ref)`: no scripts, no same-origin access, no forms, no top navigation, and the main-process CSP on that response blocks every network fetch. The header outside the frame always shows the real address next to the display name. A risky attachment (programs, scripts, disk images, macro documents) gets a plain warning; saving it is still allowed, opening it is the person's call. The frame is re-created (`key`) when images are allowed, so it reloads with the image URLs.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`packages/app-ui/test/mail-screen.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { mailUrlFor } from "@careeros/contract";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MockBridge } from "../src/mock/MockBridge";
import { MailScreen } from "../src/screens/Mail";
import { renderApp } from "./render";

const INTERVIEW = "companies/acme-corp/jobs/2026-01-12-ai-engineer/emails/2026-01-14-interview-for-ai-engineer-a1b2c3.json";

describe("Mail screen", () => {
  it("lists job mail newest first and filters it", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderApp(<MailScreen />);
    const list = await screen.findByRole("list", { name: "Job mail" });
    expect(within(list).getAllByRole("button").map((b) => b.querySelector(".mail-row__subject")?.textContent)).toEqual([
      "Interview for AI Engineer", "Urgent: confirm your details", "Re: AI Engineer application",
    ]);
    await user.click(screen.getByLabelText("Unread only"));
    await waitFor(() => expect(within(screen.getByRole("list", { name: "Job mail" })).getAllByRole("button")).toHaveLength(2));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Search mail"), "urgent");
    await waitFor(() => expect(within(screen.getByRole("list", { name: "Job mail" })).getAllByRole("button")).toHaveLength(1));
  });

  it("opens a message in a sandboxed frame with images blocked until asked", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const { bridge } = renderApp(<MailScreen />);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: /Interview for AI Engineer/ }));
    const viewer = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Message" });
    const frame = within(viewer).getByTitle("Email body");
    expect(frame).toHaveAttribute("sandbox", "");
    expect(frame).toHaveAttribute("src", mailUrlFor(INTERVIEW));
    expect(within(viewer).getByText("Acme Talent")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(viewer).getByText("talent" + "@" + "example.org")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(viewer).getByText("Images are blocked to protect your privacy.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(within(viewer).getByRole("button", { name: "Load images" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(within(viewer).queryByText("Images are blocked to protect your privacy.")).not.toBeInTheDocument());
    expect(bridge.calls.some((c) => c.method === "mail.loadImages")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("warns about a sender whose name claims another domain", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderApp(<MailScreen />);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: /Urgent: confirm your details/ }));
    const viewer = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Message" });
    const alerts = within(viewer).getAllByRole("alert");
    expect(alerts.map((a) => a.textContent)).toEqual([
      "Gmail couldn't confirm this email really came from evil.example.net. Be careful with its links and attachments.",
      `The sender's name shows examplelabs.example.net, but the email came from ${"x" + "@" + "evil.example.net"}.`,
    ]);
  });

  it("saves an attachment to the job folder, then shows it, and flags risky files", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const { bridge } = renderApp(<MailScreen />);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: /Interview for AI Engineer/ }));
    const chip = await screen.findByRole("group", { name: "Interview details.pdf" });
    await user.click(within(chip).getByRole("button", { name: "Save to job folder" }));
    await user.click(await within(chip).findByRole("button", { name: "Show in folder" }));
    expect(bridge.revealed).toEqual(["companies/acme-corp/jobs/2026-01-12-ai-engineer/emails/attachments/interview-details.pdf"]);
    expect(within(screen.getByRole("group", { name: "setup.exe" })).getByText(/can run programs/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("sorts mail: assigns with always-match, or ignores a domain", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const { bridge } = renderApp(<MailScreen />);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("tab", { name: /Mail to sort/ }));
    const item = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Your application" });
    expect(within(item).getByText("The subject names Example Labs.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.selectOptions(within(item).getByLabelText("File under"), "example-labs/2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm");
    await user.click(within(item).getByLabelText("Always match this sender"));
    await user.click(within(item).getByRole("button", { name: "Assign" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("region", { name: "Your application" })).not.toBeInTheDocument());
    expect(bridge.calls.find((c) => c.method === "mail.triage.assign")?.args).toEqual(["2026-01-14-your-application-e1e2e3", { company_slug: "example-labs", id: "2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm" }, true]);
    const other = screen.getByRole("region", { name: "New jobs for you" });
    await user.click(within(other).getByRole("button", { name: "Ignore domain" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("Nothing to sort.")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("lists drafts with their status and opens one on its job", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderApp(<MailScreen />);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("tab", { name: /Drafts/ }));
    const row = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Following up" });
    expect(within(row).getByText("Draft")).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(within(row).getByRole("button", { name: "Open" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe("#/job/example-labs/2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm?tab=mail"));
  });

  it("shows sync times, syncs on request and removes old originals", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const { bridge } = renderApp(<MailScreen />);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Sync now" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(bridge.calls.some((c) => c.method === "mail.syncNow")).toBe(true));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove originals older than 6 months" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(bridge.calls.find((c) => c.method === "mail.prune")?.args).toEqual([6]));
  });

  it("sends people to Settings when Gmail isn't connected", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderApp(<MailScreen />, { bridge: new MockBridge({ mailConnected: false }) });
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Connect Gmail" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe("#/settings"));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/app-ui/src/components/MessageViewer.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { FolderOpen, ImageOff, Paperclip, Reply } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Pill, Select, Text } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { MailAddressView, MailMessageView } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useAction, useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { bytes, fmtDateTime } from "../format";
import { ErrorState, Loading } from "./States";

export interface JobTarget { company_slug: string; company_name: string; id: string; title: string }

export function useJobTargets(): JobTarget[] {
  const jobs = useLoad((b) => b.jobs.list({ showIneligible: true, limit: 500 }), [], ["jobs"]);
  return useMemo(() => (jobs.data?.rows ?? []).map((r) => ({ company_slug: r.company_slug, company_name: r.company_name, id: r.id, title: r.title })), [jobs.data]);
}

function Who({ label, list }: { label: string; list: MailAddressView[] }) {
  if (!list.length) return null;
  return (
    <div className="mail-head__line">
      <Text role="label">{label}</Text>
      <span>
        {list.map((a, i) => (
          <span key={`${a.address}-${i}`} className="mail-addr">
            {a.name ? <span className="mail-addr__name">{a.name}</span> : null}
            <span className="mail-addr__address t-numeric">{a.address}</span>
          </span>
        ))}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

export function MessageViewer({ mailRef, onReply, showMove }: { mailRef: string; onReply?: (v: MailMessageView) => void; showMove?: boolean }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  // A move gives the message a new ref; follow it until the parent opens a different message.
  const [moved, setMoved] = useState<{ from: string; to: string } | null>(null);
  const [moveTo, setMoveTo] = useState("");
  const ref = moved && moved.from === mailRef ? moved.to : mailRef;
  const msg = useLoad((b) => b.mail.get(ref), [ref], ["mail"]);
  const targets = useJobTargets();
  const v = msg.data;
  if (msg.error && !v) return <ErrorState title="This email can't be shown" text={msg.error} />;
  if (!v) return <Loading />;

  const sameCompany = targets.filter((t) => t.company_slug === v.company_slug);
  const loadImages = () => run("images", async () => { await bridge.mail.loadImages(v.ref); msg.reload(); });
  const save = (part: string) => run(`save-${part}`, async () => { await bridge.mail.saveAttachment(v.ref, part); msg.reload(); }, "Saved to the job folder");
  const move = () => run("move", async () => {
    const r = await bridge.mail.move(v.ref, moveTo ? { company_slug: v.company_slug, id: moveTo } : { company_slug: v.company_slug });
    setMoved({ from: mailRef, to: r.ref });
  }, "Moved");

  return (
    <Card className="stack mail-viewer" aria-label="Message">
      <div className="row row--between">
        <Text role="heading-2">{v.subject || "(no subject)"}</Text>
        {onReply && v.direction === "in" ? <Button icon={Reply} onClick={() => onReply(v)}>Reply</Button> : null}
      </div>
      <div className="mail-head">
        <Who label="From" list={[v.from]} />
        <Who label="To" list={v.to} />
        <Who label="Cc" list={v.cc} />
        <Who label="Reply to" list={v.reply_to} />
        <div className="mail-head__line"><Text role="label">Date</Text><span>{fmtDateTime(v.date)}</span></div>
        <div className="mail-head__line"><Text role="label">Filed</Text><span>{v.triage ? "Mail to sort" : v.job_title ? `${v.job_title} · ${v.company_name}` : v.company_name}</span></div>
      </div>
      {v.warnings.map((w) => <p key={w} role="alert" className="mail-warning">{w}</p>)}
      {v.remote_images > 0 && !v.images_allowed ? (
        <div className="mail-images-bar row">
          <ImageOff aria-hidden="true" />
          <Text role="body-sm">Images are blocked to protect your privacy.</Text>
          <Button size="sm" loading={busy === "images"} onClick={() => void loadImages()}>Load images</Button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <iframe key={`${v.ref}-${v.images_allowed ? "img" : "noimg"}`} title="Email body" className="mail-frame" sandbox="" referrerPolicy="no-referrer" src={bridge.mailUrl(v.ref)} />
      {v.attachments.length ? (
        <div className="chip-group">
          {v.attachments.map((a) => (
            <div key={a.part} role="group" aria-label={a.name} className="mail-chip">
              <Paperclip aria-hidden="true" />
              <span className="truncate" title={a.name}>{a.name}</span>
              <Text role="body-sm" muted>{bytes(a.size)}</Text>
              {a.risky ? <Text role="body-sm" className="mail-warning">This kind of file can run programs. Only open it if you expected it.</Text> : null}
              {a.saved_rel ? (
                <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" icon={FolderOpen} onClick={() => void bridge.files.reveal(a.saved_rel)}>Show in folder</Button>
              ) : (
                <Button size="sm" disabled={v.triage || !v.has_raw} loading={busy === `save-${a.part}`} onClick={() => void save(a.part)}
                  title={v.triage ? "Assign this email to a job first." : !v.has_raw ? "The original email was removed to save space." : undefined}>Save to job folder</Button>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {showMove && !v.triage && sameCompany.length ? (
        <div className="row">
          <Select label="Move to" value={moveTo} onChange={(e) => setMoveTo(e.target.value)}
            options={[{ value: "", label: `${v.company_name} (company folder)` }, ...sameCompany.map((t) => ({ value: t.id, label: t.title }))]} />
          <Button loading={busy === "move"} disabled={moveTo === v.job_id} onClick={() => void move()}>Move to job</Button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {v.too_large ? <Pill tone="warning">Only the headers were kept</Pill> : null}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Mail.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Mail as MailIcon, RefreshCw, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Checkbox, Dialog, Input, Pill, Select, Tabs, Text, type PillTone } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { DraftStatus, MailQuery, TriageItem } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useAction, useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { formatWhen } from "../components/MailSetup";
import { MessageViewer, useJobTargets } from "../components/MessageViewer";
import { ErrorState, Loading, ScreenHead } from "../components/States";
import { bytes, fmtDateTime, plural } from "../format";
import { navigate } from "../router";

export const DRAFT_STATUS: Record<DraftStatus, [string, PillTone]> = {
  draft: ["Draft", "neutral"], waiting: ["Waiting for approval", "accent"], sent: ["Sent", "success"], failed: ["Failed", "danger"],
};

function AllMail() {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [company, setCompany] = useState("");
  const [job, setJob] = useState("");
  const [direction, setDirection] = useState("");
  const [unread, setUnread] = useState(false);
  const [attachments, setAttachments] = useState(false);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState("");
  const targets = useJobTargets();
  const q: MailQuery = {
    ...(text.trim() ? { text: text.trim() } : {}), ...(company ? { company_slug: company } : {}), ...(company && job ? { id: job } : {}),
    ...(direction ? { direction: direction as "in" | "out" } : {}), ...(unread ? { unread } : {}), ...(attachments ? { attachments } : {}),
  };
  const list = useLoad((b) => b.mail.list(q), [JSON.stringify(q)], ["mail"]);
  const status = useLoad((b) => b.mail.status(), [], ["mail"]);
  const companies = [...new Map(targets.map((t) => [t.company_slug, t.company_name])).entries()];

  return (
    <div className="stack">
      <div className="filters">
        <Input label="Search mail" value={text} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} />
        <Select label="Company" value={company} onChange={(e) => { setCompany(e.target.value); setJob(""); }} options={[{ value: "", label: "All companies" }, ...companies.map(([value, label]) => ({ value, label }))]} />
        <Select label="Job" value={job} disabled={!company} onChange={(e) => setJob(e.target.value)}
          options={[{ value: "", label: "All jobs" }, ...targets.filter((t) => t.company_slug === company).map((t) => ({ value: t.id, label: t.title }))]} />
        <Select label="Direction" value={direction} onChange={(e) => setDirection(e.target.value)} options={[{ value: "", label: "Received and sent" }, { value: "in", label: "Received" }, { value: "out", label: "Sent" }]} />
        <div className="stack">
          <Checkbox label="Unread only" checked={unread} onChange={(e) => setUnread(e.target.checked)} />
          <Checkbox label="Has attachments" checked={attachments} onChange={(e) => setAttachments(e.target.checked)} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="split">
        <div className="stack">
          {list.error && !list.data ? <ErrorState title="Mail can't load" text={list.error} /> : !list.data ? <Loading /> : list.data.rows.length === 0 ? (
            <Text role="body" muted>No job mail matches.</Text>
          ) : (
            <ul className="list-plain" aria-label="Job mail">
              {list.data.rows.map((r) => (
                <li key={r.ref}>
                  <button type="button" className={`mail-row${r.opened || r.direction === "out" ? "" : " is-unread"}${open === r.ref ? " is-current" : ""}`} onClick={() => setOpen(r.ref)}>
                    <span className="mail-row__subject truncate">{r.subject || "(no subject)"}</span>
                    <span className="mail-row__meta truncate">{r.direction === "out" ? "You" : r.from.name || r.from.address} · {r.job_title || r.company_name}</span>
                    <span className="mail-row__date">{fmtDateTime(r.date)}{r.attachments ? ` · ${plural(r.attachments, "file")}` : ""}</span>
                  </button>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
          {status.data ? (
            <div className="row">
              <Text role="body-sm" muted>Original emails use {bytes(status.data.raw_bytes)}. The text stays when you remove them.</Text>
              <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" icon={Trash2} loading={busy === "prune"}
                onClick={() => void run("prune", async () => { const r = await bridge.mail.prune(6); status.reload(); return r; }, "Old originals removed")}>Remove originals older than 6 months</Button>
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>
        <div>{open ? <MessageViewer mailRef={open} showMove /> : <Text role="body" muted>Choose an email to read it.</Text>}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function SortItem({ item }: { item: TriageItem }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const targets = useJobTargets();
  const suggested = item.suggestion ? targets.find((t) => t.company_slug === item.suggestion!.company_slug) : undefined;
  const [target, setTarget] = useState(suggested ? `${suggested.company_slug}/${suggested.id}` : "");
  const [always, setAlways] = useState(false);
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const value = target || (suggested ? `${suggested.company_slug}/${suggested.id}` : "");
  const [slug, id] = value.split("/");

  return (
    <Card className="stack" aria-label={item.subject || "(no subject)"}>
      <div className="row row--between">
        <Text role="heading-2">{item.subject || "(no subject)"}</Text>
        <Text role="body-sm" muted>{fmtDateTime(item.date)}</Text>
      </div>
      <div className="mail-addr"><span className="mail-addr__name">{item.from.name}</span><span className="mail-addr__address t-numeric">{item.from.address}</span></div>
      {item.suggestion ? <Text role="body-sm" muted>{item.suggestion.reason}</Text> : <Text role="body-sm" muted>No company matched.</Text>}
      <div className="row">
        <Select label="File under" value={value} onChange={(e) => setTarget(e.target.value)}
          options={[{ value: "", label: "Choose a job" }, ...targets.map((t) => ({ value: `${t.company_slug}/${t.id}`, label: `${t.title} · ${t.company_name}` }))]} />
        <Checkbox label="Always match this sender" checked={always} onChange={(e) => setAlways(e.target.checked)} />
        <Button variant="primary" disabled={!slug || !id} loading={busy === "assign"}
          onClick={() => void run("assign", () => bridge.mail.triage.assign(item.id, { company_slug: slug!, id: id! }, always), "Filed")}>Assign</Button>
      </div>
      <div className="row">
        <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setCreating(true)}>New company and job</Button>
        <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => void run("ignore-sender", () => bridge.mail.triage.ignore(item.id, "sender"), "Sender ignored")}>Ignore sender</Button>
        <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => void run("ignore-domain", () => bridge.mail.triage.ignore(item.id, "domain"), "Domain ignored")}>Ignore domain</Button>
      </div>
      <Dialog open={creating} onClose={() => setCreating(false)} title="New company and job"
        actions={<><Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setCreating(false)}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button variant="primary" disabled={!name.trim() || !title.trim()} loading={busy === "create"}
            onClick={() => void run("create", async () => { await bridge.mail.triage.createCompany(item.id, name.trim(), title.trim()); setCreating(false); }, "Company and job created")}>Create and file</Button></>}>
        <div className="stack">
          <Input label="Company name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
          <Input label="Job title" value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} />
          <Text role="body-sm" muted>CareerOS also matches this sender's address to the new company from now on.</Text>
        </div>
      </Dialog>
    </Card>
  );
}

function ToSort() {
  const items = useLoad((b) => b.mail.triage.list(), [], ["mail"]);
  if (items.error && !items.data) return <ErrorState title="Mail to sort can't load" text={items.error} />;
  if (!items.data) return <Loading />;
  if (!items.data.length) return <Text role="body" muted>Nothing to sort.</Text>;
  return (
    <div className="stack">
      <Text role="body-sm" muted>These look job-related but don't match a company yet. They're deleted after 30 days.</Text>
      {items.data.map((i) => <SortItem key={i.id} item={i} />)}
    </div>
  );
}

function Drafts() {
  const drafts = useLoad((b) => b.mail.drafts.all(), [], ["mail", "approvals"]);
  if (!drafts.data) return <Loading />;
  if (!drafts.data.length) return <Text role="body" muted>No drafts. Write one from a job's Mail tab, or ask Claude.</Text>;
  return (
    <div className="stack">
      {drafts.data.map((d) => {
        const [label, tone] = DRAFT_STATUS[d.status];
        return (
          <Card key={`${d.company_slug}/${d.id}/${d.draft_id}`} className="row row--between" aria-label={d.subject || "(no subject)"}>
            <div className="stack">
              <Text role="heading-2">{d.subject || "(no subject)"}</Text>
              <Text role="body-sm" muted>{d.job_title} · {d.company_name}{d.to ? ` · to ${d.to}` : ""}</Text>
              {d.note ? <Text role="body-sm" className="field__error">{d.note}</Text> : null}
            </div>
            <div className="row">
              <Pill tone={tone}>{label}</Pill>
              <Button onClick={() => navigate({ screen: "job", slug: d.company_slug, id: d.id, tab: "mail" })}>Open</Button>
            </div>
          </Card>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

export function MailScreen() {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("all");
  const status = useLoad((b) => b.mail.status(), [], ["mail"]);
  const s = status.data;
  if (!s) return <Loading />;
  if (!s.available || !s.connected) {
    return (
      <>
        <ScreenHead title="Mail" />
        <Card className="stack">
          <Text role="body">{s.available ? "Connect Gmail to see job emails here, filed under each job." : "Email isn't available in this version of the app."}</Text>
          {s.available ? <div><Button variant="primary" icon={MailIcon} onClick={() => navigate({ screen: "settings" })}>Connect Gmail</Button></div> : null}
        </Card>
      </>
    );
  }
  return (
    <>
      <ScreenHead title="Mail" sub={`Last sync ${formatWhen(s.last_sync)} · next ${formatWhen(s.next_sync)}`}
        right={<Button icon={RefreshCw} loading={busy === "sync" || s.syncing} onClick={() => void run("sync", () => bridge.mail.syncNow(), "Mail synced")}>Sync now</Button>} />
      {s.last_error ? <p role="alert" className="mail-warning">{s.last_error}</p> : null}
      <Tabs label="Mail sections" value={tab} onChange={setTab}
        tabs={[{ id: "all", label: "All job mail" }, { id: "sort", label: "Mail to sort", count: s.triage_count }, { id: "drafts", label: "Drafts" }]}>
        {tab === "all" ? <AllMail /> : null}
        {tab === "sort" ? <ToSort /> : null}
        {tab === "drafts" ? <Drafts /> : null}
      </Tabs>
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
function MailFooter({ status }: { status?: MailStatus }) {
  const ok = !!status?.connected && !status.last_error;
  const text = !status || !status.available ? "Mail · not available" : !status.connected ? "Mail · not connected" : status.last_error ? "Mail · needs attention" : `Mail · synced ${formatWhen(status.last_sync)}`;
  return <div className={ok ? undefined : "t-muted"} title={status?.last_error || undefined}><span className={ok ? "status-dot" : "status-dot status-dot--off"} />{text}</div>;
}
```

```css
.mail-row { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto; grid-template-areas: "subject date" "meta date"; gap: 0 var(--space-3); width: 100%; text-align: left; padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3); border-radius: var(--radius-md); border: var(--size-border) solid transparent; background: none; color: inherit; cursor: pointer; }

.mail-images-bar { padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3); border-radius: var(--radius-md); background: var(--color-bg-raised); border: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border); }
.mail-frame { width: 100%; min-height: calc(var(--space-12) * 8); border: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border); border-radius: var(--radius-md); background: var(--color-surface); }
.mail-chip { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-2); flex-wrap: wrap; padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3); border-radius: var(--radius-md); border: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border); max-width: 100%; }
```

Addresses use the brand's `t-numeric` class for the mono face, because the brand lint forbids `font-family` in renderer CSS. `--color-danger-soft` and `--color-danger` are brand tokens (3a Task 9).

### Task 21: Job > Mail tab (threads, Reply, New email, Move to job) and the draft editor

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-ui/src/components/EmailPreview.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/components/DraftEditor.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/components/JobMail.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/test/job-mail.test.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Job.tsx` (`mail` tab), `packages/app-ui/src/styles/app.css`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `mail.list/drafts.list/drafts.get/drafts.save/drafts.propose/status` (bridge); `MessageViewer` (Task 20); `DRAFT_STATUS` (Task 20, `screens/Mail.tsx`); `JobFile` (3a contract).
- Produces:
  - `EmailPreviewCard({ preview, note? }: { preview: EmailPreview; note?: string })` (reused by Approvals in Task 22)
  - `replyInput(v: MailMessageView): DraftInput`, `EMPTY_DRAFT: DraftInput`
  - `DraftEditor({ slug, id, draftId, initial, files, onClose }: { slug: string; id: string; draftId: string; initial: DraftInput; files: JobFile[]; onClose: () => void })`
  - `JobMail({ slug, id, files }: { slug: string; id: string; files: JobFile[] })`

Rulings: the editor's preview updates as you type (body plus the saved signature); "Save draft" returns the exact preview the services build, and that is what Approvals shows. "Review and send" saves and proposes in one step and never sends: the toast says the email waits in Approvals. A reply goes to `Reply-To` when the message has one, else to the sender, and carries `In-Reply-To` and `References` so Gmail threads it. Attachments can only come from this job's folder.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/app-ui/src/components/EmailPreview.tsx`**

```tsx
import { Lock, Paperclip } from "lucide-react";
import { Card, Text } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { EmailPreview, MailAddressView } from "@careeros/contract";
import { bytes } from "../format";

const people = (list: MailAddressView[]) => list.map((a) => (a.name ? `${a.name} <${a.address}>` : a.address)).join(", ");

/** The exact message: From, To, Cc, Subject, each attachment (name, size, path) and the plain-text body with the signature. */
export function EmailPreviewCard({ preview, note }: { preview: EmailPreview; note?: string }) {
  return (
    <Card className="stack email-preview" aria-label="Email preview">
      {preview.locked ? (
        <p role="alert" className="mail-warning"><Lock aria-hidden="true" /> You are attaching a file from a locked folder.</p>
      ) : null}
      {preview.problem ? <p role="alert" className="mail-warning">{preview.problem}</p> : null}
      <dl className="email-preview__head">
        <dt>From</dt><dd>{people([preview.from])}</dd>
        <dt>To</dt><dd>{people(preview.to) || "(nobody yet)"}</dd>
        {preview.cc.length ? <><dt>Cc</dt><dd>{people(preview.cc)}</dd></> : null}
        <dt>Subject</dt><dd>{preview.subject || "(no subject)"}</dd>
      </dl>
      {preview.attachments.length ? (
        <ul className="list-plain" aria-label="Attachments">
          {preview.attachments.map((a) => (
            <li key={a.rel} className="row">
              <Paperclip aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>{a.name}</span>
              <Text role="body-sm" muted>{a.size ? bytes(a.size) : ""} · {a.rel}</Text>
              {a.locked ? <Text role="body-sm" className="mail-warning">from {a.locked}</Text> : null}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
      <pre className="mail-preview" aria-label="Message as sent">{preview.text}</pre>
      {note ? <Text role="body-sm" muted>{note}</Text> : null}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/app-ui/src/components/DraftEditor.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Save, Send } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Checkbox, Input, Text, Textarea, useToast } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { DraftDetail, DraftInput, JobFile, MailMessageView } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useAction, useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { EmailPreviewCard } from "./EmailPreview";

export const EMPTY_DRAFT: DraftInput = { to: "", cc: "", subject: "", body: "", attach: [], in_reply_to: "", references: [], purpose: "" };

export function replyInput(v: MailMessageView): DraftInput {
  const who = v.reply_to[0] ?? v.from;
  return {
    ...EMPTY_DRAFT,
    to: who.name ? `${who.name} <${who.address}>` : who.address,
    subject: /^re:/i.test(v.subject.trim()) ? v.subject.trim() : `Re: ${v.subject.trim()}`,
    in_reply_to: v.message_id,
    references: [...v.references.filter((r) => r !== v.message_id), v.message_id].slice(-20),
  };
}

export function DraftEditor({ slug, id, draftId, initial, files, onClose }: {
  slug: string; id: string; draftId: string; initial: DraftInput; files: JobFile[]; onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const toast = useToast();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const [input, setInput] = useState<DraftInput>(initial);
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState(draftId);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState<DraftDetail | null>(null);
  const status = useLoad((b) => b.mail.status(), [], ["mail"]);
  const set = (patch: Partial<DraftInput>) => { setInput((d) => ({ ...d, ...patch })); setSaved(null); };
  const sig = status.data?.signature.trim() ?? "";
  const live = `${input.body.replace(/\s+$/, "")}${sig ? `\n\n${sig}` : ""}\n`;
  const attachable = files.filter((f) => f.exists && !f.path.startsWith("emails/drafts/"));

  const save = async (): Promise<DraftDetail> => {
    const d = await bridge.mail.drafts.save(slug, id, current, input);
    setCurrent(d.view.draft_id);
    setSaved(d);
    return d;
  };
  const onSave = () => run("save", save, "Draft saved");
  // run() reports a failure as a toast and returns undefined; close only when the proposal was made.
  const onSend = async () => {
    const ok = await run("send", async () => {
      const d = await save();
      await bridge.mail.drafts.propose(slug, id, d.view.draft_id);
      return true;
    });
    if (!ok) return;
    toast({ text: "The email is waiting in Approvals. Nothing is sent until you approve it.", tone: "success" });
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <form className="stack draft-editor" aria-label="Email draft" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); void onSave(); }}>
      <Input label="To" value={input.to} spellCheck={false} onChange={(e) => set({ to: e.target.value })} help="Separate addresses with commas." />
      <Input label="Cc" value={input.cc} spellCheck={false} onChange={(e) => set({ cc: e.target.value })} />
      <Input label="Subject" value={input.subject} onChange={(e) => set({ subject: e.target.value })} />
      <div className="split">
        <Textarea label="Message" rows={12} value={input.body} onChange={(e) => set({ body: e.target.value })} />
        <div className="field">
          <span className="field__label">Preview</span>
          <pre className="mail-preview" aria-label="Preview">{live}</pre>
        </div>
      </div>
      {attachable.length ? (
        <fieldset>
          <legend>Attach files from this job's folder</legend>
          <div className="stack">
            {attachable.map((f) => (
              <Checkbox key={f.rel} label={`${f.label} (${f.path})`} checked={input.attach.includes(f.rel)}
                onChange={(e) => set({ attach: e.target.checked ? [...input.attach, f.rel] : input.attach.filter((r) => r !== f.rel) })} />
            ))}
          </div>
        </fieldset>
      ) : <Text role="body-sm" muted>Files in this job's folder can be attached here.</Text>}
      {saved?.preview ? <EmailPreviewCard preview={saved.preview} note="This is exactly what will be sent if you approve it." /> : null}
      <div className="row">
        <Button type="submit" icon={Save} loading={busy === "save"}>Save draft</Button>
        <Button variant="primary" icon={Send} loading={busy === "send"} disabled={!input.to.trim() || !input.subject.trim()} onClick={() => void onSend()}>Review and send</Button>
        <Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>Close</Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```


- [ ] **Step 5: Write `packages/app-ui/src/components/JobMail.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { MailPlus } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Pill, Text } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { DraftInput, JobFile, MailRow } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { fmtDateTime } from "../format";
import { DRAFT_STATUS } from "../screens/Mail";
import { DraftEditor, EMPTY_DRAFT, replyInput } from "./DraftEditor";
import { MessageViewer } from "./MessageViewer";
import { Loading } from "./States";

interface Editing { draftId: string; initial: DraftInput }

function threads(rows: MailRow[]): MailRow[][] {
  const map = new Map<string, MailRow[]>();
  for (const r of rows) map.set(r.thread_key, [...(map.get(r.thread_key) ?? []), r]);
  return [...map.values()].map((t) => t.sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date))).sort((a, b) => b[0]!.date.localeCompare(a[0]!.date));
}

export function JobMail({ slug, id, files }: { slug: string; id: string; files: JobFile[] }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState("");
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Editing | null>(null);
  const list = useLoad((b) => b.mail.list({ company_slug: slug, id, includeCompany: true }), [slug, id], ["mail"]);
  const drafts = useLoad((b) => b.mail.drafts.list(slug, id), [slug, id], ["mail", "approvals"]);

  const edit = async (draftId: string) => {
    const d = await bridge.mail.drafts.get(slug, id, draftId);
    setEditing({ draftId, initial: d.draft });
  };

  if (editing) {
    return <DraftEditor key={editing.draftId || "new"} slug={slug} id={id} draftId={editing.draftId} initial={editing.initial} files={files} onClose={() => setEditing(null)} />;
  }
  return (
    <div className="stack">
      <div className="row row--between">
        <Text role="body-sm" muted>Emails for this job and its company. Company emails can be moved onto this job.</Text>
        <Button icon={MailPlus} onClick={() => setEditing({ draftId: "", initial: EMPTY_DRAFT })}>New email</Button>
      </div>
      {drafts.data?.length ? (
        <ul className="list-plain" aria-label="Drafts">
          {drafts.data.map((d) => {
            const [label, tone] = DRAFT_STATUS[d.status];
            return (
              <li key={d.draft_id} className="row">
                <span className="grow"><Text role="body" as="div">{d.subject || "(no subject)"}</Text>{d.note ? <Text role="body-sm" className="field__error" as="div">{d.note}</Text> : null}</span>
                <Pill tone={tone}>{label}</Pill>
                {d.status === "draft" || d.status === "failed" ? <Button size="sm" aria-label={`Edit ${d.subject || "(no subject)"}`} onClick={() => void edit(d.draft_id)}>Edit</Button> : null}
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
      <div className="split">
        <div>
          {!list.data ? <Loading /> : list.data.rows.length === 0 ? <Text role="body" muted>No emails for this job yet.</Text> : (
            <ul className="list-plain" aria-label="Threads">
              {threads(list.data.rows).map((t) => (
                <li key={t[0]!.thread_key} className="stack mail-thread">
                  {t.map((r) => (
                    <button key={r.ref} type="button" className={`mail-row${r.opened || r.direction === "out" ? "" : " is-unread"}${open === r.ref ? " is-current" : ""}`} onClick={() => setOpen(r.ref)}>
                      <span className="mail-row__subject truncate">{r.subject || "(no subject)"}</span>
                      <span className="mail-row__meta truncate">{r.direction === "out" ? "You" : r.from.name || r.from.address}{r.job_id ? "" : " · company mail"}</span>
                      <span className="mail-row__date">{fmtDateTime(r.date)}</span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </div>
        <div>
          {open ? <MessageViewer mailRef={open} showMove onReply={(v) => setEditing({ draftId: "", initial: replyInput(v) })} /> : (
            <Card><Text role="body" muted>Choose an email to read it.</Text></Card>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 22: Approvals show the exact email; Apply offers "Email application"; Home counts replies

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-ui/test/approvals-mail.test.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/test/apply-email.test.tsx`, `packages/services/test/apply-email.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/services/src/apply.ts` (`applyState`), `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Approvals.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Apply.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Home.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/mock/MockBridge.ts` (`applyState`), `packages/app-ui/test/approvals.test.tsx` (the 3a email test)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `withEmail` (Task 15), `isEmailAddress` (core, Task 3), `EmailPreviewCard`, `DraftEditor` (Task 21), `ApplyState.apply_email`, `ApprovalView.email`, `HomeSummary.unreadMail` (Task 13).
- Produces: `applyEmailOf(applyVia: string): string` (services `apply.ts`); `ApplyState.approval` is now the latest `apply_handoff` or `email_send` approval with `purpose: "apply"` for the job, with its `email` preview while pending; `emailApplicationDraft(s: ApplyState): DraftInput` (exported from `Apply.tsx`).

Rulings: an email application is the same `email_send` approval as any other email; the Apply flow only pre-fills the draft (to `apply_via`, subject, a short body, the ticked `application/` files, `purpose: "apply"`). After the send, Apply moves to Confirm and the person marks it applied; the app never marks it for them. On the Approvals card, Approve is disabled while the preview has a problem (the draft changed, an attachment is missing or locked), and the card says why.

`packages/services/test/apply-email.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { layout, readRecord, updateRecord } from "@careeros/core";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { applyHandlers as A } from "../src/apply.js";
import { approvalHandlers as AP } from "../src/approvals.js";
import { fakeMailHost } from "../src/fakes.js";
import { mailReadHandlers as R } from "../src/mail-read.js";
import { openSample } from "./helpers.js";
import { connectMail, GOOD_PASSWORD, LABS } from "./mail-helpers.js";

const JOBS = "jobs" + "@" + "example.org";

describe("email applications", () => {
  it("offers the apply_via address and moves to confirm after the approved send, without marking applied", async () => {
    const mail = fakeMailHost({ goodPassword: GOOD_PASSWORD });
    const { ctx, root } = openSample({ mail });
    connectMail(ctx, root);
    updateRecord("job", layout(root).jobFile(...LABS), (j) => { j.apply_via = `mailto:${JOBS}`; });
    const resume = path.join(layout(root).jobDir(...LABS), "application", "resume.md");
    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(resume), { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(resume, "# Alex Example\n");
    const s0 = A["apply.state"](ctx, ...LABS);
    expect(s0).toMatchObject({ apply_email: JOBS, step: "review" });
    const rel = `companies/${LABS[0]}/jobs/${LABS[1]}/application/resume.md`;
    const d = R["mail.drafts.save"](ctx, ...LABS, "", { to: JOBS, cc: "", subject: "Application for Backend Engineer (LLM)", body: "Hi,\n\nMy application is attached.", attach: [rel], in_reply_to: "", references: [], purpose: "apply" });
    const a = R["mail.drafts.propose"](ctx, ...LABS, d.view.draft_id);
    const s1 = A["apply.state"](ctx, ...LABS);
    expect(s1.step).toBe("handoff");
    expect(s1.approval).toMatchObject({ id: a.id, action: "email_send", email: { to: [{ name: "", address: JOBS }] } });
    await AP["approvals.approve"](ctx, a.id);
    expect(A["apply.state"](ctx, ...LABS).step).toBe("confirm");
    expect(readRecord("job", layout(root).jobFile(...LABS)).status).not.toBe("Applied");
    expect(mail.fake.sent).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it("ignores apply_via values that aren't plain addresses", () => {
    const { ctx, root } = openSample();
    for (const v of ["https://jobs.example.com/apply", "mailto:", "not an address"]) {
      updateRecord("job", layout(root).jobFile(...LABS), (j) => { j.apply_via = v; });
      expect(A["apply.state"](ctx, ...LABS).apply_email, v).toBe("");
    }
  });
});
```

```tsx
describe("Approvals: email sends", () => {
  it("shows the exact message and sends it once on Approve", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const bridge = new MockBridge();
    const d = await bridge.mail.drafts.save("acme-corp", "2026-01-12-ai-engineer", "", draft);
```

## Group E: Electron shell (`@careeros/app-electron`, spec §3.4, §3.5, §5)

### Task 23: Electron: `careeros-mail:` protocol, frame rules, mail host, background entry point, wake sync, e2e hooks

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-electron/src/background.ts`, `packages/app-electron/src/mail-host.ts`, `packages/app-electron/test/mail-shell.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/app-electron/package.json`, `packages/app-electron/tsconfig.json`, `packages/app-electron/scripts/build.mjs`, `packages/app-electron/src/security.ts` (`CSP`, `frameNavigation`), `packages/app-electron/src/e2e.ts`, `packages/app-electron/src/shell.ts` (`openExternal` note), `packages/app-electron/src/main.ts` (full replacement below), `packages/app-electron/test/security.test.ts`, `packages/app-electron/test/helpers.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createRuntime` (now with `syncMailSoon` and `resolveMailAsset`), `runBackgroundSync`, `memorySecrets`, `MailHost`, `Host` (services); `GmailProvider`, `GMAIL_HOSTS`, `fetchRemoteImage`, `fakeProviderFromFile`, `createScheduler`, `nodeOsHost`, `redact`, `type Fetcher`, `type MailProvider` (mail).
- Produces:
  - `parseBackgroundArgs(argv): { workspace: string } | null`, `backgroundCommandFor(o: { packaged; execPath; appPath; workspace }): { program: string; args: string[] }` (`background.ts`)
  - `fileLog(file): { log(line: string): void; read(): string[] }`, `electronMailHost(o: { userData: string; fetcher: Fetcher; allowedHosts: string[]; background: (workspace: string) => { program: string; args: string[] }; provider?: (a: MailAccount) => MailProvider }): MailHost` (`mail-host.ts`)
  - `frameNavigation(url: string, isMainFrame: boolean): "allow" | "block" | "link"` and `CSP` with `frame-src careeros-mail:` (`security.ts`)
  - `e2eMail(env, packaged): { fixture: string; sentDir: string } | null`, `e2eMemorySecrets(env, packaged): boolean`, `e2eIpcLog(env, packaged): string | null` (`e2e.ts`)
  - `APP_ID = "app.careeros"` (the launchd label is `app.careeros.mailsync`)

Rulings:
- `careeros-mail:` is registered `standard` and `secure` and nothing else (no `supportFetchAPI`, no `corsEnabled`, no `stream`), so the renderer can't `fetch` it; only the sandboxed frame loads it. Every response comes from `runtime.resolveMailAsset`, which sets the frame CSP, `nosniff` and `no-store`.
- Subframes may load only `careeros-mail://msg/...`. A click on a rewritten link (`careeros-mail://link/...`) is stopped before it navigates and handed to `resolveMailAsset`, which shows the 3a link dialog with the full address and any mismatch note. Everything else a frame tries is blocked.
- The renderer CSP gains `frame-src careeros-mail:` and nothing else.
- Remote images, after "Load images", are fetched by the main process in a separate in-memory session that never sends cookies, `Referer` or `Origin`, through `fetchRemoteImage` (https only, 5 MB, no SVG).
- `--background-sync --workspace <path>` opens no window. It takes the single-instance lock; if the app is already running, it passes "sync mail" to that instance and exits. The OS scheduler runs `process.execPath` with these arguments (plus the app path when running from the repo).
- When the computer wakes (`powerMonitor` resume), the app syncs soon; missed intervals are not replayed.
- E2E hooks, honoured only when not packaged: `CAREEROS_E2E_MAIL` (JSON `{ "fixture": <abs path>, "sentDir": <abs path> }`: a fake Gmail read from the fixture; sent messages are written to `sentDir`; remote images return a 1x1 PNG without touching the network), `CAREEROS_E2E_SECRETS=memory` (CI Linux has no keyring), `CAREEROS_E2E_IPC_LOG` (absolute file: one JSON line per IPC reply, so the test can prove the password never comes back).

- [ ] **Step 1: Dependencies and build**

In `packages/app-electron/package.json`, add `"@careeros/mail": "0.1.0"` to `dependencies`. In `packages/app-electron/tsconfig.json`, add `"resolveJsonModule": true` to `compilerOptions` and `"@careeros/mail": ["../mail/src/index.ts"]` to `paths`. Run `npm install`.

In `packages/app-electron/scripts/build.mjs`, change the main-process build to keep the four mail libraries as runtime `require`s from `node_modules` (they load optional encodings and parsers dynamically, which bundling breaks):

```js
await build({
  ...common, entryPoints: [path.join(pkg, "src/main.ts")], outfile: path.join(dist, "main.js"), format: "esm",
  external: ["electron", "imapflow", "nodemailer", "mailparser", "sanitize-html"],
  // Bundled CommonJS dependencies may call require() for Node built-ins; give the ESM bundle a real require.
  banner: { js: "import { createRequire as __careerosRequire } from 'node:module'; const require = __careerosRequire(import.meta.url);" },
});
```

The matcher's `data/job-senders.json` is imported as JSON by `match.ts`, so esbuild inlines it; no file copy is needed.

`packages/app-electron/test/mail-shell.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { GmailProvider } from "@careeros/mail";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { backgroundCommandFor, parseBackgroundArgs } from "../src/background.js";
import { electronMailHost, fileLog } from "../src/mail-host.js";

const tmp = () => fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "careeros-mailshell-"));
const WS = path.resolve("/fictional/alex-example/CareerOS");

describe("background arguments", () => {
  it("finds the background flag and an absolute workspace", () => {
    expect(parseBackgroundArgs(["/x/CareerOS", "--background-sync", "--workspace", WS])).toEqual({ workspace: WS });
    expect(parseBackgroundArgs(["/x/CareerOS", "--background-sync", "--workspace", "relative/ws"])).toEqual({ workspace: "" });
    expect(parseBackgroundArgs(["/x/CareerOS", "--background-sync"])).toEqual({ workspace: "" });
    expect(parseBackgroundArgs(["/x/CareerOS", "--workspace", WS])).toBeNull();
  });
  it("runs the app itself, adding the app path only when running from the repo", () => {
    expect(backgroundCommandFor({ packaged: true, execPath: "/Applications/CareerOS.app/Contents/MacOS/CareerOS", appPath: "/ignored", workspace: WS }))
      .toEqual({ program: "/Applications/CareerOS.app/Contents/MacOS/CareerOS", args: ["--background-sync", "--workspace", WS] });
    expect(backgroundCommandFor({ packaged: false, execPath: "/repo/node_modules/electron/dist/electron", appPath: "/repo/packages/app-electron", workspace: WS }))
      .toEqual({ program: "/repo/node_modules/electron/dist/electron", args: ["/repo/packages/app-electron", "--background-sync", "--workspace", WS] });
  });
});

describe("mail log", () => {
  it("redacts addresses, stays one line per entry, is private, and keeps its size bounded", () => {
    const file = path.join(tmp(), "logs", "mail-sync.log");
    const l = fileLog(file);
    l.log(`sync failed for ${"alex" + "@" + "example.com"}\nsecond line`);
    expect(l.read()).toEqual(["sync failed for [address] second line"]);
    if (process.platform !== "win32") expect(fs.statSync(file).mode & 0o777).toBe(0o600);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3000; i++) l.log(`2026-01-15T09:00:00.000Z sync stored=${i} sorted=0 skipped=0 duplicates=0 too_large=0 capped=no`);
    expect(fs.statSync(file).size).toBeLessThan(300 * 1024);
    expect(l.read()).toHaveLength(50);
    expect(l.read().at(-1)).toContain("stored=2999");
  });
});

describe("electron mail host", () => {
  it("uses Gmail with the allowed hosts, a lock file in user data, and the image fetcher", async () => {
    const userData = tmp();
    const fetcher = vi.fn(async (_url: string, _init: unknown) => new Response(Buffer.from("GIF89a"), { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "image/gif" } }));
    const h = electronMailHost({ userData, fetcher, allowedHosts: ["imap.gmail.com", "smtp.gmail.com"], background: (ws) => ({ program: "/x", args: [ws] }) });
    expect(h.provider({ address: "alex" + "@" + "example.com", password: "abcdefghijklmnop", displayName: "Alex Example" })).toBeInstanceOf(GmailProvider);
    expect(h.lockFile).toBe(path.join(userData, "mail-sync.lock"));
    expect(h.backgroundCommand(WS)).toEqual({ program: "/x", args: [WS] });
    expect(await h.fetchImage("https://images.example.com/p.gif")).toMatchObject({ type: "image/gif" });
    const init = fetcher.mock.calls[0]![1] as unknown as { credentials: string; referrerPolicy: string; redirect: string };
    expect(init).toMatchObject({ credentials: "omit", referrerPolicy: "no-referrer", redirect: "manual" });
    await expect(h.fetchImage("http://images.example.com/p.gif")).rejects.toThrow("Images load over https only.");
  });
});
```

```ts
      expect(frameNavigation(u, false), u).toBe("block");
    }
    expect(frameNavigation("careeros-mail://msg/abc", true)).toBe("block");
  });
});
```

- in `"keeps Electron out of the pure modules"`, add `"background.ts"` and `"mail-host.ts"` to the list.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the pure modules**

`packages/app-electron/src/background.ts`:

```ts
import path from "node:path";

/** `--background-sync --workspace <abs path>`; a missing or relative workspace gives "" (the run then reports it). */
export function parseBackgroundArgs(argv: readonly string[]): { workspace: string } | null {
  if (!argv.includes("--background-sync")) return null;
  const i = argv.indexOf("--workspace");
  const ws = i >= 0 ? argv[i + 1] : undefined;
  return { workspace: ws && path.isAbsolute(ws) && !ws.includes("\u0000") ? path.normalize(ws) : "" };
}

/** What the OS scheduler runs: the app binary itself, so safeStorage can decrypt the password. */
export function backgroundCommandFor(o: { packaged: boolean; execPath: string; appPath: string; workspace: string }): { program: string; args: string[] } {
  const args = ["--background-sync", "--workspace", o.workspace];
  return o.packaged ? { program: o.execPath, args } : { program: o.execPath, args: [o.appPath, ...args] };
}
```

`packages/app-electron/src/mail-host.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fetchRemoteImage, GmailProvider, redact, type Fetcher, type MailAccount, type MailProvider } from "@careeros/mail";
import type { MailHost } from "@careeros/services";

const MAX_LOG_BYTES = 256 * 1024;

/** The mail log in user data: redacted, one line per entry, owner-only, trimmed to its newest half past 256 KB. */
export function fileLog(file: string): { log(line: string): void; read(): string[] } {
  const lines = () => { try { return fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8").split("\n").filter(Boolean); } catch { return []; } };
  return {
    log(line) {
      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
      fs.appendFileSync(file, `${redact(line)}\n`, { mode: 0o600 });
      if (fs.statSync(file).size > MAX_LOG_BYTES) {
        const all = lines();
        const tmp = `${file}.${process.pid}.tmp`;
        fs.writeFileSync(tmp, `${all.slice(Math.floor(all.length / 2)).join("\n")}\n`, { mode: 0o600 });
        fs.renameSync(tmp, file);
      }
    },
    read: () => lines().slice(-50),
  };
}

export function electronMailHost(o: {
  userData: string; fetcher: Fetcher; allowedHosts: string[];
  background: (workspace: string) => { program: string; args: string[] };
  provider?: (a: MailAccount) => MailProvider;
}): MailHost {
  const logs = fileLog(path.join(o.userData, "logs", "mail-sync.log"));
  return {
    provider: o.provider ?? ((account) => new GmailProvider(account, { allowedHosts: o.allowedHosts })),
    fetchImage: (url) => fetchRemoteImage(url, o.fetcher),
    lockFile: path.join(o.userData, "mail-sync.lock"),
    log: (line) => logs.log(line),
    readLog: () => logs.read(),
    backgroundCommand: o.background,
  };
}
```

In `packages/app-electron/src/security.ts`, replace `CSP` and add `frameNavigation`:

```ts
export const CSP = [
  "default-src 'none'", "script-src 'self'", "style-src 'self'", "font-src 'self'", "img-src 'self' careeros-file:", "media-src careeros-file:",
  "frame-src careeros-mail:", "connect-src 'self'", "base-uri 'none'", "form-action 'none'", "frame-ancestors 'none'", "object-src 'none'",
].join("; ");

/** Subframes (the email viewer) may load stored-message documents only; link clicks become the link dialog. */
export function frameNavigation(url: string, isMainFrame: boolean): "allow" | "block" | "link" {
  if (isMainFrame) return "block";
  if (url.startsWith("careeros-mail://msg/")) return "allow";
  if (url.startsWith("careeros-mail://link/")) return "link";
  return "block";
}
```

(`will-navigate` already blocks every main-frame navigation; `frameNavigation` is only consulted for subframes.)

Append to `packages/app-electron/src/e2e.ts`:

```ts
/** A fake Gmail for end-to-end runs: messages from `fixture`, sent mail written to `sentDir`. */
export function e2eMail(env: Env, packaged: boolean): { fixture: string; sentDir: string } | null {
  if (packaged || !env.CAREEROS_E2E_MAIL) return null;
  try {
    const v = JSON.parse(env.CAREEROS_E2E_MAIL) as { fixture?: unknown; sentDir?: unknown };
    return typeof v.fixture === "string" && path.isAbsolute(v.fixture) && typeof v.sentDir === "string" && path.isAbsolute(v.sentDir)
      ? { fixture: v.fixture, sentDir: v.sentDir } : null;
  } catch { return null; }
}

export const e2eMemorySecrets = (env: Env, packaged: boolean): boolean => !packaged && env.CAREEROS_E2E_SECRETS === "memory";

export function e2eIpcLog(env: Env, packaged: boolean): string | null {
  const f = env.CAREEROS_E2E_IPC_LOG;
  return !packaged && f && path.isAbsolute(f) ? f : null;
}
```

```ts
      const { response } = win ? await dialog.showMessageBox(win, opts) : await dialog.showMessageBox(opts);
      if (response !== 0) return false;
      await shell.openExternal(url);
      return true;
    },
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Replace `packages/app-electron/src/main.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";
import { app, BrowserWindow, ipcMain, net, powerMonitor, protocol, safeStorage, session } from "electron";
import { createScheduler, fakeProviderFromFile, GMAIL_HOSTS, nodeOsHost, type Fetcher, type MailProvider } from "@careeros/mail";
import { createRuntime, isSafeLink, memorySecrets, runBackgroundSync, type Host, type Runtime } from "@careeros/services";
import { backgroundCommandFor, parseBackgroundArgs } from "./background.js";
import { CALL, CHANGE } from "./channels.js";
import { e2eIpcLog, e2eMail, e2eMemorySecrets, e2ePicks, e2eUserData } from "./e2e.js";
import { electronMailHost } from "./mail-host.js";
import { electronPrinter } from "./printer.js";
import { safeStorageSecrets } from "./secret-store.js";
import { APP_ORIGIN, appFileFor, contentType, CSP, devUrlFrom, fileUrlToRel, frameNavigation, isTrustedSender } from "./security.js";
import { electronShell } from "./shell.js";
import { fileStateStore } from "./state-store.js";

export const APP_ID = "app.careeros";
const HERE = import.meta.dirname;
const RENDERER = path.join(HERE, "renderer");
const PRELOAD = path.join(HERE, "preload.cjs");
const PNG_1X1 = Buffer.from("iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAQAAAC1HAwCAAAAC0lEQVR42mNkYAAAAAYAAjCB0C8AAAAASUVORK5CYII=", "base64");
const devUrl = devUrlFrom(process.env, app.isPackaged);
const userData = e2eUserData(process.env, app.isPackaged);
if (userData) app.setPath("userData", userData);
const background = parseBackgroundArgs(process.argv);
const e2eMailHook = e2eMail(process.env, app.isPackaged);
const ipcLog = e2eIpcLog(process.env, app.isPackaged);

protocol.registerSchemesAsPrivileged([
  { scheme: "app", privileges: { standard: true, secure: true, supportFetchAPI: true } },
  { scheme: "careeros-file", privileges: { standard: true, secure: true, stream: true } },
  // Only the sandboxed email frame loads this scheme; no fetch, no CORS, no streaming.
  { scheme: "careeros-mail", privileges: { standard: true, secure: true } },
]);
app.enableSandbox();

let win: BrowserWindow | null = null;
let runtime: Runtime | null = null;

/** Remote images for "Load images": a separate in-memory session with no cookies, Referer or Origin. */
function imageFetcher(): Fetcher {
  if (e2eMailHook) return async () => new Response(PNG_1X1, { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "image/png" } });
  const ses = session.fromPartition("careeros-mail-images", { cache: false });
  ses.setPermissionRequestHandler((_wc, _permission, cb) => cb(false));
  ses.setPermissionCheckHandler(() => false);
  ses.webRequest.onBeforeSendHeaders((details, cb) => {
    const headers = { ...details.requestHeaders };
    for (const k of Object.keys(headers)) if (/^(cookie|referer|origin)$/i.test(k)) delete headers[k];
    cb({ requestHeaders: headers });
  });
  return (url, init) => ses.fetch(url, init as unknown as RequestInit) as unknown as Promise<Response>;
}

function makeHost(): Host {
  const data = app.getPath("userData");
  let sentCount = 0;
  const fake: MailProvider | null = e2eMailHook
    ? fakeProviderFromFile(e2eMailHook.fixture, (raw) => {
      fs.mkdirSync(e2eMailHook.sentDir, { recursive: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(e2eMailHook.sentDir, `${++sentCount}.eml`), raw);
    })
    : null;
  const allowedHosts = [...GMAIL_HOSTS];
  return {
    secrets: e2eMemorySecrets(process.env, app.isPackaged) ? memorySecrets(true) : safeStorageSecrets(path.join(data, "secrets.json"), safeStorage, process.platform),
    state: fileStateStore(path.join(data, "state.json")),
    shell: electronShell(() => win, e2ePicks(process.env, app.isPackaged)),
    info: { version: app.getVersion(), shell: "electron", platform: process.platform },
    printer: electronPrinter(), // addendum Task 37, kept
    mail: electronMailHost({
      userData: data, fetcher: imageFetcher(), allowedHosts, provider: fake ? () => fake : undefined,
      background: (workspace) => backgroundCommandFor({ packaged: app.isPackaged, execPath: process.execPath, appPath: app.getAppPath(), workspace }),
    }),
    scheduler: createScheduler(nodeOsHost(), APP_ID),
    net: { allowedHosts },
  };
}

async function serveApp(req: Request): Promise<Response> {
  const file = appFileFor(RENDERER, req.url);
  if (!file) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  const res = await net.fetch(pathToFileURL(file).toString());
  if (!res.ok) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  return new Response(res.body, { status: 200, headers: { "Content-Type": contentType(file), "Content-Security-Policy": CSP, "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff" } });
}

async function serveFile(req: Request): Promise<Response> {
  const rel = fileUrlToRel(req.url);
  if (!rel || !runtime) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  let abs: string;
  try { abs = runtime.resolveMedia(rel); } catch { return new Response("Not available", { status: 403 }); }
  const range = req.headers.get("Range");
  return net.fetch(pathToFileURL(abs).toString(), range ? { headers: { Range: range } } : undefined);
}

async function serveMail(req: Request): Promise<Response> {
  if (!runtime) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  const r = await runtime.resolveMailAsset(req.url);
  return new Response(r.status === 204 ? null : r.body, { status: r.status, headers: r.headers });
}

function createWindow(): void {
  win = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 1280, height: 820, minWidth: 960, minHeight: 640, show: false, title: "CareerOS",
    backgroundColor: "#0d0e11", // Graphite background (color.ink.900); avoids a white flash before the CSS loads.
    webPreferences: { preload: PRELOAD, contextIsolation: true, sandbox: true, nodeIntegration: false, webSecurity: true, spellcheck: false, devTools: !app.isPackaged },
  });
  win.once("ready-to-show", () => win?.show());
  win.on("closed", () => { win = null; });
  void win.loadURL(devUrl ?? `${APP_ORIGIN}/index.html`);
}

// Every web contents, now or later: no navigation, no new windows (https links go to the browser after a prompt), no webviews.
app.on("web-contents-created", (_e, contents) => {
  contents.on("will-navigate", (e) => e.preventDefault());
  contents.on("will-redirect", (e) => e.preventDefault());
  contents.on("will-attach-webview", (e) => e.preventDefault());
  // The email frame: stored-message documents only; a link click opens the link dialog instead of navigating.
  contents.on("will-frame-navigate", (e) => {
    if (e.isMainFrame) return;
    const decision = frameNavigation(e.url, false);
    if (decision === "allow") return;
    e.preventDefault();
    if (decision === "link") void runtime?.resolveMailAsset(e.url);
  });
  contents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {
    if (isSafeLink(url)) void runtime?.ctx.host.shell.openExternal(url);
    return { action: "deny" };
  });
});

if (!app.requestSingleInstanceLock(background ? { syncMail: true } : {})) {
  // Another CareerOS is running. A background run has handed it "sync mail" through the lock's data.
  app.quit();
} else if (background) {
  app.dock?.hide();
  void app.whenReady().then(async () => {
    let code = 1;
    try { code = (await runBackgroundSync(makeHost(), background.workspace)).code; } catch { code = 1; }
    app.exit(code);
  });
} else {
  app.on("second-instance", (_e, _argv, _cwd, data) => {
    if ((data as { syncMail?: boolean } | undefined)?.syncMail) { runtime?.syncMailSoon(); return; }
    if (!win) return;
    if (win.isMinimized()) win.restore();
    win.focus();
  });

  void app.whenReady().then(() => {
    session.defaultSession.setPermissionRequestHandler((_wc, _permission, cb) => cb(false));
    session.defaultSession.setPermissionCheckHandler(() => false);
    protocol.handle("app", serveApp);
    protocol.handle("careeros-file", serveFile);
    protocol.handle("careeros-mail", serveMail);

    runtime = createRuntime(makeHost());
    runtime.onChange((e) => win?.webContents.send(CHANGE, e));

    ipcMain.handle(CALL, async (event, method: unknown, args: unknown) => {
      if (!win || event.sender !== win.webContents || !isTrustedSender(event.senderFrame?.url, devUrl)) throw new Error("This request was blocked.");
      const reply = await runtime!.dispatch(String(method), args);
      if (ipcLog) fs.appendFileSync(ipcLog, `${JSON.stringify({ method: String(method), reply: reply ?? null })}\n`);
      return reply;
    });

    // A laptop that slept past a grant's end re-locks as soon as it wakes, and mail catches up.
    powerMonitor.on("resume", () => { runtime?.checkGrants(); runtime?.syncMailSoon(); });
    powerMonitor.on("unlock-screen", () => { runtime?.checkGrants(); });

    createWindow();
  });

  app.on("before-quit", () => { runtime?.shutdown(); });
  app.on("window-all-closed", () => { app.quit(); });
}
```
